========================================
DOSYA: PROJE_ARCHITECTURE.md

# fishLab — Mimari Anayasa, Kurallar ve Yol Haritası

> **ÖNEMLİ UYARI (AI ASİSTAN İÇİN BAĞLAYICI KURALLAR):**
> Bu dosya projenin kesinleşmiş teknik kararlarını, 3D modelleme standartlarını, fiziksel yerleşim kurallarını ve ilerleme planını içerir. Herhangi bir kod üretmeden önce bu kurallar kesin olarak uygulanmalıdır.

---

## 1. Proje Amacı ve Temel Vizyon
**fishLab**, kullanıcıların akvaryum hacmini belirleyip; kum, bitki, balık, omurgasız (karides/salyangoz), aydınlatma ve filtrasyon/havalandırma ekipmanlarını gerçekçi biyolojik ve fiziksel kurallara göre kurgulayabildiği **etkileşimli 3D akvaryum ekosistem simülasyonu ve e-ticaret** web platformudur.

---

## 2. Kesin Teknoloji ve Mimari Kurallar (YASAKLAR & ZORUNLULUKLAR)
1. **%100 Prosedürel Three.js Mimarisi (GLB/GLTF KESİNLİKLE YASAK):**
   - Projede **hiçbir zaman** dışarıdan `.glb`, `.gltf`, `.obj` veya `.fbx` dosyası yüklenmeyecektir (`useGLTF`, `GLTFLoader` yasaktır).
   - Tüm canlılar, bitkiler, zeminler, aydınlatmalar ve ekipmanlar **saf Three.js geometrileri (`BufferGeometry`, `LatheGeometry`, `ExtrudeGeometry`, `TubeGeometry`, `CylinderGeometry` vb.), prosedürel `CanvasTexture` dokuları ve özel GLSL shader'lar** ile yüksek detaylı ve anatomik/endüstriyel gerçekçilikte kodlanacaktır.
2. **Katmanlı Ayrım (Decoupled Architecture):**
   - **3D Görselleştirme (`models/` ve `components/scene/`):** Sadece sahne render, shader animasyonları, fiziksel konumlandırma ve sürükle-bırak etkileşiminden sorumludur.
   - **Ekosistem Kuralları (`lib/simulation/ecosystemEngine.ts`):** Su kimyası (amonyak, nitrat, pH, sıcaklık, oksijen doygunluğu), canlı uyumluluğu, hacim yeterliliği ve biyolojik yük hesaplamaları 3D döngüsünden tamamen bağımsızdır.
   - **Durum Yönetimi (`store/useAquariumStore.ts`):** Tüm sepet, tank içi ürünler, yerleşimli ekipman koordinatları ve simülasyon parametreleri Zustand üzerinden yönetilir.
   - **Veri Katmanı (`services/catalogService.ts`):** Katalog verileri Supabase'den normalize edilerek çekilir; katalog ikonları ve alt kategori eşleşmeleri eksiksiz olmalıdır.

---

## 3. Klasör Yapısı ve Modülerlik Standartları
```text
├── PROJECT_ARCHITECTURE.md          # Bu anayasa dosyası
├── app/                             # Next.js App Router sayfaları
├── components/
│   ├── scene/                       # R3F Canvas, Tank, Sahne Kayıtçısı, Sürükleme & Bağlantılar
│   └── ui/                          # Katalog, Sepet, Ekosistem Analiz Paneli, Ekipman Kontrol HUD
├── lib/
│   └── simulation/                  # ecosystemEngine.ts (Biyolojik & fiziksel hesaplamalar)
├── models/                          # Yüksek detaylı prosedürel 3D varlık fabrikaları
│   ├── baliklar/                    # lepistes/, beta/, vatoz/
│   ├── canlilar/                    # karides/, salyangoz/
│   ├── kumlar/                      # silis-kum/, hagen/, aktif-toprak/
│   └── malzemeler/
│       ├── aydinlatma/              # BeyazIsikMesh, BeyazMaviIsikMesh, UcRenkIsikMesh
│       └── ekipman/                 # HavaMotoruMesh, PipoFiltreMesh, IcFiltreMesh, HavaTasiMesh
├── services/                        # catalogService.ts (Supabase entegrasyonu)
└── store/                           # useAquariumStore.ts (Zustand state)

-- =========================================================
-- AkvaryumLab (fishLab) Supabase 'catalog' Tablosu Kurulumu
-- Bu SQL kodunu Supabase Dashboard -> SQL Editor kısmına yapıştırıp RUN butonuna basın.
-- =========================================================

-- 1. 'catalog' tablosunu oluştur
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.catalog (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    category TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (category IN ('baliklar', 'bitkiler', 'kumlar', 'malzemeler')),
    price NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    min_liters INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    ideal_temp_min NUMERIC(4, 1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
    ideal_temp_max NUMERIC(4, 1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 28,
    ideal_ph_min NUMERIC(3, 1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 6.5,
    ideal_ph_max NUMERIC(3, 1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 7.5,
    bio_load INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    description TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    color TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '#38bdf8',
    swim_zone TEXT DEFAULT 'orta' CHECK (swim_zone IN ('dip', 'orta', 'ust')),
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()) NOT NULL
);

-- 2. Herkesin okuyabilmesi için RLS (Row Level Security) politikasını etkinleştir
ALTER TABLE public.catalog ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Herkes katalog ürünlerini görebilir" 
ON public.catalog 
FOR SELECT 
TO anon, authenticated 
USING (true);

-- 3. Başlangıç Ürün Verilerini Ekle (Mevcutsa güncelle)

========================================
DOSYA: components\canvas\AquariumScene.tsx

'use client';

import React, { useRef, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { OrbitControls, PerspectiveCamera, Environment } from '@react-three/drei';
import * as THREE from 'three';

import { useAquariumStore } from '@/store/useAquariumStore';
import { SceneObjectRenderer } from './SceneObjectRegistry';
import { StudioEnvironment } from './StudioEnvironment';
import { DraggableEquipment } from './DraggableEquipment';
import { SilisKumMesh } from './models/kumlar/silis_kum/SilisKumMesh';

interface AquariumSceneProps {
  onFpsUpdate?: (fps: number) => void;
}

/**
 * FPS Ölçer & Canlıların Tek Döngüde Yüzme / Gezinme Animasyonu
 */
function AnimatedTankContents() {
  const tankConfig = useAquariumStore((s) => s.tankConfig);
  const tankItems = useAquariumStore((s) => s.tankItems);
  const catalog = useAquariumStore((s) => s.catalog);
  const placedEquipments = useAquariumStore((s) => s.placedEquipments);
  const isDraggingEquipment = useAquariumStore((s) => s.isDraggingEquipment);

  const { width, height, depth } = tankConfig.dimensions;
  const itemRefs = useRef>(new Map());

  // Katalog detaylarını hızlı erişim için haritala
  const catalogMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, (typeof catalog)[number]>();
    catalog.forEach((c) => map.set(c.id, c));
    return map;
  }, [catalog]);

  // Her öğe için deterministik başlangıç konumu ve hareket parametresi
  const itemSeeds = useMemo(() => {
    const seeds = new Map<string, { phase: number; speed: number; radiusX: number; radiusZ: number; baseY: number }>();
    tankItems.forEach((item, idx) => {
      const hash = item.instanceId.split('').reduce((acc, ch) => acc + ch.charCodeAt(0), idx * 31);
      seeds.set(item.instanceId, {
        phase: (hash % 360) * (Math.PI / 180),
        speed: 0.35 + (hash % 10) * 0.04,
        radiusX: (width * 0.32) * (0.4 + ((hash % 7) / 10)),
        radiusZ: (depth * 0.32) * (0.4 + ((hash % 5) / 10)),
        baseY: -height * 0.15 + ((hash % 10) / 10) * (height * 0.45),
      });
    });
    return seeds;
  }, [tankItems, width, depth, height]);

  // Tek merkezi useFrame döngüsü (Yüksek FPS koruması)
  useFrame(({ clock }) => {
    const t = clock.getElapsedTime();

    tankItems.forEach((item) => {
      const group = itemRefs.current.get(item.instanceId);
      if (!group) return;

      const meta = catalogMap.get(item.itemId);
      const category = meta?.category || 'fish';
      const nameLower = (meta?.name || item.itemId).toLowerCase();
      const seed = itemSeeds.get(item.instanceId);
      if (!seed) return;

      // Aydınlatma sabit üstte durur
      if (category === 'lighting' || nameLower.includes('ışık')) {
        group.position.set(0, 0, 0);
        return;
      }

      // Bitki ve dekorlar tabanda sabit durur
      if (category === 'plant' || category === 'decor' || category === 'hardscape') {
        const px = Math.cos(seed.phase) * (width * 0.34);
        const pz = Math.sin(seed.phase) * (depth * 0.32);
        group.position.set(px, -height / 2 + 0.25, pz);
        return;
      }

      // Vatoz, Karides ve Salyangoz tabanda ağır gezinir
      const isBottomDweller =
        nameLower.includes('vatoz') ||
        nameLower.includes('karides') ||
        nameLower.includes('salyangoz');

      if (isBottomDweller) {
        const slowT = t * (seed.speed * 0.25) + seed.phase;
        const nx = Math.cos(slowT) * seed.radiusX;
        const nz = Math.sin(slowT * 0.8) * seed.radiusZ;
        const dx = -Math.sin(slowT) * seed.radiusX;
        const dz = Math.cos(slowT * 0.8) * seed.radiusZ * 0.8;

        group.position.set(nx, -height / 2 + 0.32, nz);
        group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz) - Math.PI / 2;
        return;
      }

      // Yüzen balıklar (Beta, Lepistes vb.)
      const swimT = t * seed.speed + seed.phase;
      const nx = Math.cos(swimT) * seed.radiusX;
      const nz = Math.sin(swimT * 1.3) * seed.radiusZ;
      const ny = seed.baseY + Math.sin(swimT * 2.1) * 0.14;

      const dx = -Math.sin(swimT) * seed.radiusX;
      const dz = Math.cos(swimT * 1.3) * seed.radiusZ * 1.3;

      group.position.set(nx, ny, nz);
      group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz) - Math.PI / 2;
      // Hafif kuyruk salınımı
      group.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 7 + seed.phase) * 0.04;
    });
  });

  // Taban kumu seçili mi kontrol et
  const hasSilicaSand = tankItems.some((i) => i.itemId.toLowerCase().includes('silis') || i.itemId.toLowerCase().includes('kum'));

  return (
    <>
      {/* Stüdyo Ortamı ve Masa */}
      <StudioEnvironment tankHeight={height} />

      {/* Akvaryum Cam Yapısı */}
      <group>
        {/* Taban Camı */}
        <mesh position={[0, -height / 2, 0]} receiveShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[width, 0.08, depth]} />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#cffafe" transparent opacity={0.45} roughness={0.1} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Silis Taban Kumu */}
        <SilisKumMesh
          width={width - 0.12}
          depth={depth - 0.12}
          height={hasSilicaSand ? 0.32 : 0.22}
          position={[0, -height / 2 + 0.15, 0]}
        />

        {/* 4 Yan Cam Paneli (İçten görünümü bozmamak için double-pass yerine ince kenarlı cam) */}
        {/* Arka Cam */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0, -depth / 2]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[width, height, 0.05]} />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#e0f2fe" transparent opacity={0.22} roughness={0.08} depthWrite={false} />
        </mesh>
        {/* Ön Cam */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0, depth / 2]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[width, height, 0.05]} />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#ffffff" transparent opacity={0.12} roughness={0.05} depthWrite={false} />
        </mesh>
        {/* Sol Cam */}
        <mesh position={[-width / 2, 0, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.05, height, depth]} />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#e0f2fe" transparent opacity={0.18} roughness={0.08} depthWrite={false} />
        </mesh>
        {/* Sağ Cam */}
        <mesh position={[width / 2, 0, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.05, height, depth]} />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#e0f2fe" transparent opacity={0.18} roughness={0.08} depthWrite={false} />
        </mesh>
      </group>

      {/* Katalogdan Eklenen Tüm Canlılar, Bitkiler ve Işıklar */}
      {tankItems.map((item) => {
        const meta = catalogMap.get(item.itemId);
        return (
          <group
            key={item.instanceId}
            ref={(el) => {
              if (el) itemRefs.current.set(item.instanceId, el);
              else itemRefs.current.delete(item.instanceId);
            }}
          >
            <SceneObjectRenderer
              itemId={item.itemId}
              itemName={meta?.name || item.itemId}
              category={meta?.category || 'fish'}
              tankWidth={width}
              tankHeight={height}
              tankDepth={depth}
            />
          </group>
        );
      })}

      {/* Sürüklenebilir Akvaryum Ekipmanları (Filtre, Hava Motoru, Hava Taşı) */}
      {placedEquipments.map((eq) => (
        <DraggableEquipment key={eq.instanceId} equipment={eq} />
      ))}

      {/* Ekipman sürüklenirken kamera dönmesini kilitle */}
      <OrbitControls
        enabled={!isDraggingEquipment}
        enableDamping
        dampingFactor={0.06}
        minDistance={3}
        maxDistance={22}
        maxPolarAngle={Math.PI / 2 - 0.04}
      />
    </>
  );
}

function FpsMonitor({ onFpsUpdate }: { onFpsUpdate?: (fps: number) => void }) {
  const frames = useRef(0);
  const lastTime = useRef(performance.now());

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!onFpsUpdate) return;
    frames.current += 1;
    const now = performance.now();
    if (now - lastTime.current >= 1000) {
      onFpsUpdate(Math.round((frames.current * 1000) / (now - lastTime.current)));
      frames.current = 0;
      lastTime.current = now;
    }
  });

  return null;
}

export default function AquariumScene({ onFpsUpdate }: AquariumSceneProps) {
  const waterColor = useAquariumStore((s) => s.ecosystemStatus.waterVisuals.color);
  const waterOpacity = useAquariumStore((s) => s.ecosystemStatus.waterVisuals.opacity);
  const tankDimensions = useAquariumStore((s) => s.tankConfig.dimensions);

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full relative">
      <Canvas
        dpr={[1, 1.5]} // Retina ekranlarda 2x/3x pixel ratio kaynaklı GPU donmasını önler
        gl={{
          antialias: true,
          powerPreference: 'high-performance',
          toneMapping: THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping,
          toneMappingExposure: 1.05,
        }}
      >
        <PerspectiveCamera makeDefault position={[0, 2.2, 10.5]} fov={42} />

        {/* Stüdyo Aydınlatması */}
        <ambientLight intensity={0.65} />
        <directionalLight position={[6, 10, 7]} intensity={1.15} color="#ffffff" />
        <directionalLight position={[-5, 4, -4]} intensity={0.4} color="#bae6fd" />
        <Environment preset="studio" />

        {/* Dinamik Su Kütlesi (Biyolojik yük / amonyak arttıkça bulanıklaşır) */}
        <mesh position={[0, -0.08, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry
            args={[
              tankDimensions.width - 0.08,
              tankDimensions.height - 0.25,
              tankDimensions.depth - 0.08,
            ]}
          />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial
            color={waterColor || '#38bdf8'}
            transparent
            opacity={Math.min(Math.max(waterOpacity * 0.35, 0.14), 0.45)}
            roughness={0.12}
            depthWrite={false}
          />
        </mesh>

        <AnimatedTankContents />
        <FpsMonitor onFpsUpdate={onFpsUpdate} />
      </Canvas>
    </div>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: components\canvas\DraggableEquipment.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React, { useRef, useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { ThreeEvent, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import {
  useAquariumStore,
  PlacedEquipment,
} from '../../store/useAquariumStore';
import { getTankDimensionsCm } from '../../lib/simulation/ecosystemEngine';
import HavaMotoruMesh from '../../models/malzemeler/ekipman/HavaMotoruMesh';
import FiltreEkipmanMesh from '../../models/malzemeler/ekipman/FiltreEkipmanMesh';
import HavaTasiMesh from '../../models/malzemeler/ekipman/HavaTasiMesh';
import EquipmentConnectionsAndBubbles from './EquipmentConnectionsAndBubbles';

function ProceduralEquipmentMesh({
  item,
  isSelected,
  isColliding,
  isPumpActive,
  waterSurfaceY,
  tankHeight,
}: {
  item: PlacedEquipment;
  isSelected: boolean;
  isColliding: boolean;
  isPumpActive: boolean;
  waterSurfaceY: number;
  tankHeight: number;
}) {
  const sub = (item.product.sub_category || '').toLowerCase();
  const slug = (item.product.slug || '').toLowerCase();

  const ringColor = isColliding
    ? '#ef4444'
    : isSelected
    ? '#38bdf8'
    : '#94a3b8';

  const renderSelectionRing = () => {
    if (!isSelected && !isColliding) return null;

    if (item.zone === 'internal-wall') {
      // Cama yapışık iç filtre için arka vantuz çerçevesi vurgusu
      return (
        <mesh position={[0, 0.42, -0.095]}>
          <ringGeometry args={[0.16, 0.195, 32]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color={ringColor} side={THREE.DoubleSide} />
        </mesh>
      );
    }

    return (
      <mesh position={[0, 0.008, 0]} rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
        <ringGeometry
          args={[item.footprintRadius, item.footprintRadius + 0.036, 32]}
        />
        <meshBasicMaterial color={ringColor} side={THREE.DoubleSide} />
      </mesh>
    );
  };

  if (item.zone === 'external') {
    return (
      <group>
        <HavaMotoruMesh item={item} isActive={isPumpActive} />
        {renderSelectionRing()}
      </group>
    );
  }

  if (
    sub === 'hava-taslari' ||
    slug.includes('hava-tasi') ||
    slug.includes('difuzor')
  ) {
    return (
      <group>
        <HavaTasiMesh item={item} />
        {renderSelectionRing()}
      </group>
    );
  }

  return (
    <group>
      <FiltreEkipmanMesh
        item={item}
        waterSurfaceY={waterSurfaceY}
        tankHeight={tankHeight}
      />
      {renderSelectionRing()}
    </group>
  );
}

interface DraggableEquipmentItemProps {
  item: PlacedEquipment;
  sceneWidth: number;
  sceneHeight: number;
  sceneDepth: number;
  isPumpActive: boolean;
}

function DraggableEquipmentItem({
  item,
  sceneWidth,
  sceneHeight,
  sceneDepth,
  isPumpActive,
}: DraggableEquipmentItemProps) {
  const {
    placedEquipments,
    selectedEquipmentId,
    setSelectedEquipmentId,
    setIsDraggingEquipment,
    updateEquipmentTransform,
    rotateEquipment,
  } = useAquariumStore();

  const { camera, gl } = useThree();
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const [isColliding, setIsColliding] = useState(false);

  const dragPlane = useMemo(() => new THREE.Plane(), []);
  const intersectionPoint = useMemo(() => new THREE.Vector3(), []);
  const dragOffset = useRef(new THREE.Vector3());

  const isSelected = selectedEquipmentId === item.instanceId;
  const waterSurfaceY = -0.1 + (sceneHeight - 0.25) / 2;

  const getWallWallOrientation = (): 'back' | 'left' | 'right' => {
    const norm = Math.round(((item.rotationY % (Math.PI * 2)) / (Math.PI / 2)) % 4);
    if (norm === 1) return 'left';
    if (norm === 3) return 'right';
    return 'back';
  };

  const clampWallPosition = (
    rawX: number,
    rawY: number,
    rawZ: number
  ): [number, number, number] => {
    const orient = getWallWallOrientation();
    const filterH = 0.88;
    const minY = -sceneHeight / 2 + 0.28;
    const maxY = waterSurfaceY - filterH - 0.04;
    const clampedY = Math.min(Math.max(minY, maxY), Math.max(minY, rawY));

    if (orient === 'left') {
      const marginZ = 0.28;
      return [
        -sceneWidth / 2 + 0.145,
        clampedY,
        Math.min(sceneDepth / 2 - marginZ, Math.max(-sceneDepth / 2 + marginZ, rawZ)),
      ];
    }

    if (orient === 'right') {
      const marginZ = 0.28;
      return [
        sceneWidth / 2 - 0.145,
        clampedY,
        Math.min(sceneDepth / 2 - marginZ, Math.max(-sceneDepth / 2 + marginZ, rawZ)),
      ];
    }

    // Arka iç cam
    const marginX = 0.28;
    return [
      Math.min(sceneWidth / 2 - marginX, Math.max(-sceneWidth / 2 + marginX, rawX)),
      clampedY,
      -sceneDepth / 2 + 0.145,
    ];
  };

  const clampFloorPosition = (
    rawX: number,
    rawZ: number
  ): [number, number, number] => {
    const r = item.footprintRadius;
    const y = item.position[1];

    if (item.zone === 'internal') {
      const margin = 0.06 + r;
      const minX = -sceneWidth / 2 + margin;
      const maxX = sceneWidth / 2 - margin;
      const minZ = -sceneDepth / 2 + margin;
      const maxZ = sceneDepth / 2 - margin;

      return [
        Math.min(maxX, Math.max(minX, rawX)),
        y,
        Math.min(maxZ, Math.max(minZ, rawZ)),
      ];
    }

    const tankLeftEdge = -sceneWidth / 2;
    const minX = tankLeftEdge - 1.35;
    const maxX = tankLeftEdge - r - 0.12;
    const minZ = -sceneDepth / 2;
    const maxZ = sceneDepth / 2;

    return [
      Math.min(maxX, Math.max(minX, rawX)),
      y,
      Math.min(maxZ, Math.max(minZ, rawZ)),
    ];
  };

  const checkCollision = (x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean => {
    for (const other of placedEquipments) {
      if (other.instanceId === item.instanceId) continue;
      if (other.zone !== item.zone) continue;

      if (item.zone === 'internal-wall') {
        const dist3D = Math.hypot(
          other.position[0] - x,
          other.position[1] - y,
          other.position[2] - z
        );
        if (dist3D < 0.34) return true;
        continue;
      }

      const dx = other.position[0] - x;
      const dz = other.position[2] - z;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (dist < item.footprintRadius + other.footprintRadius) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  };

  const configureDragPlane = () => {
    if (item.zone === 'internal-wall') {
      const orient = getWallWallOrientation();
      if (orient === 'left') {
        dragPlane.setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(
          new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0),
          new THREE.Vector3(item.position[0], 0, 0)
        );
      } else if (orient === 'right') {
        dragPlane.setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(
          new THREE.Vector3(-1, 0, 0),
          new THREE.Vector3(item.position[0], 0, 0)
        );
      } else {
        dragPlane.setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(
          new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1),
          new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, item.position[2])
        );
      }
    } else {
      dragPlane.setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, item.position[1], 0)
      );
    }
  };

  const handlePointerDown = (e: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setSelectedEquipmentId(item.instanceId);
    setDragging(true);
    setIsDraggingEquipment(true);

    configureDragPlane();

    if (e.ray.intersectPlane(dragPlane, intersectionPoint)) {
      dragOffset.current.set(
        item.position[0] - intersectionPoint.x,
        item.position[1] - intersectionPoint.y,
        item.position[2] - intersectionPoint.z
      );
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dragging) return;

    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    const pointer = new THREE.Vector2();

    const onWindowPointerMove = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
      const rect = gl.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();
      pointer.x = ((ev.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      pointer.y = -((ev.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;

      raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);
      if (raycaster.ray.intersectPlane(dragPlane, intersectionPoint)) {
        const targetX = intersectionPoint.x + dragOffset.current.x;
        const targetY = intersectionPoint.y + dragOffset.current.y;
        const targetZ = intersectionPoint.z + dragOffset.current.z;

        const clamped =
          item.zone === 'internal-wall'
            ? clampWallPosition(targetX, targetY, targetZ)
            : clampFloorPosition(targetX, targetZ);

        const collides = checkCollision(clamped[0], clamped[1], clamped[2]);
        setIsColliding(collides);

        if (!collides) {
          updateEquipmentTransform(item.instanceId, clamped);
        }
      }
    };

    const onWindowPointerUp = () => {
      setDragging(false);
      setIsDraggingEquipment(false);
      setIsColliding(false);
    };

    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onWindowPointerMove);
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onWindowPointerUp);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onWindowPointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onWindowPointerUp);
    };
  }, [
    dragging,
    camera,
    gl,
    dragPlane,
    item.instanceId,
    item.zone,
    item.rotationY,
    placedEquipments,
    sceneWidth,
    sceneHeight,
    sceneDepth,
  ]);

  const handleDoubleClick = (e: ThreeEvent<MouseEvent>) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    rotateEquipment(item.instanceId, Math.PI / 4);
  };

  return (
    <group
      position={item.position}
      rotation={[0, item.rotationY, 0]}
      onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
      onDoubleClick={handleDoubleClick}
    >
      <ProceduralEquipmentMesh
        item={item}
        isSelected={isSelected}
        isColliding={isColliding}
        isPumpActive={isPumpActive}
        waterSurfaceY={waterSurfaceY}
        tankHeight={sceneHeight}
      />
    </group>
  );
}

export interface DraggableEquipmentProps {
  tankWidth?: number;
  tankHeight?: number;
  tankDepth?: number;
}

export default function DraggableEquipment({
  tankWidth,
  tankHeight,
  tankDepth,
}: DraggableEquipmentProps) {
  const placedEquipments = useAquariumStore((state) => state.placedEquipments);
  const tankCapacity = useAquariumStore((state) => state.tankCapacity);

  if (!placedEquipments || placedEquipments.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const defaultDims = getTankDimensionsCm(tankCapacity);
  const resolvedWidth = tankWidth ?? defaultDims.sceneWidth;
  const resolvedHeight = tankHeight ?? defaultDims.sceneHeight;
  const resolvedDepth = tankDepth ?? defaultDims.sceneDepth;

  const hasAirConsumer = placedEquipments.some(
    (e) => e.zone === 'internal' && e.product.needs_air_pump === true
  );

  return (
    <group name="draggable-equipments-group">
      {placedEquipments.map((item) => (
        <DraggableEquipmentItem
          key={item.instanceId}
          item={item}
          sceneWidth={resolvedWidth}
          sceneHeight={resolvedHeight}
          sceneDepth={resolvedDepth}
          isPumpActive={item.zone === 'external' && hasAirConsumer}
        />
      ))}

      <EquipmentConnectionsAndBubbles
        placedEquipments={placedEquipments}
        tankWidth={resolvedWidth}
        tankHeight={resolvedHeight}
      />
    </group>
  );
}

export { DraggableEquipment };

========================================
DOSYA: components\canvas\EquipmentConnectionsAndBubbles.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React, { useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { PlacedSceneItem } from './SceneObjectRegistry';

export interface TankBounds {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  depth: number;
  minX: number;
  maxX: number;
  minY: number;
  maxY: number;
  minZ: number;
  maxZ: number;
  substrateTop: number;
  waterTop: number;
}

interface EquipmentConnectionsAndBubblesProps {
  items: PlacedSceneItem[];
  tankBounds: TankBounds;
}

interface BubbleParticle {
  originX: number;
  originY: number;
  originZ: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  z: number;
  speed: number;
  wobblePhase: number;
  wobbleFreq: number;
  scale: number;
}

const _dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
const MAX_BUBBLES = 240;

export function EquipmentConnectionsAndBubbles({
  items,
  tankBounds,
}: EquipmentConnectionsAndBubblesProps) {
  const instancedBubblesRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);

  const { airPumps, aerators } = useMemo(() => {
    const pumps = items.filter((i) => i.category === 'hava_motorlari');
    const stonesAndFilters = items.filter(
      (i) => i.category === 'hava_taslari' || i.category === 'filtreler'
    );
    return { airPumps: pumps, aerators: stonesAndFilters };
  }, [items]);

  const tubeGeometries = useMemo(() => {
    if (airPumps.length === 0 || aerators.length === 0) return [];

    const primaryPump = airPumps[0];
    const pumpPos = new THREE.Vector3(
      primaryPump.position?.[0] ?? tankBounds.maxX + 0.08,
      primaryPump.position?.[1] ?? 0.04,
      primaryPump.position?.[2] ?? 0
    );

    return aerators.map((aerator) => {
      const targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(
        aerator.position?.[0] ?? 0,
        (aerator.position?.[1] ?? tankBounds.substrateTop) + 0.03,
        aerator.position?.[2] ?? 0
      );

      const rimY = tankBounds.height + 0.025;
      const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
        pumpPos,
        new THREE.Vector3(pumpPos.x, rimY * 0.6, pumpPos.z),
        new THREE.Vector3(
          THREE.MathUtils.lerp(pumpPos.x, targetPos.x, 0.35),
          rimY,
          THREE.MathUtils.lerp(pumpPos.z, targetPos.z, 0.35)
        ),
        new THREE.Vector3(targetPos.x, rimY - 0.02, targetPos.z),
        targetPos,
      ]);

      return new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 32, 0.0022, 6, false);
    });
  }, [airPumps, aerators, tankBounds]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      tubeGeometries.forEach((geo) => geo.dispose());
    };
  }, [tubeGeometries]);

  const hoseMaterial = useMemo(
    () =>
      new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({
        color: '#cbd5e1',
        transparent: true,
        opacity: 0.48,
        depthWrite: false,
      }),
    []
  );

  const bubbleParticles = useMemo<BubbleParticle[]>(() => {
    if (aerators.length === 0) return [];
    const perSource = Math.floor(MAX_BUBBLES / aerators.length);
    const list: BubbleParticle[] = [];

    aerators.forEach((src) => {
      const ox = src.position?.[0] ?? 0;
      const oy = (src.position?.[1] ?? tankBounds.substrateTop) + 0.035;
      const oz = src.position?.[2] ?? 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < perSource; i++) {
        const startY = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(oy, tankBounds.waterTop, Math.random());
        list.push({
          originX: ox,
          originY: oy,
          originZ: oz,
          x: ox + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.025,
          y: startY,
          z: oz + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.025,
          speed: 0.14 + Math.random() * 0.12,
          wobblePhase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
          wobbleFreq: 3.5 + Math.random() * 2.5,
          scale: 0.0016 + Math.random() * 0.0018,
        });
      }
    });

    return list;
  }, [aerators, tankBounds]);

  useFrame((state, delta) => {
    const mesh = instancedBubblesRef.current;
    if (!mesh || bubbleParticles.length === 0) return;

    const clampedDelta = Math.min(delta, 0.05);
    const elapsed = state.clock.elapsedTime;
    const waterSurface = tankBounds.waterTop;

    for (let i = 0; i < bubbleParticles.length; i++) {
      const p = bubbleParticles[i];
      p.y += p.speed * clampedDelta;

      if (p.y >= waterSurface) {
        p.y = p.originY;
        p.x = p.originX + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02;
        p.z = p.originZ + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.02;
      }

      const wobbleX = Math.sin(elapsed * p.wobbleFreq + p.wobblePhase) * 0.004;
      const wobbleZ = Math.cos(elapsed * p.wobbleFreq + p.wobblePhase) * 0.004;

      _dummy.position.set(p.x + wobbleX, p.y, p.z + wobbleZ);
      _dummy.scale.setScalar(p.scale);
      _dummy.updateMatrix();
      mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _dummy.matrix);
    }

    mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      hoseMaterial.dispose();
    };
  }, [hoseMaterial]);

  return (
    <group name="equipment-connections-and-bubbles">
      {tubeGeometries.map((geo, idx) => (
        <mesh
          key={idx}
          geometry={geo}
          material={hoseMaterial}
          raycast={() => null}
        />
      ))}

      {bubbleParticles.length > 0 && (
        <instancedMesh
          ref={instancedBubblesRef}
          args={[undefined, undefined, bubbleParticles.length]}
          frustumCulled={false}
          raycast={() => null}
        >
          <sphereGeometry args={[1, 8, 8]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial
            color="#e0f2fe"
            transparent
            opacity={0.55}
            depthWrite={false}
          />
        </instancedMesh>
      )}
    </group>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: components\canvas\SceneObjectRegistry.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React, { useMemo, useRef, useLayoutEffect, useEffect } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { TankBounds } from './EquipmentConnectionsAndBubbles';

export type CatalogCategory =
  | 'baliklar'
  | 'karidesler'
  | 'salyangozlar'
  | 'bitkiler'
  | 'kumlar'
  | 'aydinlatmalar'
  | 'hava_motorlari'
  | 'hava_taslari'
  | 'filtreler';

export interface PlacedSceneItem {
  instanceId: string;
  catalogId: string | number;
  name: string;
  category: CatalogCategory;
  variant?: string;
  primaryColor?: string;
  secondaryColor?: string;
  scale?: number;
  position?: [number, number, number];
  rotation?: [number, number, number];
}

interface SceneObjectRegistryProps {
  items: PlacedSceneItem[];
  tankBounds: TankBounds;
  selectedItemId?: string | null;
  onSelectItem?: (id: string | null) => void;
}

// ============================================================================
// 1. SİNGLETON YÜKSEK ÇÖZÜNÜRLÜKLÜ (512x256) ÖNBELLEK + BOŞTA ÖN-ÜRETİM
// ============================================================================
interface CachedAsset {
  geometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  material: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
}

const proceduralAssetCache = new Map<string, CachedAsset>();
const _creatureDummy = new THREE.Object3D();

/**
 * Yüksek kaliteli (512x256) prosedürel doku üreticisi.
 * Önbellekte tutulduğu için her varyant sadece 1 kez üretilir.
 */
function createHighResCreatureTexture(
  primaryHex: string,
  secondaryHex: string,
  pattern: 'fish' | 'shrimp' | 'snail'
): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = 512;
  canvas.height = 256;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 512, 0);
  grad.addColorStop(0, primaryHex);
  grad.addColorStop(0.55, secondaryHex);
  grad.addColorStop(1, primaryHex);
  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 256);

  if (pattern === 'fish') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
    for (let x = 40; x < 460; x += 28) {
      for (let y = 24; y < 232; y += 28) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(x, y, 10, 0, Math.PI);
        ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  } else if (pattern === 'shrimp') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.24)';
    for (let x = 64; x < 440; x += 72) {
      ctx.fillRect(x, 0, 20, 256);
    }
  } else if (pattern === 'snail') {
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(30,20,10,0.30)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 10;
    for (let y = 28; y < 256; y += 44) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, y);
      ctx.lineTo(512, y + 14);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }

  const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  texture.anisotropy = 4;
  texture.needsUpdate = true;
  return texture;
}

/**
 * InstancedMesh uyumlu, her örneğe (instance) özel `aPhase` fazı tanıyan
 * GPU Vertex Shader yüzme materyali.
 */
function createInstancedSwimmingMaterial(
  texture: THREE.Texture,
  swimAmplitude: number,
  swimFrequency: number
): THREE.MeshStandardMaterial {
  const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map: texture,
    roughness: 0.32,
    metalness: 0.08,
  });

  mat.customProgramCacheKey = () =>
    `fishlab_instanced_swim_${swimAmplitude}_${swimFrequency}`;

  mat.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.uTime = { value: 0 };
    shader.uniforms.uAmp = { value: swimAmplitude };
    shader.uniforms.uFreq = { value: swimFrequency };
    mat.userData.shader = shader;

    shader.vertexShader =
      `
      attribute float aPhase;
      uniform float uTime;
      uniform float uAmp;
      uniform float uFreq;
      ` + shader.vertexShader;

    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
      '#include <begin_vertex>',
      `
      #include <begin_vertex>
      float tailFactor = clamp((-transformed.z + 0.02) * 18.0, 0.0, 1.5);
      transformed.x += sin(uTime * uFreq + aPhase + transformed.z * 35.0) * uAmp * tailFactor;
      `
    );
  };

  return mat;
}

function buildFishAssets(primary: string, secondary: string): CachedAsset {
  const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.016, 20, 14);
  geo.scale(0.55, 0.85, 2.2);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  const tex = createHighResCreatureTexture(primary, secondary, 'fish');
  const mat = createInstancedSwimmingMaterial(tex, 0.0045, 9.0);
  return { geometry: geo, material: mat };
}

function buildShrimpAssets(primary: string, secondary: string): CachedAsset {
  const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.003, 0.014),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.005, 0.004),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.003, -0.006),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.001, -0.015),
  ]);
  const geo = new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 18, 0.0032, 10, false);
  const tex = createHighResCreatureTexture(primary, secondary, 'shrimp');
  const mat = createInstancedSwimmingMaterial(tex, 0.0015, 6.0);
  return { geometry: geo, material: mat };
}

function buildSnailAssets(primary: string, secondary: string): CachedAsset {
  const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.012, 20, 16);
  geo.scale(1.05, 0.95, 1.15);
  const tex = createHighResCreatureTexture(primary, secondary, 'snail');
  const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    map: tex,
    roughness: 0.24,
    metalness: 0.05,
  });
  return { geometry: geo, material: mat };
}

function getOrCreateCachedAsset(
  category: CatalogCategory,
  primary: string,
  secondary: string
): CachedAsset {
  const cacheKey = `${category}_${primary}_${secondary}`;
  const existing = proceduralAssetCache.get(cacheKey);
  if (existing) return existing;

  const created =
    category === 'karidesler'
      ? buildShrimpAssets(primary, secondary)
      : category === 'salyangozlar'
      ? buildSnailAssets(primary, secondary)
      : buildFishAssets(primary, secondary);

  proceduralAssetCache.set(cacheKey, created);
  return created;
}

// ============================================================================
// 2. TÜR BAZLI INSTANCED CANLI SÜRÜSÜ (30 CANLI = 1 DRAW CALL + BAĞIMSIZ HAREKET)
// ============================================================================
interface CreatureNav {
  pos: THREE.Vector3;
  target: THREE.Vector3;
  yaw: number;
  speed: number;
  timer: number;
  phase: number;
}

function SpeciesInstancedSwarm({
  groupKey,
  members,
  tankBounds,
  selectedItemId,
  onSelectItem,
}: {
  groupKey: string;
  members: PlacedSceneItem[];
  tankBounds: TankBounds;
  selectedItemId?: string | null;
  onSelectItem?: (id: string | null) => void;
}) {
  const instancedRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
  const sample = members[0];
  const isBottomDweller =
    sample.category === 'karidesler' || sample.category === 'salyangozlar';

  const primary =
    sample.primaryColor || (sample.category === 'karidesler' ? '#dc2626' : '#f59e0b');
  const secondary = sample.secondaryColor || '#fde68a';

  const { geometry, material } = useMemo(() => {
    const base = getOrCreateCachedAsset(sample.category, primary, secondary);
    const clonedGeo = base.geometry.clone();
    const phases = new Float32Array(Math.max(members.length, 64));
    for (let i = 0; i < phases.length; i++) {
      phases[i] = (i * 1.6180339) % (Math.PI * 2);
    }
    clonedGeo.setAttribute('aPhase', new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(phases, 1));
    return { geometry: clonedGeo, material: base.material };
  }, [sample.category, primary, secondary, members.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      geometry.dispose();
    };
  }, [geometry]);

  // Her bir canlı örneğinin bağımsız yapay zeka/yüzme durumu
  const navMapRef = useRef(new Map<string, CreatureNav>());

  const navList = useMemo(() => {
    const map = navMapRef.current;
    return members.map((m, idx) => {
      let nav = map.get(m.instanceId);
      if (!nav) {
        nav = {
          pos: new THREE.Vector3(
            m.position?.[0] ?? (Math.random() - 0.5) * (tankBounds.width * 0.65),
            isBottomDweller
              ? tankBounds.substrateTop + 0.012
              : THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                  tankBounds.minY + 0.03,
                  tankBounds.maxY - 0.03,
                  Math.random()
                ),
            m.position?.[2] ?? (Math.random() - 0.5) * (tankBounds.depth * 0.65)
          ),
          target: new THREE.Vector3(
            THREE.MathUtils.lerp(tankBounds.minX, tankBounds.maxX, Math.random()),
            isBottomDweller
              ? tankBounds.substrateTop + 0.012
              : THREE.MathUtils.lerp(tankBounds.minY, tankBounds.maxY, Math.random()),
            THREE.MathUtils.lerp(tankBounds.minZ, tankBounds.maxZ, Math.random())
          ),
          yaw: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
          speed:
            m.category === 'salyangozlar'
              ? 0.006
              : m.category === 'karidesler'
              ? 0.026
              : 0.068,
          timer: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3.0,
          phase: idx * 1.1,
        };
        map.set(m.instanceId, nav);
      }
      return nav;
    });
  }, [members, tankBounds, isBottomDweller]);

  useFrame((state, delta) => {
    const mesh = instancedRef.current;
    if (!mesh || navList.length === 0) return;

    const dt = Math.min(delta, 0.05);
    const elapsed = state.clock.elapsedTime;

    if (material.userData?.shader) {
      material.userData.shader.uniforms.uTime.value = elapsed;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < navList.length; i++) {
      const nav = navList[i];
      const item = members[i];
      nav.timer -= dt;

      if (nav.timer <= 0 || nav.pos.distanceToSquared(nav.target) < 0.001) {
        nav.timer = 2.2 + Math.random() * 3.5;
        nav.target.set(
          THREE.MathUtils.lerp(tankBounds.minX, tankBounds.maxX, Math.random()),
          isBottomDweller
            ? tankBounds.substrateTop + 0.012
            : THREE.MathUtils.lerp(tankBounds.minY, tankBounds.maxY, Math.random()),
          THREE.MathUtils.lerp(tankBounds.minZ, tankBounds.maxZ, Math.random())
        );
      }

      const dx = nav.target.x - nav.pos.x;
      const dy = nav.target.y - nav.pos.y;
      const dz = nav.target.z - nav.pos.z;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);

      if (dist > 0.0001) {
        const step = Math.min(nav.speed * dt, dist);
        nav.pos.x += (dx / dist) * step;
        nav.pos.y += (dy / dist) * step;
        nav.pos.z += (dz / dist) * step;

        const targetYaw = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
        nav.yaw = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(nav.yaw, targetYaw, dt * 4.5);
      }

      const s = item.scale ?? 1;
      _creatureDummy.position.copy(nav.pos);
      _creatureDummy.rotation.set(0, nav.yaw, 0);
      _creatureDummy.scale.set(s, s, s);
      _creatureDummy.updateMatrix();
      mesh.setMatrixAt(i, _creatureDummy.matrix);
    }

    mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  const selectedIndex = members.findIndex((m) => m.instanceId === selectedItemId);

  return (
    <group name={`swarm-${groupKey}`}>
      <instancedMesh
        ref={instancedRef}
        args={[geometry, material, members.length]}
        frustumCulled={false}
        castShadow={false}
        receiveShadow={false}
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (e.instanceId !== undefined && members[e.instanceId]) {
            onSelectItem?.(members[e.instanceId].instanceId);
          }
        }}
      />

      {selectedIndex !== -1 && navList[selectedIndex] && (
        <mesh
          position={[
            navList[selectedIndex].pos.x,
            navList[selectedIndex].pos.y + 0.026,
            navList[selectedIndex].pos.z,
          ]}
        >
          <octahedronGeometry args={[0.006, 0]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color="#38bdf8" />
        </mesh>
      )}
    </group>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// 3. INSTANCEDMESH BİTKİ KÜMESİ
// ============================================================================
function InstancedAquariumPlants({
  plants,
  tankBounds,
  selectedItemId,
  onSelectItem,
}: {
  plants: PlacedSceneItem[];
  tankBounds: TankBounds;
  selectedItemId?: string | null;
  onSelectItem?: (id: string | null) => void;
}) {
  const leafInstancedRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
  const leavesPerPlant = 9;
  const totalLeaves = plants.length * leavesPerPlant;

  const { leafGeometry, leafMaterial } = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.018, 12, 10);
    geo.scale(0.65, 0.12, 1.5);
    geo.translate(0, 0, 0.018);

    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: '#15803d',
      roughness: 0.52,
      metalness: 0.02,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    });

    return { leafGeometry: geo, leafMaterial: mat };
  }, []);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const mesh = leafInstancedRef.current;
    if (!mesh || totalLeaves === 0) return;

    const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
    const color = new THREE.Color();
    let idx = 0;

    plants.forEach((plant, pIdx) => {
      const px = plant.position?.[0] ?? ((pIdx % 4) - 1.5) * 0.14;
      const py = tankBounds.substrateTop;
      const pz = plant.position?.[2] ?? 0;
      const baseScale = plant.scale ?? 1;
      color.set(plant.primaryColor || '#16a34a');

      for (let l = 0; l < leavesPerPlant; l++) {
        const angle = (l / leavesPerPlant) * Math.PI * 2 + pIdx * 0.7;
        const tilt = 0.35 + (l % 3) * 0.28;
        const leafScale = baseScale * (0.75 + (l % 3) * 0.22);

        dummy.position.set(px, py + 0.008, pz);
        dummy.rotation.set(-tilt, angle, 0);
        dummy.scale.setScalar(leafScale);
        dummy.updateMatrix();

        mesh.setMatrixAt(idx, dummy.matrix);
        mesh.setColorAt(idx, color);
        idx++;
      }
    });

    mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    if (mesh.instanceColor) mesh.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
  }, [plants, tankBounds, totalLeaves]);

  if (plants.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <group name="instanced-aquarium-plants">
      <instancedMesh
        ref={leafInstancedRef}
        args={[leafGeometry, leafMaterial, totalLeaves]}
        castShadow={false}
        receiveShadow
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (e.instanceId !== undefined) {
            const plantIndex = Math.floor(e.instanceId / leavesPerPlant);
            const targetPlant = plants[plantIndex];
            if (targetPlant) onSelectItem?.(targetPlant.instanceId);
          }
        }}
      />
      {plants.map(
        (plant, idx) =>
          selectedItemId === plant.instanceId && (
            <mesh
              key={plant.instanceId}
              position={[
                plant.position?.[0] ?? ((idx % 4) - 1.5) * 0.14,
                tankBounds.substrateTop + 0.08,
                plant.position?.[2] ?? 0,
              ]}
            >
              <octahedronGeometry args={[0.008, 0]} />
              <meshBasicMaterial color="#4ade80" />
            </mesh>
          )
      )}
    </group>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// 4. EKİPMAN BİLEŞENİ
// ============================================================================
function StaticEquipmentItem({
  item,
  tankBounds,
  isSelected,
  onSelect,
}: {
  item: PlacedSceneItem;
  tankBounds: TankBounds;
  isSelected: boolean;
  onSelect?: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const defaultPos: [number, number, number] = useMemo(() => {
    if (item.position) return item.position;
    if (item.category === 'hava_motorlari') {
      return [tankBounds.maxX + 0.09, 0.03, -tankBounds.depth * 0.25];
    }
    if (item.category === 'aydinlatmalar') {
      return [0, tankBounds.height + 0.035, 0];
    }
    return [tankBounds.minX + 0.06, tankBounds.substrateTop, tankBounds.minZ + 0.06];
  }, [item.category, item.position, tankBounds]);

  return (
    <group
      position={defaultPos}
      rotation={item.rotation ?? [0, 0, 0]}
      onClick={(e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        onSelect?.(item.instanceId);
      }}
    >
      {item.category === 'filtreler' && (
        <group>
          <mesh position={[0, 0.035, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.026, 0.026, 0.07, 16]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.9} />
          </mesh>
          <mesh position={[0, 0.09, 0]} castShadow>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.006, 0.006, 0.06, 10]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#334155" roughness={0.4} />
          </mesh>
        </group>
      )}

      {item.category === 'hava_taslari' && (
        <mesh position={[0, 0.01, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.014, 0.016, 0.02, 12]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.85} />
        </mesh>
      )}

      {item.category === 'hava_motorlari' && (
        <mesh position={[0, 0.025, 0]} castShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.065, 0.045, 0.095]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.35} metalness={0.2} />
        </mesh>
      )}

      {item.category === 'aydinlatmalar' && (
        <mesh castShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[tankBounds.width * 0.88, 0.014, 0.055]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#cbd5e1" roughness={0.3} metalness={0.6} />
        </mesh>
      )}

      {isSelected && (
        <mesh position={[0, 0.13, 0]}>
          <octahedronGeometry args={[0.008, 0]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color="#facc15" />
        </mesh>
      )}
    </group>
  );
}

// ============================================================================
// 5. ANA KAYIT DEFTERİ VE BOŞTA ÖN-ISITMA (IDLE PREWARM)
// ============================================================================
export function SceneObjectRegistry({
  items,
  tankBounds,
  selectedItemId = null,
  onSelectItem,
}: SceneObjectRegistryProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const prewarmPresets: Array<[CatalogCategory, string, string]> = [
      ['karidesler', '#1e293b', '#e2e8f0'], // Carbon Rilli
      ['karidesler', '#2563eb', '#60a5fa'], // Mavi Melek
      ['karidesler', '#dc2626', '#f87171'], // Bloody Mary
      ['karidesler', '#ea580c', '#fdba74'], // Turuncu Sakura
      ['karidesler', '#eab308', '#fef08a'], // Sarı Ateş Neon
      ['salyangozlar', '#78350f', '#b45309'], // Kahverengi Elma Salyangozu
      ['baliklar', '#f97316', '#fde047'], // Lepistes
    ];

    const warmUp = () => {
      for (const [cat, c1, c2] of prewarmPresets) {
        getOrCreateCachedAsset(cat, c1, c2);
      }
    };

    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'requestIdleCallback' in window) {
      const id = (
        window as unknown as { requestIdleCallback: (cb: () => void) => number }
      ).requestIdleCallback(warmUp);
      return () =>
        (
          window as unknown as { cancelIdleCallback: (id: number) => void }
        ).cancelIdleCallback(id);
    } else {
      const timer = setTimeout(warmUp, 600);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, []);

  const { creatureGroups, plants, equipments } = useMemo(() => {
    const groups = new Map<string, PlacedSceneItem[]>();
    const plantList: PlacedSceneItem[] = [];
    const equipmentList: PlacedSceneItem[] = [];

    for (const item of items) {
      if (
        item.category === 'baliklar' ||
        item.category === 'karidesler' ||
        item.category === 'salyangozlar'
      ) {
        const key = `${item.category}_${item.catalogId}_${item.primaryColor ?? ''}_${item.secondaryColor ?? ''}`;
        const list = groups.get(key) ?? [];
        list.push(item);
        groups.set(key, list);
      } else if (item.category === 'bitkiler') {
        plantList.push(item);
      } else if (item.category !== 'kumlar') {
        equipmentList.push(item);
      }
    }

    return {
      creatureGroups: Array.from(groups.entries()),
      plants: plantList,
      equipments: equipmentList,
    };
  }, [items]);

  return (
    <group name="scene-object-registry">
      {creatureGroups.map(([groupKey, members]) => (
        <SpeciesInstancedSwarm
          key={groupKey}
          groupKey={groupKey}
          members={members}
          tankBounds={tankBounds}
          selectedItemId={selectedItemId}
          onSelectItem={onSelectItem}
        />
      ))}

      <InstancedAquariumPlants
        plants={plants}
        tankBounds={tankBounds}
        selectedItemId={selectedItemId}
        onSelectItem={onSelectItem}
      />

      {equipments.map((eq) => (
        <StaticEquipmentItem
          key={eq.instanceId}
          item={eq}
          tankBounds={tankBounds}
          isSelected={selectedItemId === eq.instanceId}
          onSelect={onSelectItem}
        />
      ))}
    </group>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ContactShadows } from '@react-three/drei';

interface StudioEnvironmentProps {
  tankWidth: number;
  tankDepth: number;
  tankHeight: number;
}

/**
 * Sol teknik zondaki 4 sabit hava motoru slotunun koordinatları (sadece kod içinde hesaplanır, sahnede çizilmez).
 */
export function getAirPumpSlotPosition(
  slotIndex: number,
  tankWidth: number,
  tankDepth: number,
  tankHeight: number
): [number, number, number] {
  const clampedIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, slotIndex));
  const tableTopY = -tankHeight / 2;
  const x = -tankWidth / 2 - 0.78;
  const startZ = tankDepth / 2 - 0.55;
  const stepZ = Math.max(0.75, (tankDepth - 0.85) / 3);
  const z = startZ - clampedIndex * stepZ;

  return [x, tableTopY, z];
}

export default function StudioEnvironment({
  tankWidth,
  tankDepth,
  tankHeight,
}: StudioEnvironmentProps) {
  const tableTopY = -tankHeight / 2;
  const matThickness = 0.03;
  const topThickness = 0.18;
  const cabinetHeight = 4.2;

  // Kompakt yan paylar (sol: motorlar, sağ: ileride yem/gübre)
  const sideMargin = 1.45;
  const benchWidth = tankWidth + sideMargin * 2;
  const benchDepth = tankDepth + 0.55;

  return (
    <group name="studio-environment">
      {/* Suyun mavisini ve camı öne çıkaran, ışığa boğmayan dengeli koyu-slate arka plan */}
      <color attach="background" args={['#1b222c']} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={['#1b222c', 16, 36]} />

      {/* İnce Siyah Akvaryum Altlık Şiltesi */}
      <mesh position={[0, tableTopY - matThickness / 2, 0]} receiveShadow>
        <boxGeometry args={[tankWidth + 0.02, matThickness, tankDepth + 0.02]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#111418" roughness={0.9} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Kompakt Mat Antrasit Üst Tabla */}
      <mesh
        position={[0, tableTopY - matThickness - topThickness / 2, 0]}
        receiveShadow
        castShadow
      >
        <boxGeometry args={[benchWidth, topThickness, benchDepth]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#2a313c" roughness={0.65} metalness={0.08} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Alt Akvaryum Dolabı Gövdesi */}
      <mesh
        position={[
          0,
          tableTopY - matThickness - topThickness - cabinetHeight / 2,
          0,
        ]}
        receiveShadow
        castShadow
      >
        <boxGeometry
          args={[benchWidth - 0.18, cabinetHeight, benchDepth - 0.16]}
        />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#1f252e" roughness={0.78} metalness={0.05} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Yumuşak Temas Gölgesi */}
      <ContactShadows
        position={[0, tableTopY + 0.002, 0]}
        opacity={0.45}
        scale={benchWidth + 1}
        blur={1.6}
        far={3}
        color="#0b0f14"
      />
    </group>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: components\canvas\models\modelCache.ts
========================================
import * as THREE from 'three';

type BuildFn = () => THREE.Group;

interface CachedEntry {
  template: THREE.Group;
  refCount: number;
}

const modelCache = new Map<string, CachedEntry>();

/**
 * Ağır prosedürel geometrileri ve Canvas dokularını (Beta, Lepistes, Karides, Vatoz, Salyangoz)
 * tür/varyant anahtarına göre bellekte 1 kez üretir ve klonlayarak döndürür.
 * GPU shader derleme maliyetini azaltmak için ağır materyalleri hafifletir.
 */
export function getCachedModel(key: string, builder: BuildFn): THREE.Group {
  let entry = modelCache.get(key);

  if (!entry) {
    const rawGroup = builder();

    // Performans optimizasyonu: Gereksiz çoklu transparent/physical pass yükünü hafiflet
    rawGroup.traverse((child) => {
      if ((child as THREE.Mesh).isMesh) {
        const mesh = child as THREE.Mesh;
        mesh.castShadow = false; // Küçük canlılarda gölge haritası FPS'i %40'a kadar düşürür
        mesh.receiveShadow = false;
        mesh.frustumCulled = true;

        const mat = mesh.material as THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
        if (mat && mat.isMeshPhysicalMaterial) {
          // Çok ağır clearcoat + iridescence kombinasyonunu tek pass'e indir
          mat.clearcoat = Math.min(mat.clearcoat || 0, 0.35);
          mat.iridescence = Math.min(mat.iridescence || 0, 0.45);
          mat.envMapIntensity = 0.85;
          mat.needsUpdate = true;
        }
      }
    });

    entry = { template: rawGroup, refCount: 0 };
    modelCache.set(key, entry);
  }

  entry.refCount += 1;

  // Geometri ve materyalleri paylaşarak sadece transform hiyerarşisini klonla
  const clone = entry.template.clone(true);
  clone.userData = { ...entry.template.userData, cacheKey: key };
  return clone;
}

export function clearModelCache(): void {
  modelCache.forEach((entry) => {
    entry.template.traverse((child) => {
      if ((child as THREE.Mesh).isMesh) {
        const mesh = child as THREE.Mesh;
        mesh.geometry?.dispose();
        const materials = Array.isArray(mesh.material) ? mesh.material : [mesh.material];
        materials.forEach((m) => {
          if (!m) return;
          Object.values(m).forEach((val) => {
            if (val && (val as THREE.Texture).isTexture) {
              (val as THREE.Texture).dispose();
            }
          });
          m.dispose();
        });
      }
    });
  });
  modelCache.clear();
}
