interface SubCategoryConfig {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}

interface CategoryThemeConfig {
  id: Product['category'];
  label: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  activeTabClass: string;
  activeSubClass: string;
  badgeTextClass: string;
  borderAccentClass: string;
  subCategories: SubCategoryConfig[];
}

const CATEGORY_THEMES: CategoryThemeConfig[] = [
  {
    id: 'canlilar',
    label: 'Canlılar',
    icon: Fish,
    activeTabClass:
      'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border-cyan-400/60 shadow-sm shadow-cyan-500/15',
    activeSubClass: 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 font-bold',
    badgeTextClass: 'text-cyan-400',
    borderAccentClass: 'hover:border-cyan-500/40',
    subCategories: [
      { id: 'baliklar', label: 'Balıklar', icon: Fish },
      { id: 'karidesler', label: 'Karidesler', icon: Sparkles },
      { id: 'salyangozlar', label: 'Salyangozlar', icon: Shell },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'bitkiler',
    label: 'Bitkiler',
    icon: Sprout,
    activeTabClass:
      'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-400/60 shadow-sm shadow-emerald-500/15',
    activeSubClass: 'bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-bold',
    badgeTextClass: 'text-emerald-400',
    borderAccentClass: 'hover:border-emerald-500/40',
    subCategories: [
      { id: 'bitkiler', label: 'Tüm Bitkiler', icon: Sprout },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'kumlar',
    label: 'Taban & Kumlar',
    icon: Layers,
    activeTabClass:
      'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/60 shadow-sm shadow-amber-500/15',
    activeSubClass: 'bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-bold',
    badgeTextClass: 'text-amber-300',
    borderAccentClass: 'hover:border-amber-500/40',
    subCategories: [
      { id: 'kumlar', label: 'Taban Kumları', icon: Layers },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: 'malzemeler',
    label: 'Malzemeler',
    icon: Wrench,
    activeTabClass:
      'bg-violet-500/20 text-violet-300 border-violet-400/60 shadow-sm shadow-violet-500/15',
    activeSubClass: 'bg-violet-400 text-slate-950 font-bold',
    badgeTextClass: 'text-violet-300',
    borderAccentClass: 'hover:border-violet-500/40',
    subCategories: [
      { id: 'aydinlatmalar', label: 'Aydınlatma', icon: Sun },
      { id: 'filtreler', label: 'Filtreler', icon: InternalFilterIcon },
      { id: 'hava-motorlari', label: 'Hava Motorları', icon: AirPumpIcon },
      { id: 'hava-taslari', label: 'Hava Taşları', icon: AirStoneIcon },
    ],
  },
];

function ProductThumbnail({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  if (product.image_url) {
    return (
      <img
        src={product.image_url}
        alt={product.name}
        className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl object-contain p-1 border border-slate-700/70 shrink-0 bg-slate-950"
      />
    );
  }

  if (product.sub_category === 'aydinlatmalar' || product.slug.startsWith('led-')) {
    const isThreeColor = product.light_spectrum === 'uc-renk';
    const isBlueWhite = product.light_spectrum === 'beyaz-mavi';
    return (
      <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-950/80 to-slate-950 border border-violet-500/30 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-1.5 relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="w-12 h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-600 mb-1.5 shadow" />
        <div
          className="w-11 h-1.5 rounded-full blur-[0.5px]"
          style={{
            background: isThreeColor
              ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #f43f5e, #fef08a, #38bdf8)'
              : isBlueWhite
              ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, #f8fafc, #0284c7)'
              : product.kelvin && product.kelvin >= 10000
              ? '#bae6fd'
              : '#fef3c7',
          }}
        />
        <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-amber-300 mt-1.5">
          {product.length_cm}cm
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (product.category === 'canlilar') {
    const IconComp =
      product.sub_category === 'salyangozlar'
        ? Shell
        : product.sub_category === 'karidesler'
        ? Sparkles
        : Fish;
    return (
      <div
        className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl border border-cyan-500/30 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden"
        style={{
          background: `radial-gradient(circle at 30% 30%, ${product.color}44, #082f49 90%)`,
        }}
      >
        <IconComp className="w-7 h-7" style={{ color: product.color }} />
        <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-cyan-200/80 mt-0.5 capitalize">
          {product.variant && product.variant !== 'standart'
            ? product.variant
            : product.sub_category}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (product.category === 'bitkiler') {
    return (
      <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-950 to-slate-900 border border-emerald-500/30 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <Sprout className="w-7 h-7 text-emerald-400" />
        <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-emerald-300 mt-0.5">Bitki</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (product.category === 'kumlar') {
    return (
      <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-950/70 to-stone-900 border border-amber-500/30 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <Layers className="w-7 h-7 text-amber-300" />
        <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-amber-200/80 mt-0.5">Taban</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Hava Taşı Özel Thumbnail İkonu
  if (product.sub_category === 'hava-taslari') {
    const isLong =
      product.slug.includes('uzun') ||
      product.slug.includes('cubuk') ||
      (product.length_cm || 0) >= 8;
    const isDisc =
      product.slug.includes('disk') || product.slug.includes('difuzor');

    return (
      <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-sky-950/80 to-slate-900 border border-sky-500/35 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <AirStoneIcon className="w-7 h-7 text-sky-400" />
        <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-sky-300 mt-0.5">
          {isDisc ? 'Disk' : isLong ? `${product.length_cm || 15}cm` : 'Silindir'}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Filtreler Özel Thumbnail İkonu (Pipo Filtre vs Motorlu İç Filtre)
  if (product.sub_category === 'filtreler') {
    const isPipo = isSpongeOrAirFilter(product);
    const FilterIcon = isPipo ? SpongeFilterIcon : InternalFilterIcon;
    return (
      <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-950/80 to-slate-900 border border-indigo-500/35 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
        <FilterIcon className="w-7 h-7 text-indigo-400" />
        <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-indigo-300 mt-0.5">
          {isPipo ? 'Pipo' : 'İç Filtre'}
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Hava Motoru Özel Thumbnail İkonu
  return (
    <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-950/75 to-slate-900 border border-violet-500/35 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
      <AirPumpIcon className="w-7 h-7 text-violet-400" />
      <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-violet-300 mt-0.5">
        {product.outlet_count && product.outlet_count >= 2 ? '2 Çıkış' : '1 Çıkış'}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function CatalogDrawer({ onClose }: CatalogDrawerProps) {
  const {
    catalog,
    catalogSource,
    catalogError,
    loadCatalog,
    tankItems,
    selectedCategory,
    setSelectedCategory,
    addToTank,
    decrementFromTank,
    removeFromTank,
    ecosystemStatus,
  } = useAquariumStore();

  const activeTheme =
    CATEGORY_THEMES.find((t) => t.id === selectedCategory) || CATEGORY_THEMES[0];

  const [selectedSubCategory, setSelectedSubCategory] = useState<string>(
    activeTheme.subCategories[0].id
  );
  const [lightLengthFilter, setLightLengthFilter] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [lightColorFilter, setLightColorFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lightKelvinFilter, setLightKelvinFilter] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (catalog.length === 0) {
      loadCatalog();
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedSubCategory(activeTheme.subCategories[0].id);
  }, [activeTheme]);

  const filtered = catalog.filter((p) => {
    if (p.category !== activeTheme.id) return false;
    if (p.sub_category !== selectedSubCategory) return false;

    if (activeTheme.id === 'malzemeler' && p.sub_category === 'aydinlatmalar') {
      if (lightLengthFilter !== null && p.length_cm !== lightLengthFilter) return false;
      if (lightColorFilter !== null && p.light_spectrum !== lightColorFilter) return false;
      if (lightKelvinFilter !== null && p.kelvin !== lightKelvinFilter) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  const getItemQuantity = (productId: string) => {
    const found = tankItems.find((item) => item.product.id === productId);
    return found ? found.quantity : 0;
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-2xl border-r border-slate-700/80 p-4 shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-3">
      {/* Üst Başlık */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-14 pb-2 border-b border-slate-800">
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
          <activeTheme.icon className={`w-5 h-5 ${activeTheme.badgeTextClass}`} />
          <span>Ürün Kataloğu</span>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-normal text-slate-400">
            ({catalog.length})
          </span>
        </h3>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <button
            onClick={() => loadCatalog()}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-slate-400 hover:text-cyan-300 hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
            title="Supabase'den Yenile"
          >
            <RefreshCw
              className={`w-4 h-4 ${catalogSource === 'loading' ? 'animate-spin text-cyan-400' : ''}`}
            />
          </button>
          {onClose && (
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
              title="Kataloğu Kapat"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Supabase Bağlantı / RLS Hata Bildirimi */}
      {catalogError && (
        <div className="bg-rose-950/70 border border-rose-500/40 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-[11px] text-rose-200 flex items-start gap-2">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="break-words">{catalogError}</div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 1. Ana Kategori Sekmeleri */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
        {CATEGORY_THEMES.map((cat) => {
          const isActive = activeTheme.id === cat.id;
          const IconComp = cat.icon;
          return (
            <button
              key={cat.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat.id)}
              className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
                isActive
                  ? cat.activeTabClass
                  : 'bg-slate-800/55 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 border-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-800'
              }`}
            >
              <IconComp className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{cat.label}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* 2. Aktif Kategorinin Alt Kategorileri */}
      {activeTheme.subCategories.length > 1 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 bg-slate-950/60 p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/80">
          {activeTheme.subCategories.map((sub) => {
            const isSubActive = selectedSubCategory === sub.id;
            const SubIcon = sub.icon;
            return (
              <button
                key={sub.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedSubCategory(sub.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] transition ${
                  isSubActive
                    ? activeTheme.activeSubClass
                    : 'bg-slate-800/70 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <SubIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span>{sub.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 3. Malzemeler -> Aydınlatma seçiliyken LED Boy / Renk / Kelvin Filtresi */}
      {activeTheme.id === 'malzemeler' && selectedSubCategory === 'aydinlatmalar' && (
        <div className="bg-violet-950/25 border border-violet-500/25 rounded-xl p-2.5 space-y-2">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-violet-300 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Sun className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" /> LED Armatür Filtresi (Boy • Renk • Kelvin)
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
            {[30, 40, 50, 60, 100, 200].map((len) => (
              <button
                key={len}
                onClick={() =>
                  setLightLengthFilter((prev) => (prev === len ? null : len))
                }
                className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-medium transition ${
                  lightLengthFilter === len
                    ? 'bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-bold'
                    : 'bg-slate-900/80 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                {len}cm
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-0.5">
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {[
                { id: 'beyaz', label: 'Beyaz' },
                { id: 'beyaz-mavi', label: 'B-Mavi' },
                { id: 'uc-renk', label: '3 Renk' },
              ].map((sp) => (
                <button
                  key={sp.id}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setLightColorFilter((prev) => (prev === sp.id ? null : sp.id))
                  }
                  className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition ${
                    lightColorFilter === sp.id
                      ? 'bg-violet-400 text-slate-950 font-bold'
                      : 'bg-slate-900/80 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {sp.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {[6500, 10000].map((kv) => (
                <button
                  key={kv}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setLightKelvinFilter((prev) => (prev === kv ? null : kv))
                  }
                  className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] transition ${
                    lightKelvinFilter === kv
                      ? 'bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 font-bold'
                      : 'bg-slate-900/80 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
                  }`}
                >
                  {kv / 1000}K
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Bitki Sekmesinde Kapasite Bilgisi */}
      {activeTheme.id === 'bitkiler' && (
        <div className="text-[11px] bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/25 text-emerald-300 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl flex justify-between">
          <span>Tank Bitki Kapasitesi:</span>
          <span className="font-bold">
            {ecosystemStatus.currentPlants} / {ecosystemStatus.maxPlants} adet
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 4. Resimli Ürün Listesi */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto pr-1 space-y-2.5 min-h-0">
        {catalogSource === 'loading' ? (
          <div className="text-center py-10 text-xs text-cyan-400">
            Ürünler Supabase&apos;den yükleniyor...
          </div>
        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-10 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Bu alt kategoride henüz ürün bulunmuyor.
          </div>
        ) : (
          filtered.map((product) => {
            const qty = getItemQuantity(product.id);

            return (
              <div
                key={product.id}
                className={`bg-slate-800/55 hover:bg-slate-800/85 border border-slate-700/60 ${activeTheme.borderAccentClass} rounded-2xl p-2.5 flex items-center gap-3 transition`}
              >
                <ProductThumbnail product={product} />

                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100 truncate">
                    {product.name}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 line-clamp-2 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
                    {product.description}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1.5 flex-wrap">
                    <span className={`text-xs font-bold ${activeTheme.badgeTextClass}`}>
                      {product.price.toFixed(2)} ₺
                    </span>
                    {product.lumen > 0 && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-amber-300">
                        • {product.lumen} lm ({product.kelvin || 6500}K)
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {product.watt !== undefined && product.watt > 0 && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-violet-300">
                        • {product.watt}W
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {product.min_liters > 0 && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                        • Min {product.min_liters}L
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {qty === 0 ? (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => addToTank(product)}
                    className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/20"
                    title="Akvaryuma Ekle"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1 shrink-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-0.5">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => decrementFromTank(product.id)}
                        className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-500/30 text-slate-200 hover:text-rose-200 flex items-center justify-center transition"
                        title={qty === 1 ? 'Tanktan Kaldır' : 'Adet Azalt'}
                      >
                        {qty === 1 ? (
                          <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3 text-rose-400" />
                        ) : (
                          <Minus className="w-3 h-3" />
                        )}
                      </button>
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold text-cyan-300 w-5 text-center">
                        {qty}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => addToTank(product)}
                        className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center transition"
                        title="Adet Artır"
                      >
                        <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => removeFromTank(product.id)}
                      className="text-[10px] text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300 transition pr-0.5"
                    >
                      Kaldır
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: components\ui\SearchBar.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Search, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAquariumStore } from '../../store/useAquariumStore';

export default function SearchBar() {
  const { catalog, searchQuery, setSearchQuery, addToTank } = useAquariumStore();

  const filtered = catalog.filter(p =>
    p.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
    p.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="relative w-full">
      {/* Yukarı Doğru Açılan Sonuç Listesi */}
      {searchQuery.trim().length > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute bottom-full mb-2 left-0 right-0 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-700 rounded-2xl p-2 shadow-2xl z-30 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto space-y-1">
          {filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-3 text-xs text-slate-400 text-center">Ürün bulunamadı.</div>
          ) : (
            filtered.map(product => (
              <div
                key={product.id}
                className="flex items-center justify-between p-2.5 hover:bg-slate-800/80 rounded-xl transition border border-transparent hover:border-slate-700"
              >
                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">{product.name}</div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{product.price.toFixed(2)} ₺ • Min {product.min_liters}L</div>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => {
                    addToTank(product);
                    setSearchQuery('');
                  }}
                  className="w-7 h-7 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center transition shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/20"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Arama Çubuğu */}
      <div className="relative flex items-center">
        <Search className="absolute left-4 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
        <input
          type="text"
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Katalogda ara (örn. Lepistes, Anubias)..."
          className="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-900/85 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-700/80 focus:border-cyan-400 rounded-full text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-400 shadow-2xl focus:outline-none transition"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



========================================
DOSYA: components\ui\ShoppingCartPanel.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ShoppingCart, Trash2, Plus, Minus, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAquariumStore } from '../../store/useAquariumStore';

interface ShoppingCartPanelProps {
  onClose?: () => void;
}

export default function ShoppingCartPanel({ onClose }: ShoppingCartPanelProps) {
  const { tankItems, updateQuantity, removeFromTank } = useAquariumStore();

  const totalPrice = tankItems.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.product.price * item.quantity, 0);
  const totalItemsCount = tankItems.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.quantity, 0);

  return (
    <div className="w-80 max-h-[72vh] bg-slate-900/85 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-700/70 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xl flex flex-col justify-between min-h-0">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2.5 border-b border-slate-700/60">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShoppingCart className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400" /> Alışveriş Listesi
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[11px] bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">
            {totalItemsCount}
          </span>
        </div>
        {onClose && (
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {tankItems.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center py-8 text-slate-500 text-xs">
          <ShoppingCart className="w-7 h-7 mb-1.5 opacity-40" />
          Tankınızda henüz ürün yok.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 my-3 pr-1 min-h-0">
          {tankItems.map(item => (
            <div
              key={item.product.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-800/50 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/40"
            >
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-2">
                <div className="text-xs font-medium text-white truncate">{item.product.name}</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-cyan-400 font-medium">{(item.product.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)} ₺</div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <button
                  onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.product.id, item.quantity - 1)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center transition"
                >
                  <Minus className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white w-4 text-center">{item.quantity}</span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.product.id, item.quantity + 1)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-300 flex items-center justify-center transition"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => removeFromTank(item.product.id)}
                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded bg-rose-500/15 hover:bg-rose-500/30 text-rose-300 flex items-center justify-center transition ml-1"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Alt Toplam ve Onay */}
      <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-700/70 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Tahmini Toplam</div>
          <div className="text-base font-bold text-emerald-400">{totalPrice.toFixed(2)} ₺</div>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => alert('Sipariş başarıyla oluşturuldu! Akvaryum ekosisteminiz hazırlanıyor.')}
          disabled={tankItems.length === 0}
          className="bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-slate-950 font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs transition shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/20"
        >
          Sepeti Onayla
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Activity,
  Thermometer,
  Droplet,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Waves,
  Sprout,
  Wind,
  FlaskConical,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAquariumStore } from '../../store/useAquariumStore';
import { getTankDimensionsCm } from '../../lib/simulation/ecosystemEngine';

export default function SideStatusPanel() {
  const { tankCapacity, setTankCapacity, ecosystemStatus } = useAquariumStore();
  const [errorsExpanded, setErrorsExpanded] = useState(false);

  const dims = getTankDimensionsCm(tankCapacity);
  const { waterVisuals } = ecosystemStatus;
  const warningCount = ecosystemStatus.warnings.length;

  const getBadgeTone = (isBad: boolean, isWarn: boolean) =>
    isBad
      ? 'text-rose-400 font-bold'
      : isWarn
      ? 'text-amber-300 font-semibold'
      : 'text-emerald-300 font-semibold';

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900/85 backdrop-blur-xl border border-slate-700/70 rounded-2xl p-3.5 shadow-2xl space-y-2.5 max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto">
      {/* 1. Tank Hacmi Seçici */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" /> Su Kimyası
        </span>
        <div className="bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700/60 rounded-xl p-0.5 flex items-center gap-0.5">
          {[50, 100, 200].map((vol) => (
            <button
              key={vol}
              onClick={() => setTankCapacity(vol)}
              className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold transition ${
                tankCapacity === vol
                  ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-700/70'
              }`}
            >
              {vol}L
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2. Fiziksel Ölçüler & Bitki Kapasitesi */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 bg-slate-800/50 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/40">
        <span>
          {dims.widthCm}×{dims.depthCm}×{dims.heightCm} cm
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-400 font-medium">
          <Sprout className="w-3 h-3" /> {ecosystemStatus.currentPlants}/{ecosystemStatus.maxPlants} Bitki
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* 3. Dinamik Su Rengi & Alg Yoğunluğu */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/60 rounded-xl p-2 border border-slate-700/50 space-y-1">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Waves className="w-3 h-3 text-cyan-400" /> Su Rengi:
          </span>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-100 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span
              className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full border border-white/30 shadow-sm"
              style={{ backgroundColor: waterVisuals.color }}
            />
            {waterVisuals.label}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-700/40">
          <span>Alg Yoğunluğu:</span>
          <span
            className={getBadgeTone(
              ecosystemStatus.algaeLevel >= 50,
              ecosystemStatus.algaeLevel >= 25
            )}
          >
            %{ecosystemStatus.algaeLevel}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4. Azot Döngüsü (NH3 Amonyak / NO2 Nitrit / NO3 Nitrat) */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/50 rounded-xl p-2 border border-slate-700/40 space-y-1">
        <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1">
          <FlaskConical className="w-3 h-3 text-purple-400" /> Azot Döngüsü (mg/L)
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 text-center pt-0.5">
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 rounded-lg py-1 px-1">
            <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">NH₃ Amonyak</div>
            <div
              className={`text-[11px] ${getBadgeTone(
                ecosystemStatus.ammonia >= 0.5,
                ecosystemStatus.ammonia >= 0.2
              )}`}
            >
              {ecosystemStatus.ammonia}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 rounded-lg py-1 px-1">
            <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">NO₂ Nitrit</div>
            <div
              className={`text-[11px] ${getBadgeTone(
                ecosystemStatus.nitrite >= 0.8,
                ecosystemStatus.nitrite >= 0.3
              )}`}
            >
              {ecosystemStatus.nitrite}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 rounded-lg py-1 px-1">
            <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">NO₃ Nitrat</div>
            <div
              className={`text-[11px] ${getBadgeTone(
                ecosystemStatus.nitrate >= 45,
                ecosystemStatus.nitrate >= 25
              )}`}
            >
              {ecosystemStatus.nitrate}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 5. Çözünmüş Gazlar (O2 & CO2) */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/50 rounded-xl p-2 border border-slate-700/40 space-y-1">
        <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1">
          <Wind className="w-3 h-3 text-sky-400" /> Gaz Dengesi
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 text-center">
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 rounded-lg py-1 px-1">
            <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">O₂ (Oksijen)</div>
            <div
              className={`text-[11px] ${getBadgeTone(
                ecosystemStatus.oxygen <= 4.5,
                ecosystemStatus.oxygen <= 6.0
              )}`}
            >
              {ecosystemStatus.oxygen} mg/L
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 rounded-lg py-1 px-1">
            <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">CO₂ (Karbondioksit)</div>
            <div
              className={`text-[11px] ${getBadgeTone(
                ecosystemStatus.co2 >= 32,
                ecosystemStatus.co2 >= 24
              )}`}
            >
              {ecosystemStatus.co2} ppm
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 6. Mineraller (PPM, GH/KH), pH ve Sıcaklık */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 text-center">
        <div className="bg-slate-800/60 rounded-xl p-1.5 border border-slate-700/40">
          <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">TDS / Mineral</div>
          <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-cyan-300 mt-0.5">{ecosystemStatus.ppm} ppm</div>
          <div className="text-[8px] text-slate-500">
            GH {ecosystemStatus.gh} / KH {ecosystemStatus.kh}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-slate-800/60 rounded-xl p-1.5 border border-slate-700/40">
          <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5">
            <Droplet className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-cyan-400" /> pH
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-white mt-0.5">{ecosystemStatus.ph}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-slate-800/60 rounded-xl p-1.5 border border-slate-700/40">
          <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center gap-0.5">
            <Thermometer className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-orange-400" /> Isı
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-white mt-0.5">
            {ecosystemStatus.temperature}°C
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 7. Gizli "Hatalar" Çekmecesi (Tıklandığında detayları açılır) */}
      {warningCount === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 p-2 rounded-xl text-emerald-300 text-[11px]">
          <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-400" />
          <span>Ekosistem dengede.</span>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/30 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
          <button
            onClick={() => setErrorsExpanded((prev) => !prev)}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-500/15 transition"
          >
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
              <span>Hatalar ({warningCount})</span>
            </span>
            {errorsExpanded ? (
              <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
            )}
          </button>

          {errorsExpanded && (
            <div className="px-2.5 pb-2 pt-1 space-y-1.5 border-t border-rose-500/20 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">
              {ecosystemStatus.warnings.map((warning, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className="text-[10px] text-rose-200 leading-snug bg-rose-950/50 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-rose-500/20"
                >
                  • {warning}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: lib\simulation\ecosystemEngine.ts
========================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { TankItem, CompatibilityRule } from '../supabase/catalogService';

export interface WaterVisualState {
  phase: 'clean' | 'moderate' | 'algae' | 'tannin' | 'critical';
  color: string;
  opacity: number;
  roughness: number;
  label: string;
  isLethal: boolean;
}

export interface EcosystemStatus {
  ph: number;
  temperature: number;
  bioLoad: number;

  // Azot Döngüsü (mg/L)
  ammonia: number;
  nitrite: number;
  nitrate: number;

  // Gaz Dengesi
  oxygen: number;
  co2: number;

  // Mineraller & Sertlik
  ppm: number;
  gh: number;
  kh: number;

  // Aydınlatma & Alg Fotokimyası
  totalLumens: number;
  lightHours: number;
  lumenPerLiter: number;
  algaeLevel: number;

  // Görsel Su Rengi ve Uyarılar
  waterVisuals: WaterVisualState;
  warnings: string[];
  positiveNotes: string[];
  isHealthy: boolean;
  maxPlants: number;
  currentPlants: number;
}

export interface TankDimensionsCm {
  widthCm: number;
  depthCm: number;
  heightCm: number;
  sceneWidth: number;
  sceneHeight: number;
  sceneDepth: number;
}

export function getTankDimensionsCm(tankCapacity: number): TankDimensionsCm {
  if (tankCapacity === 200) {
    return {
      widthCm: 100,
      depthCm: 40,
      heightCm: 50,
      sceneWidth: 10.0,
      sceneHeight: 5.0,
      sceneDepth: 4.0,
    };
  }
  if (tankCapacity === 100) {
    return {
      widthCm: 80,
      depthCm: 35,
      heightCm: 40,
      sceneWidth: 8.0,
      sceneHeight: 4.0,
      sceneDepth: 3.5,
    };
  }
  return {
    widthCm: 50,
    depthCm: 30,
    heightCm: 35,
    sceneWidth: 5.0,
    sceneHeight: 3.5,
    sceneDepth: 3.0,
  };
}

export function getMaxPlantsForTank(tankCapacity: number): number {
  const { widthCm, depthCm } = getTankDimensionsCm(tankCapacity);
  return Math.max(4, Math.floor((widthCm * depthCm) / 250));
}

export interface TankZones {
  maxSandKg: number;
  totalSandKg: number;
  sandVolumeLt: number;
  sandHeightRatio: number;
  sandHeight: number;
  sandTopY: number;
  usableWaterHeight: number;
  zones: {
    dip: [number, number];
    orta: [number, number];
    ust: [number, number];
  };
}

export function calculateTankZones(
  tankVolume: number,
  totalSandKg: number,
  tankBottomY: number,
  waterSurfaceY: number
): TankZones {
  const maxSandKg = Math.floor((tankVolume * 0.25) / 0.65);
  const clampedSandKg = Math.max(0, totalSandKg);
  const sandVolumeLt = clampedSandKg * 0.65;
  const sandHeightRatio = tankVolume > 0 ? Math.min(sandVolumeLt / tankVolume, 0.30) : 0;
  const totalWaterHeight = waterSurfaceY - tankBottomY;
  const sandHeight = totalWaterHeight * sandHeightRatio;
  const sandTopY = tankBottomY + sandHeight;
  const usableWaterHeight = Math.max(0.1, waterSurfaceY - sandTopY);

  return {
    maxSandKg,
    totalSandKg: clampedSandKg,
    sandVolumeLt,
    sandHeightRatio,
    sandHeight,
    sandTopY,
    usableWaterHeight,
    zones: {
      dip: [sandTopY + 0.08, sandTopY + usableWaterHeight * 0.33],
      orta: [sandTopY + usableWaterHeight * 0.33, sandTopY + usableWaterHeight * 0.66],
      ust: [sandTopY + usableWaterHeight * 0.66, waterSurfaceY - 0.08],
    },
  };
}

function computeBlendedWaterVisuals(params: {
  ammonia: number;
  nitrite: number;
  nitrate: number;
  oxygen: number;
  algaeLevel: number;
  tanninScore: number;
  warnings: string[];
}): WaterVisualState {
  const { ammonia, nitrite, oxygen, algaeLevel, tanninScore } = params;

  const toxicityFactor = Math.min(
    1,
    Math.max(
      ammonia / 1.2,
      nitrite / 2.0,
      oxygen < 4.2 ? (4.2 - oxygen) / 2.5 : 0
    )
  );

  const algaeFactor = Math.min(1, algaeLevel / 100);
  const tanninFactor = Math.min(1, tanninScore / 10);

  const cleanColor = new THREE.Color('#0284c7');
  const algaeColor = new THREE.Color('#16a34a');
  const toxicColor = new THREE.Color('#dc2626');
  const tanninColor = new THREE.Color('#b45309');

  const finalColor = cleanColor.clone();
  if (tanninFactor > 0.05) {
    finalColor.lerp(tanninColor, tanninFactor * 0.65);
  }
  if (algaeFactor > 0.05) {
    finalColor.lerp(algaeColor, algaeFactor * 0.85);
  }
  if (toxicityFactor > 0.05) {
    finalColor.lerp(toxicColor, toxicityFactor * 0.85);
  }

  const hexColor = `#${finalColor.getHexString()}`;
  const isLethal = ammonia >= 1.0 || nitrite >= 2.0 || oxygen <= 3.2;

  const turbidity = Math.max(toxicityFactor, algaeFactor * 0.85, tanninFactor * 0.4);
  const opacity = Number(Math.min(0.42, 0.13 + turbidity * 0.24).toFixed(3));
  const roughness = Number(Math.min(0.45, 0.10 + turbidity * 0.28).toFixed(2));

  if (isLethal) {
    return {
      phase: 'critical',
      color: hexColor,
      opacity,
      roughness,
      label: 'Kritik Toksisite / Hipoksi',
      isLethal: true,
    };
  }
  if (algaeFactor >= 0.45) {
    return {
      phase: 'algae',
      color: hexColor,
      opacity,
      roughness,
      label: `Alg Patlaması (%${Math.round(algaeLevel)} Yeşil Su)`,
      isLethal: false,
    };
  }
  if (toxicityFactor >= 0.35) {
    return {
      phase: 'moderate',
      color: hexColor,
      opacity,
      roughness,
      label: 'Azot / Oksijen Dengesizliği',
      isLethal: false,
    };
  }
  if (tanninFactor >= 0.35) {
    return {
      phase: 'tannin',
      color: hexColor,
      opacity,
      roughness,
      label: 'Doğal Tanenli (Amber Su)',
      isLethal: false,
    };
  }

  return {
    phase: 'clean',
    color: hexColor,
    opacity,
    roughness,
    label: 'Berrak & Dengeli',
    isLethal: false,
  };
}

export function evaluateEcosystem(
  tankCapacity: number,
  items: TankItem[],
  rules: CompatibilityRule[] = [],
  lightHours: number = 8
): EcosystemStatus {
  const warnings: string[] = [];
  const positiveNotes: string[] = [];

  const volumeScale = 50 / Math.max(10, tankCapacity);

  let totalBioLoad = 0;
  let minLitersRequired = 0;

  let rawAmmonia = 0;
  let rawNitrite = 0;
  let rawNitrate = 5.0;
  let rawO2 = 7.8;
  let rawCo2 = 6.0;
  let ppm = 160;
  let gh = 6.0;
  let kh = 4.5;
  let ph = 7.2;
  let tanninScore = 0;
  let algaeControlPower = 0;
  let extraLumens = 0;

  const sandItems = items.filter((i) => i.product.category === 'kumlar');
  const totalSandKg = sandItems.reduce((acc, i) => acc + i.quantity, 0);
  const unitVol = sandItems[0]?.product.volume_per_unit_lt || 0.65;
  const maxSandKg = Math.floor((tankCapacity * 0.25) / unitVol);

  if (totalSandKg > maxSandKg) {
    warnings.push(`Maksimum kum sınırı aşıldı! (${tankCapacity}L için max ${maxSandKg} kg)`);
  }

  const plantItems = items.filter((i) => i.product.category === 'bitkiler' && i.quantity > 0);
  const currentPlants = plantItems.reduce((acc, i) => acc + i.quantity, 0);
  const maxPlants = getMaxPlantsForTank(tankCapacity);

  if (currentPlants > 0 && totalSandKg <= 0) {
    warnings.push('Bitki eklemek için önce akvaryuma taban kumu eklemelisiniz!');
  }
  if (currentPlants > maxPlants) {
    warnings.push(`Maksimum bitki sınırı aşıldı! (${tankCapacity}L tabanı için max ${maxPlants} adet)`);
  }

  // Hava motoru ve hava gerektiren ekipman (pipo filtre / hava taşı) kontrolü
  const totalAirOutlets = items
    .filter((i) => i.quantity > 0 && i.product.sub_category === 'hava-motorlari')
    .reduce((acc, i) => acc + (i.product.outlet_count || 1) * i.quantity, 0);

  const airDrivenEquipmentCount = items
    .filter((i) => i.quantity > 0 && i.product.needs_air_pump === true)
    .reduce((acc, i) => acc + i.quantity, 0);

  if (airDrivenEquipmentCount > 0 && totalAirOutlets === 0) {
    warnings.push(
      'Pipo filtre veya hava taşının çalışması için dışarıya en az 1 adet Hava Motoru eklemelisiniz!'
    );
  } else if (airDrivenEquipmentCount > totalAirOutlets) {
    warnings.push(
      `Hava motoru çıkışı yetersiz! (${airDrivenEquipmentCount} ekipman için ${totalAirOutlets} hava çıkışı mevcut)`
    );
  }

  // Karides + karides güvenli olmayan iç filtre kontrolü
  const hasShrimp = items.some(
    (i) => i.quantity > 0 && i.product.sub_category === 'karidesler'
  );
  const hasUnsafeFilter = items.some(
    (i) =>
      i.quantity > 0 &&
      i.product.sub_category === 'filtreler' &&
      i.product.shrimp_safe === false
  );
  if (hasShrimp && hasUnsafeFilter) {
    warnings.push(
      'Dikkat: Motorlu iç filtre yavru karidesleri çekebilir! Karides tankları için Pipo Filtre önerilir.'
    );
  }

  // Tüm ürünlerin kimyasal/fiziksel etkilerini topla
  items.forEach((item) => {
    const p = item.product;
    const qty = item.quantity;
    if (qty <= 0) return;

    // Hava motoru gerektiren ürün (pipo/hava taşı) hava motoru yoksa aktif O2 vermez
    const isAirStarved = p.needs_air_pump === true && totalAirOutlets === 0;
    const activeFactor = isAirStarved ? 0.15 : 1.0;

    totalBioLoad += p.bio_load * qty;
    if (p.min_liters > 0) {
      minLitersRequired = Math.max(minLitersRequired, p.min_liters);
    }

    rawAmmonia += p.ammonia_rate * qty * volumeScale;
    rawNitrite += p.nitrite_rate * qty * volumeScale;
    rawNitrate += p.nitrate_rate * qty * volumeScale;
    rawO2 += p.o2_rate * qty * volumeScale * activeFactor;
    rawCo2 += p.co2_rate * qty * volumeScale * activeFactor;

    ppm += p.ppm_impact * qty * volumeScale;
    gh += p.gh_impact * qty * volumeScale;
    kh += p.kh_impact * qty * volumeScale;
    ph += p.ph_impact * qty * volumeScale;
    tanninScore += p.tannin_rate * qty * volumeScale;
    algaeControlPower += p.algae_control_rate * qty * volumeScale;
    extraLumens += p.lumen * qty;
  });

  if (minLitersRequired > tankCapacity) {
    warnings.push(
      `Seçilen tank hacmi (${tankCapacity}L) yetersiz! Önerilen min: ${minLitersRequired}L`
    );
  }

  // Azot Döngüsü (Nitrifikasyon): SQL'deki 'filtration_power' değerine göre dönüşüm
  const activeFilters = items.filter(
    (i) => i.quantity > 0 && i.product.sub_category === 'filtreler'
  );
  if (activeFilters.length > 0) {
    const totalFiltrationPower = activeFilters.reduce((acc, f) => {
      const airModifier =
        f.product.needs_air_pump && totalAirOutlets === 0 ? 0.1 : 1.0;
      const power = f.product.filtration_power ?? 0.7;
      return acc + power * f.quantity * airModifier;
    }, 0);

    const conversionEfficiency = Math.min(0.92, totalFiltrationPower * 0.85);
    const convertedAmmonia = Math.max(0, rawAmmonia * conversionEfficiency);
    rawAmmonia = Math.max(0, rawAmmonia - convertedAmmonia);
    rawNitrite = Math.max(0, rawNitrite - convertedAmmonia * 0.72);
    rawNitrate += convertedAmmonia * 2.1;
  } else if (totalBioLoad > 3) {
    warnings.push('Filtrasyon yok! Amonyak ve Nitrit hızla birikiyor.');
  }

  // Işık (Lümen × Saat) ve Bitki Fotosentez / Alg Dengesi
  const baseTankLumens = tankCapacity === 200 ? 2200 : tankCapacity === 100 ? 1200 : 650;
  const totalLumens = baseTankLumens + extraLumens;
  const lumenPerLiter = totalLumens / Math.max(10, tankCapacity);
  const dailyLightEnergy = lumenPerLiter * lightHours;

  const plantLightCapacity = (currentPlants / Math.max(1, maxPlants)) * 180;
  const excessLightEnergy = Math.max(0, dailyLightEnergy - (95 + plantLightCapacity));

  if (currentPlants > 0 && lightHours >= 6) {
    const photosynthesisBoost = Math.min(1.5, lightHours / 8) * (currentPlants / maxPlants);
    rawO2 += photosynthesisBoost * 1.4;
    rawCo2 = Math.max(3, rawCo2 - photosynthesisBoost * 3.5);
    rawNitrate = Math.max(0, rawNitrate - photosynthesisBoost * 8.0);
  }

  const nutrientFuel = Math.max(0, rawNitrate - 10) * 1.1 + Math.max(0, rawAmmonia) * 15;
  const rawAlgae =
    excessLightEnergy * 0.48 +
    nutrientFuel * (lightHours / 10) -
    algaeControlPower * 12;
  const algaeLevel = Number(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, rawAlgae)).toFixed(1));

  const co2PhShift = -Math.max(0, rawCo2 - 10) * 0.015;
  const finalPh = Number(Math.min(8.6, Math.max(5.4, ph + co2PhShift)).toFixed(2));
  const finalAmmonia = Number(Math.max(0, rawAmmonia).toFixed(2));
  const finalNitrite = Number(Math.max(0, rawNitrite).toFixed(2));
  const finalNitrate = Number(Math.max(0, rawNitrate).toFixed(1));
  const finalO2 = Number(Math.min(12.0, Math.max(1.5, rawO2)).toFixed(1));
  const finalCo2 = Number(Math.min(60.0, Math.max(2.0, rawCo2)).toFixed(1));
  const finalPpm = Math.round(Math.max(40, ppm));
  const finalGh = Number(Math.max(1, gh).toFixed(1));
  const finalKh = Number(Math.max(1, kh).toFixed(1));

  if (finalAmmonia >= 0.5) {
    warnings.push(`Yüksek Amonyak (${finalAmmonia} mg/L)! Su değişimi veya filtre gerekli.`);
  }
  if (finalNitrite >= 0.8) {
    warnings.push(`Kritik Nitrit (${finalNitrite} mg/L)! Balık solungaçları zarar görüyor.`);
  }
  if (finalNitrate >= 45) {
    warnings.push(`Yüksek Nitrat (${finalNitrate} mg/L)! Bitki ekleyin veya su değişimi yapın.`);
  }
  if (finalO2 <= 4.5) {
    warnings.push(`Düşük Oksijen (${finalO2} mg/L)! Hava/filtre akıntısını artırın.`);
  }
  if (finalCo2 >= 32) {
    warnings.push(`Yüksek CO2 (${finalCo2} ppm)! Balıklarda boğulma riski.`);
  }
  if (algaeLevel >= 50) {
    warnings.push(
      `Alg Patlaması (%${Math.round(algaeLevel)})! Işık süresini (${lightHours}s) azaltın veya alg yiyici ekleyin.`
    );
  }

  // Supabase compatibility_rules kontrolü (Hem tam slug hem de species bazlı varyant eşleşmesi)
  const activeSlugs = new Set(items.filter((i) => i.quantity > 0).map((i) => i.product.slug));
  const activeSpecies = new Set(
    items
      .filter((i) => i.quantity > 0 && i.product.species)
      .map((i) => i.product.species!)
  );

  const matchesRuleKey = (ruleSlug: string) => {
    if (activeSlugs.has(ruleSlug)) return true;
    // Örn: rule 'beta-erkek' iken 'beta-super-red-crowntail' (species: 'beta', variant: 'crowntail') için de uyar
    if (ruleSlug === 'beta-erkek') {
      return items.some(
        (i) =>
          i.quantity > 0 &&
          i.product.species === 'beta' &&
          i.product.variant !== 'disi' &&
          i.product.variant !== 'yavru'
      );
    }
    if (ruleSlug === 'lepistes-erkek') {
      return items.some(
        (i) =>
          i.quantity > 0 &&
          i.product.species === 'lepistes' &&
          i.product.variant !== 'disi' &&
          i.product.variant !== 'yavru'
      );
    }
    return activeSpecies.has(ruleSlug);
  };

  rules.forEach((rule) => {
    if (rule.item_a_slug === rule.item_b_slug) {
      // Örn: İki erkek Beta aynı tankta kuralı
      const matchingCount = items
        .filter((i) => i.quantity > 0)
        .reduce((sum, i) => {
          if (i.product.slug === rule.item_a_slug) return sum + i.quantity;
          if (
            rule.item_a_slug === 'beta-erkek' &&
            i.product.species === 'beta' &&
            i.product.variant !== 'disi' &&
            i.product.variant !== 'yavru'
          ) {
            return sum + i.quantity;
          }
          return sum;
        }, 0);

      if (matchingCount >= 2 && rule.status === 'uyumsuz') {
        if (!warnings.includes(rule.reason)) warnings.push(rule.reason);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (matchesRuleKey(rule.item_a_slug) && matchesRuleKey(rule.item_b_slug)) {
      if (rule.status === 'uyumsuz') {
        if (!warnings.includes(rule.reason)) warnings.push(rule.reason);
      } else if (rule.status === 'uyumlu') {
        if (!positiveNotes.includes(rule.reason)) positiveNotes.push(rule.reason);
      }
    }
  });

  const waterVisuals = computeBlendedWaterVisuals({
    ammonia: finalAmmonia,
    nitrite: finalNitrite,
    nitrate: finalNitrate,
    oxygen: finalO2,
    algaeLevel,
    tanninScore,
    warnings,
  });

  return {
    ph: finalPh,
    temperature: 25.0,
    bioLoad: Number(totalBioLoad.toFixed(1)),
    ammonia: finalAmmonia,
    nitrite: finalNitrite,
    nitrate: finalNitrate,
    oxygen: finalO2,
    co2: finalCo2,
    ppm: finalPpm,
    gh: finalGh,
    kh: finalKh,
    totalLumens: Math.round(totalLumens),
    lightHours,
    lumenPerLiter: Number(lumenPerLiter.toFixed(1)),
    algaeLevel,
    waterVisuals,
    warnings,
    positiveNotes,
    isHealthy: warnings.length === 0,
    maxPlants,
    currentPlants,
  };
}

========================================
DOSYA: lib\supabase\catalogService.ts
