export interface Product {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  species?: string;
  variant?: 'erkek' | 'disi' | 'yavru' | 'standart' | string;
  name: string;
  category: 'canlilar' | 'bitkiler' | 'kumlar' | 'malzemeler';
  sub_category: string;
  price: number;
  min_liters: number;
  ideal_temp_min: number;
  ideal_temp_max: number;
  ideal_ph_min: number;
  ideal_ph_max: number;
  bio_load: number;
  volume_per_unit_lt?: number;
  temperament?: string;
  description: string;
  color: string;
  fin_color?: string;
  image_url?: string;
  swim_zone?: 'dip' | 'orta' | 'ust';
  length_cm: number;

  // Biyokimya, Mineral, Gaz ve Işık Parametreleri
  ammonia_rate: number;
  nitrite_rate: number;
  nitrate_rate: number;
  o2_rate: number;
  co2_rate: number;
  ppm_impact: number;
  gh_impact: number;
  kh_impact: number;
  ph_impact: number;
  tannin_rate: number;
  algae_control_rate: number;
  lumen: number;
  kelvin?: number;
  light_spectrum?: 'beyaz' | 'beyaz-mavi' | 'uc-renk' | string;

  // Ekipman & Teknik Parametreler
  watt?: number;
  outlet_count?: number;
  flow_rate_lph?: number;
  filtration_power?: number;
  shrimp_safe?: boolean;
  needs_air_pump?: boolean;
  placement_zone?: 'outside_left' | 'substrate' | 'glass_wall' | 'top_rim' | string;
}

export interface CatalogCategory {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  parent_slug: string | null;
  target_table: string | null;
}

export interface CompatibilityRule {
  id: string;
  item_a_slug: string;
  item_b_slug: string;
  status: 'uyumlu' | 'uyumsuz' | string;
  reason: string;
}

export interface TankItem {
  product: Product;
  quantity: number;
}

export interface CatalogResponse {
  products: Product[];
  categories: CatalogCategory[];
  rules: CompatibilityRule[];
  source: 'supabase' | 'error';
  errorMessage?: string;
}

function getDefaultColorBySlug(slug: string, category: Product['category']): string {
  if (slug.includes('golden') || slug.includes('yellow')) return '#facc15';
  if (slug.includes('moscow-blue')) return '#1d4ed8';
  if (slug.includes('blue-neon') || slug.includes('turquoise')) return '#38bdf8';
  if (slug.includes('full-red') || slug.includes('super-red')) return '#ef4444';
  if (slug.includes('mustard-gas')) return '#0284c7';
  if (slug.includes('platinum') || slug.includes('white')) return '#f8fafc';
  if (slug === 'lepistes-erkek' || slug === 'lepistes') return '#ff6b6b';
  if (slug === 'lepistes-disi') return '#f59e0b';
  if (slug === 'lepistes-yavru') return '#fda4af';
  if (slug === 'beta-erkek' || slug === 'beta') return '#2563eb';
  if (slug === 'beta-disi') return '#ec4899';
  if (slug === 'beta-yavru') return '#a78bfa';
  if (slug === 'anubias') return '#2b8a3e';
  if (slug === 'silis-kum') return '#e2e8f0';
  if (category === 'canlilar') return '#38bdf8';
  if (category === 'bitkiler') return '#22c55e';
  if (category === 'kumlar') return '#f59e0b';
  return '#a855f7';
}

function buildAutoDescription(row: any, category: Product['category'], subCategory: string): string {
  if (row.description && String(row.description).trim().length > 0) {
    return String(row.description);
  }
  if (subCategory === 'aydinlatmalar') {
    return `${row.length_cm || 50} cm • ${row.kelvin || 6500}K • ${row.lumen || 0} Lümen LED akvaryum armatürü.`;
  }
  if (subCategory === 'filtreler') {
    const flow = row.flow_rate_lph ? `${row.flow_rate_lph} L/h debi • ` : '';
    const shrimp = row.shrimp_safe ? 'Karides güvenli • ' : '';
    return `${flow}${shrimp}Filtrasyon gücü: %${Math.round(Number(row.filtration_power || 0) * 100)}.`;
  }
  if (subCategory === 'hava-motorlari') {
    return `${row.watt || 0}W • ${row.outlet_count || 1} çıkışlı • ${row.flow_rate_lph || 0} L/h hava motoru.`;
  }
  if (subCategory === 'hava-taslari') {
    return `${row.length_cm || 3} cm hava taşı / difüzör (Hava motoru gerektirir).`;
  }
  if (category === 'kumlar') {
    return `1 Kg başına ${row.volume_per_unit_lt ?? 0.65}L taban hacmi sağlar.`;
  }
  if (category === 'bitkiler') {
    return `Doğal nitrat ve CO₂ tüketimi ile su kalitesini dengeler.`;
  }
  const temp =
    row.min_temp && row.max_temp ? `${row.min_temp}-${row.max_temp}°C • ` : '';
  const ph =
    row.min_ph && row.max_ph ? `pH ${row.min_ph}-${row.max_ph}` : '';
  return `${temp}${ph}`.trim() || 'Akvaryum ekosistemi uyumlu ürün.';
}

function normalizeRowToProduct(
  row: any,
  category: Product['category'],
  subCategory: string
): Product {
  const slug = String(row.slug || row.id);
  const name = String(row.name || 'İsimsiz Ürün');
  const bodyColor = row.color
    ? String(row.color)
    : getDefaultColorBySlug(slug, category);

  return {
    id: String(row.id),
    slug,
    species: row.species ? String(row.species) : undefined,
    variant: row.variant ? String(row.variant) : 'standart',
    name,
    category,
    sub_category: subCategory,
    price: Number(row.price ?? 0),
    min_liters: Number(row.min_volume_lt ?? row.min_liters ?? 0),
    ideal_temp_min: Number(row.min_temp ?? 20),
    ideal_temp_max: Number(row.max_temp ?? 28),
    ideal_ph_min: Number(row.min_ph ?? 6.5),
    ideal_ph_max: Number(row.max_ph ?? 7.8),
    bio_load: Number(row.bio_load ?? 0),
    volume_per_unit_lt:
      row.volume_per_unit_lt !== undefined && row.volume_per_unit_lt !== null
        ? Number(row.volume_per_unit_lt)
        : undefined,
    temperament: row.temperament ? String(row.temperament) : undefined,
    description: buildAutoDescription(row, category, subCategory),
    color: bodyColor,
    fin_color: row.fin_color ? String(row.fin_color) : bodyColor,
    image_url: row.image_url ? String(row.image_url) : undefined,
    swim_zone: (row.swim_zone as Product['swim_zone']) || 'orta',
    length_cm: Number(row.length_cm ?? (category === 'bitkiler' ? 14 : 4.5)),

    ammonia_rate: Number(row.ammonia_rate ?? 0),
    nitrite_rate: Number(row.nitrite_rate ?? 0),
    nitrate_rate: Number(row.nitrate_rate ?? 0),
    o2_rate: Number(row.o2_rate ?? 0),
    co2_rate: Number(row.co2_rate ?? 0),
    ppm_impact: Number(row.ppm_impact ?? 0),
    gh_impact: Number(row.gh_impact ?? 0),
    kh_impact: Number(row.kh_impact ?? 0),
    ph_impact: Number(row.ph_impact ?? 0),
    tannin_rate: Number(row.tannin_rate ?? 0),
    algae_control_rate: Number(row.algae_control_rate ?? 0),
    lumen: Number(row.lumen ?? 0),
    kelvin: row.kelvin ? Number(row.kelvin) : undefined,
    light_spectrum: row.light_spectrum ? String(row.light_spectrum) : undefined,

    watt: row.watt !== undefined && row.watt !== null ? Number(row.watt) : undefined,
    outlet_count:
      row.outlet_count !== undefined && row.outlet_count !== null
        ? Number(row.outlet_count)
        : undefined,
    flow_rate_lph:
      row.flow_rate_lph !== undefined && row.flow_rate_lph !== null
        ? Number(row.flow_rate_lph)
        : undefined,
    filtration_power:
      row.filtration_power !== undefined && row.filtration_power !== null
        ? Number(row.filtration_power)
        : undefined,
    shrimp_safe:
      row.shrimp_safe !== undefined && row.shrimp_safe !== null
        ? Boolean(row.shrimp_safe)
        : undefined,
    needs_air_pump:
      row.needs_air_pump !== undefined && row.needs_air_pump !== null
        ? Boolean(row.needs_air_pump)
        : undefined,
    placement_zone: row.placement_zone ? String(row.placement_zone) : undefined,
  };
}

export async function fetchCatalogWithStatus(): Promise<CatalogResponse> {
  try {
    const { createClient } = await import('./client');
    const supabase = createClient();

    if (!supabase) {
      return {
        products: [],
        categories: [],
        rules: [],
        source: 'error',
        errorMessage: '.env.local dosyasında NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL veya ANON_KEY eksik.',
      };
    }

    // Kolon bağımlılığı olmaması için .order() kullanmadan ham SELECT * çekip JS tarafında sıralıyoruz
    const [
      categoriesRes,
      rulesRes,
      baliklarRes,
      karideslerRes,
      salyangozlarRes,
      bitkilerRes,
      kumlarRes,
      aydinlatmalarRes,
      havaMotorlariRes,
      havaTaslariRes,
      filtrelerRes,
    ] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('catalog_categories').select('*'),
      supabase.from('compatibility_rules').select('*'),
      supabase.from('baliklar').select('*'),
      supabase.from('karidesler').select('*'),
      supabase.from('salyangozlar').select('*'),
      supabase.from('bitkiler').select('*'),
      supabase.from('kumlar').select('*'),
      supabase.from('aydinlatmalar').select('*'),
      supabase.from('hava_motorlari').select('*'),
      supabase.from('hava_taslari').select('*'),
      supabase.from('filtreler').select('*'),
    ]);

    const tableQueries = [
      { table: 'baliklar', res: baliklarRes },
      { table: 'karidesler', res: karideslerRes },
      { table: 'salyangozlar', res: salyangozlarRes },
      { table: 'bitkiler', res: bitkilerRes },
      { table: 'kumlar', res: kumlarRes },
      { table: 'aydinlatmalar', res: aydinlatmalarRes },
      { table: 'hava_motorlari', res: havaMotorlariRes },
      { table: 'hava_taslari', res: havaTaslariRes },
      { table: 'filtreler', res: filtrelerRes },
    ];

    const errors = tableQueries
      .filter((q) => q.res.error)
      .map((q) => `${q.table}: ${q.res.error!.message} (${q.res.error!.code})`);

    const allProducts: Product[] = [
      ...(baliklarRes.data || []).map((r) => normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'canlilar', 'baliklar')),
      ...(karideslerRes.data || []).map((r) => normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'canlilar', 'karidesler')),
      ...(salyangozlarRes.data || []).map((r) => normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'canlilar', 'salyangozlar')),
      ...(bitkilerRes.data || []).map((r) => normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'bitkiler', 'bitkiler')),
      ...(kumlarRes.data || []).map((r) => normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'kumlar', 'kumlar')),
      ...(aydinlatmalarRes.data || []).map((r) =>
        normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'malzemeler', 'aydinlatmalar')
      ),
      ...(havaMotorlariRes.data || []).map((r) =>
        normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'malzemeler', 'hava-motorlari')
      ),
      ...(havaTaslariRes.data || []).map((r) =>
        normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'malzemeler', 'hava-taslari')
      ),
      ...(filtrelerRes.data || []).map((r) =>
        normalizeRowToProduct(r, 'malzemeler', 'filtreler')
      ),
    ];

    // İsim sırasına göre JS içinde güvenli sırala
    allProducts.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'tr'));

    const normalizedCategories: CatalogCategory[] = (categoriesRes.data || [])
      .map((c: any) => ({
        id: Number(c.id),
        name: String(c.name),
        slug: String(c.slug),
        parent_slug: c.parent_slug ? String(c.parent_slug) : null,
        target_table: c.target_table ? String(c.target_table) : null,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);

    const normalizedRules: CompatibilityRule[] = (rulesRes.data || []).map((r: any) => ({
      id: String(r.id),
      item_a_slug: String(r.item_a_slug),
      item_b_slug: String(r.item_b_slug),
      status: String(r.status),
      reason: String(r.reason),
    }));

    // Hiç ürün dönmediyse nedenini açıkça raporla (RLS veya tablo hatası)
    if (allProducts.length === 0) {
      const detail =
        errors.length > 0
          ? `Tablo Hataları -> ${errors.join(' | ')}`
          : 'Tüm tablolardan 0 satır döndü. Supabase RLS (Row Level Security) açık kalmış olabilir; tablolara SELECT (anon) izni verin.';
      return {
        products: [],
        categories: normalizedCategories,
        rules: normalizedRules,
        source: 'error',
        errorMessage: detail,
      };
    }

    return {
      products: allProducts,
      categories: normalizedCategories,
      rules: normalizedRules,
      source: 'supabase',
      errorMessage: errors.length > 0 ? errors.join(' | ') : undefined,
    };
  } catch (err: any) {
    return {
      products: [],
      categories: [],
      rules: [],
      source: 'error',
      errorMessage: err?.message || 'Bilinmeyen bağlantı hatası',
    };
  }
}

export async function fetchCatalog(): Promise<Product[]> {
  const res = await fetchCatalogWithStatus();
  if (res.errorMessage) {
    console.error('[Supabase Katalog Uyarısı]:', res.errorMessage);
  }
  return res.products;
}

========================================
DOSYA: lib\supabase\client.ts
========================================
import { createClient as createSupabaseClient, SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

let supabaseInstance: SupabaseClient | null = null;

export const createClient = () => {
  if (supabaseInstance) {
    return supabaseInstance;
  }

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const supabaseKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY;

  if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseKey) {
    return null;
  }

  supabaseInstance = createSupabaseClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey);
  return supabaseInstance;
};

========================================
DOSYA: models\baliklar\beta\betaAnatomyBuilder.ts
========================================
import * as THREE from 'three';

export type BettaSex = 'erkek' | 'disi';
export type BettaFinVariant = 'halfmoon' | 'crowntail' | 'plakat' | 'dumbo' | 'round';

export interface ProceduralBettaAssets {
  bodyGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  bodyMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  finGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  finMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  eyeGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  eyeMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  updateShaderTime: (time: number) => void;
}

function createBettaSkinTextures(sex: BettaSex): {
  diffuseMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  bumpMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  roughnessMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
} {
  const width = 512;
  const height = 256;

  const diffCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  diffCanvas.width = width;
  diffCanvas.height = height;
  const dCtx = diffCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const bumpCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  bumpCanvas.width = width;
  bumpCanvas.height = height;
  const bCtx = bumpCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const roughCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  roughCanvas.width = width;
  roughCanvas.height = height;
  const rCtx = roughCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  // A. Açık Gümüş-İridofor Taban (instanceColor ile her rengi net verir)
  const baseGrad = dCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#475569');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.30, '#cbd5e1');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.60, '#ffffff');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.88, '#f1f5f9');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#cbd5e1');
  } else {
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#64748b');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.35, '#e2e8f0');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.75, '#f8fafc');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#ffffff');
  }
  dCtx.fillStyle = baseGrad;
  dCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  // Kafa maskesi (pulsuz koyu kadife doku)
  const headGrad = dCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width * 0.25, 0);
  headGrad.addColorStop(0.0, 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.72)');
  headGrad.addColorStop(0.75, 'rgba(30, 41, 59, 0.45)');
  headGrad.addColorStop(1.0, 'rgba(30, 41, 59, 0)');
  dCtx.fillStyle = headGrad;
  dCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width * 0.25, height);

  bCtx.fillStyle = '#404040';
  bCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
  rCtx.fillStyle = '#343434';
  rCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  // B. Kiremit Dizilimli Sikloid Pullar
  const scaleStartX = Math.floor(width * 0.23);
  const scaleRadiusX = 9.5;
  const scaleRadiusY = 8.5;
  const stepX = 11.5;
  const stepY = 10.5;

  for (let row = 0; row < height / stepY + 2; row++) {
    const offsetX = (row % 2) * (stepX * 0.5);
    const cy = row * stepY;

    for (let cx = scaleStartX + offsetX; cx < width + stepX; cx += stepX) {
      const distFromMid = Math.abs(cy - height * 0.5) / (height * 0.5);
      if (distFromMid > 0.93) continue;

      const scaleGrad = dCtx.createRadialGradient(cx - 2, cy - 1, 1, cx, cy, scaleRadiusX);
      scaleGrad.addColorStop(0.0, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.52)');
      scaleGrad.addColorStop(0.65, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14)');
      scaleGrad.addColorStop(1.0, 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.34)');

      dCtx.beginPath();
      dCtx.ellipse(cx, cy, scaleRadiusX, scaleRadiusY, 0, -Math.PI * 0.46, Math.PI * 0.46);
      dCtx.fillStyle = scaleGrad;
      dCtx.fill();
      dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.28)';
      dCtx.lineWidth = 1.1;
      dCtx.stroke();

      const bumpGrad = bCtx.createRadialGradient(cx - 1.5, cy, 1, cx, cy, scaleRadiusX);
      bumpGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#ededed');
      bumpGrad.addColorStop(0.78, '#858585');
      bumpGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#1c1c1c');

      bCtx.beginPath();
      bCtx.ellipse(cx, cy, scaleRadiusX, scaleRadiusY, 0, -Math.PI * 0.48, Math.PI * 0.48);
      bCtx.fillStyle = bumpGrad;
      bCtx.fill();

      rCtx.beginPath();
      rCtx.ellipse(cx, cy, scaleRadiusX * 0.78, scaleRadiusY * 0.78, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      rCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(22, 22, 22, 0.40)';
      rCtx.fill();
    }
  }

  // C. Dişi Ovipositor (Beyaz Üreme Papillası) & Dikey Üreme Bantları
  if (sex === 'disi') {
    dCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18)';
    for (let x = width * 0.30; x < width * 0.72; x += 26) {
      dCtx.fillRect(x, height * 0.22, 9, height * 0.54);
    }

    const oviX = width * 0.52;
    const oviY = height * 0.93;
    const oviGrad = dCtx.createRadialGradient(oviX, oviY, 1, oviX, oviY, 11);
    oviGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#ffffff');
    oviGrad.addColorStop(0.55, '#fef3c7');
    oviGrad.addColorStop(1.0, 'rgba(254, 243, 199, 0)');
    dCtx.fillStyle = oviGrad;
    dCtx.beginPath();
    dCtx.arc(oviX, oviY, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    dCtx.fill();
  }

  // D. Kesikli Yan Çizgi (Lateral Line)
  const drawBettaLateralLine = (
    ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
    color: string,
    dotRadius: number
  ) => {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    const steps = 28;
    for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
      const t = i / steps;
      const x = width * (0.24 + t * 0.73);
      const y = height * (0.43 - Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 0.045 + t * 0.05);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(x, y, dotRadius * 1.6, dotRadius * 0.85, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  };
  drawBettaLateralLine(dCtx, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.72)', 1.5);
  drawBettaLateralLine(bCtx, '#f5f5f5', 1.8);

  // E. Solungaç Kapağı + Koyu Branchiostegal Sakal
  dCtx.save();
  dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)';
  dCtx.lineWidth = 2.4;
  dCtx.beginPath();
  dCtx.arc(width * 0.175, height * 0.50, 26, -Math.PI * 0.34, Math.PI * 0.34);
  dCtx.stroke();

  dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.85)';
  dCtx.lineWidth = 3.0;
  dCtx.beginPath();
  dCtx.arc(width * 0.195, height * 0.50, 28, -Math.PI * 0.42, Math.PI * 0.42);
  dCtx.stroke();

  dCtx.fillStyle = sex === 'erkek' ? 'rgba(5, 5, 10, 0.90)' : 'rgba(30, 12, 20, 0.65)';
  dCtx.beginPath();
  dCtx.ellipse(width * 0.16, height * 0.84, 24, 12, 0.15, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  dCtx.fill();
  dCtx.restore();

  const diffuseMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(diffCanvas);
  diffuseMap.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  diffuseMap.needsUpdate = true;

  const bumpMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(bumpCanvas);
  bumpMap.needsUpdate = true;

  const roughnessMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(roughCanvas);
  roughnessMap.needsUpdate = true;

  return { diffuseMap, bumpMap, roughnessMap };
}

/**
 * Yüzgeç Işınları ve Zar Dokusu (Crowntail için zar gerilemesi alpha maskesi destekli)
 */
function createBettaFinTextures(
  sex: BettaSex,
  finVariant: BettaFinVariant
): {
  finDiffuse: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  finAlpha: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  finBump: THREE.CanvasTexture;
} {
  const width = 512;
  const height = 256;

  const diffCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  diffCanvas.width = width;
  diffCanvas.height = height;
  const dCtx = diffCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  alphaCanvas.width = width;
  alphaCanvas.height = height;
  const aCtx = alphaCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const bumpCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  bumpCanvas.width = width;
  bumpCanvas.height = height;
  const bCtx = bumpCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  // Açık beyaz-gümüş butterfly gradyanı (instanceColor ile boyanır)
  const memGrad = dCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
  memGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#cbd5e1');
  memGrad.addColorStop(0.35, '#ffffff');
  memGrad.addColorStop(0.85, '#f8fafc');
  memGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#e2e8f0');
  dCtx.fillStyle = memGrad;
  dCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const alphaGrad = aCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
  if (finVariant === 'crowntail') {
    // Crowntail: %65'ten sonra zar içbükey çekilir, sadece kemik ışınları uzar
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#ffffff');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.58, '#dcdcdc');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.68, '#000000');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#000000');
  } else if (sex === 'erkek') {
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#ffffff');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.65, '#dcdcdc');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.92, '#a8a8a8');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#787878');
  } else {
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#e5e5e5');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.7, '#9e9e9e');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#666666');
  }
  aCtx.fillStyle = alphaGrad;
  aCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  bCtx.fillStyle = '#2b2b2b';
  bCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const rayCount = sex === 'erkek' ? 24 : 16;
  for (let i = 0; i < rayCount; i++) {
    const vNorm = (i + 0.5) / rayCount;
    const yStart = vNorm * height;
    const fanAngle = (vNorm - 0.5) * 18;
    const split1X = width * 0.42;
    const split2X = width * 0.72;
    const y1 = yStart + fanAngle * 0.5;

    const primaryColor = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.48)';
    const secondaryColor = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.40)';

    drawBettaRay(dCtx, 0, yStart, split1X, y1, 2.6, primaryColor);
    drawBettaRay(aCtx, 0, yStart, split1X, y1, 3.2, '#ffffff');
    drawBettaRay(bCtx, 0, yStart, split1X, y1, 3.2, '#ffffff');

    const spread1 = 4.2;
    const y2Top = y1 - spread1 + fanAngle * 0.3;
    const y2Bot = y1 + spread1 + fanAngle * 0.3;

    drawBettaRay(dCtx, split1X, y1, split2X, y2Top, 1.8, secondaryColor);
    drawBettaRay(dCtx, split1X, y1, split2X, y2Bot, 1.8, secondaryColor);
    drawBettaRay(aCtx, split1X, y1, split2X, y2Top, 2.5, '#ffffff');
    drawBettaRay(aCtx, split1X, y1, split2X, y2Bot, 2.5, '#ffffff');
    drawBettaRay(bCtx, split1X, y1, split2X, y2Top, 2.2, '#e5e5e5');
    drawBettaRay(bCtx, split1X, y1, split2X, y2Bot, 2.2, '#e5e5e5');

    // Crowntail varyantında uçlara uzanan dikenli taç ışınları tam opak (#ffffff) çizilir
    const tipAlpha = finVariant === 'crowntail' ? '#ffffff' : '#e5e5e5';
    const tipWidth = finVariant === 'crowntail' ? 2.4 : 1.4;
    const spread2 = 2.4;
    [
      [y2Top, y2Top - spread2],
      [y2Top, y2Top + spread2 * 0.6],
      [y2Bot, y2Bot - spread2 * 0.6],
      [y2Bot, y2Bot + spread2],
    ].forEach(([ya, yb]) => {
      drawBettaRay(dCtx, split2X, ya, width, yb, 1.4, secondaryColor);
      drawBettaRay(aCtx, split2X, ya, width, yb, tipWidth, tipAlpha);
      drawBettaRay(bCtx, split2X, ya, width, yb, 1.6, '#cccccc');
    });
  }

  const finDiffuse = new THREE.CanvasTexture(diffCanvas);
  finDiffuse.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  finDiffuse.needsUpdate = true;

  const finAlpha = new THREE.CanvasTexture(alphaCanvas);
  finAlpha.needsUpdate = true;

  const finBump = new THREE.CanvasTexture(bumpCanvas);
  finBump.needsUpdate = true;

  return { finDiffuse, finAlpha, finBump };
}

function drawBettaRay(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  x1: number,
  yA: number,
  x2: number,
  yB: number,
  lw: number,
  style: string
) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = style;
  ctx.lineWidth = lw;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x1, yA);
  ctx.lineTo(x2, yB);
  ctx.stroke();
}

function createBettaEyeTexture(sex: BettaSex): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const size = 128;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = size;
  canvas.height = size;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const cx = size / 2;
  const cy = size / 2;

  ctx.fillStyle = '#0f172a';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);

  const irisGrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 14, cx, cy, 56);
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    irisGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#38bdf8');
    irisGrad.addColorStop(0.55, '#1d4ed8');
    irisGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#020617');
  } else {
    irisGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#f472b6');
    irisGrad.addColorStop(0.55, '#9d174d');
    irisGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#1f0913');
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = irisGrad;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx, cy, 56, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = '#030305';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx, cy, 30, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.92)';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx - 11, cy - 11, 7.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

function buildBettaBodyGeometry(sex: BettaSex, finVariant: BettaFinVariant): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  interface CrossSection {
    z: number;
    rx: number;
    ryTop: number;
    ryBot: number;
    yCenter: number;
  }

  // Plakat varyantında daha kaslı/kalın peduncle ve gövde oranı
  const muscleBoost = finVariant === 'plakat' ? 1.12 : 1.0;

  const sections: CrossSection[] =
    sex === 'erkek'
      ? [
          { z: 0.230, rx: 0.010, ryTop: 0.008, ryBot: 0.012, yCenter: 0.022 },
          { z: 0.212, rx: 0.025, ryTop: 0.020, ryBot: 0.026, yCenter: 0.016 },
          { z: 0.175, rx: 0.038, ryTop: 0.035, ryBot: 0.040, yCenter: 0.008 },
          { z: 0.130, rx: 0.048 * muscleBoost, ryTop: 0.046 * muscleBoost, ryBot: 0.056, yCenter: 0.000 },
          { z: 0.075, rx: 0.046 * muscleBoost, ryTop: 0.054 * muscleBoost, ryBot: 0.054 * muscleBoost, yCenter: 0.000 },
          { z: 0.005, rx: 0.042 * muscleBoost, ryTop: 0.056 * muscleBoost, ryBot: 0.052 * muscleBoost, yCenter: 0.000 },
          { z: -0.070, rx: 0.034 * muscleBoost, ryTop: 0.048 * muscleBoost, ryBot: 0.044 * muscleBoost, yCenter: 0.001 },
          { z: -0.145, rx: 0.024 * muscleBoost, ryTop: 0.036 * muscleBoost, ryBot: 0.034 * muscleBoost, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.218, rx: 0.015 * muscleBoost, ryTop: 0.030 * muscleBoost, ryBot: 0.028 * muscleBoost, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.230, rx: 0.004, ryTop: 0.022, ryBot: 0.022, yCenter: 0.002 },
        ]
      : [
          { z: 0.215, rx: 0.010, ryTop: 0.008, ryBot: 0.012, yCenter: 0.020 },
          { z: 0.198, rx: 0.026, ryTop: 0.022, ryBot: 0.026, yCenter: 0.014 },
          { z: 0.162, rx: 0.040, ryTop: 0.036, ryBot: 0.042, yCenter: 0.007 },
          { z: 0.118, rx: 0.050, ryTop: 0.048, ryBot: 0.056, yCenter: 0.000 },
          { z: 0.055, rx: 0.056, ryTop: 0.056, ryBot: 0.074, yCenter: -0.005 },
          { z: -0.015, rx: 0.054, ryTop: 0.054, ryBot: 0.076, yCenter: -0.006 },
          { z: -0.080, rx: 0.038, ryTop: 0.044, ryBot: 0.052, yCenter: -0.001 },
          { z: -0.148, rx: 0.024, ryTop: 0.034, ryBot: 0.032, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.215, rx: 0.014, ryTop: 0.028, ryBot: 0.026, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.228, rx: 0.004, ryTop: 0.020, ryBot: 0.020, yCenter: 0.002 },
        ];

  const radialSegments = 26;
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const zMin = sections[sections.length - 1].z;
  const zMax = sections[0].z;

  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length; s++) {
    const sec = sections[s];
    const u = (zMax - sec.z) / (zMax - zMin);

    for (let r = 0; r <= radialSegments; r++) {
      const theta = (r / radialSegments) * Math.PI * 2;
      const sinT = Math.sin(theta);
      const cosT = Math.cos(theta);

      const x = sinT * sec.rx;
      const ry = cosT >= 0 ? sec.ryTop : sec.ryBot;
      const y = sec.yCenter + cosT * ry;
      const v = 0.5 - cosT * 0.5;

      positions.push(x, y, sec.z);
      uvs.push(u, v);
    }
  }

  const ringStride = radialSegments + 1;
  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length - 1; s++) {
    for (let r = 0; r < radialSegments; r++) {
      const a = s * ringStride + r;
      const b = (s + 1) * ringStride + r;
      const c = (s + 1) * ringStride + (r + 1);
      const d = s * ringStride + (r + 1);

      indices.push(a, b, d);
      indices.push(b, c, d);
    }
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

/**
 * Beta Varyant Yüzgeçleri:
 * - halfmoon: 180° geniş yarım daire yelpaze
 * - crowntail: Dikenli taç uzantılı 180° kuyruk
 * - plakat: Kısa ve kompakt kürek/yarım-delta yüzgeçler
 * - dumbo: Fil kulağı gibi aşırı genişlemiş yelpaze göğüs yüzgeçleri + Halfmoon/Delta kuyruk
 * - round: Dişi yuvarlak kompakt yüzgeçler
 */
function buildBettaFinsGeometry(sex: BettaSex, finVariant: BettaFinVariant): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const addFinRibbon = (
    rootPoints: THREE.Vector3[],
    tipPoints: THREE.Vector3[],
    uSteps = 8
  ) => {
    const vSteps = rootPoints.length;
    const baseVertexIndex = positions.length / 3;

    for (let ui = 0; ui <= uSteps; ui++) {
      const u = ui / uSteps;
      for (let vi = 0; vi < vSteps; vi++) {
        const v = vi / Math.max(1, vSteps - 1);
        const p = new THREE.Vector3().lerpVectors(rootPoints[vi], tipPoints[vi], u);
        positions.push(p.x, p.y, p.z);
        uvs.push(u, v);
      }
    }

    for (let ui = 0; ui < uSteps; ui++) {
      for (let vi = 0; vi < vSteps - 1; vi++) {
        const a = baseVertexIndex + ui * vSteps + vi;
        const b = baseVertexIndex + (ui + 1) * vSteps + vi;
        const c = baseVertexIndex + (ui + 1) * vSteps + (vi + 1);
        const d = baseVertexIndex + ui * vSteps + (vi + 1);

        indices.push(a, b, d);
        indices.push(b, c, d);
      }
    }
  };

  if (sex === 'erkek' && finVariant === 'plakat') {
    // Plakat (Kısa Yüzgeçli Geleneksel): Kompakt kürek/yarım-delta kuyruk + kısa dorsal ve anal
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.028, -0.215),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.010, -0.222),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.010, -0.222),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.028, -0.215),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.115, -0.340),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.048, -0.375),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.048, -0.375),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.115, -0.340),
      ],
      7
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.056, -0.015),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.048, -0.075),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.038, -0.135),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.125, -0.045),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.135, -0.115),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.105, -0.175),
      ],
      6
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.052, -0.030),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.042, -0.100),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.032, -0.170),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.128, -0.055),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.135, -0.130),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.102, -0.195),
      ],
      6
    );

    // Pelvik yüzgeçler
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0.016, -0.052, 0.085),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.014, -0.052, 0.058),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0.028, -0.175, 0.030),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.022, -0.185, 0.005),
      ],
      5
    );
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.016, -0.052, 0.085),
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.014, -0.052, 0.058),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.028, -0.175, 0.030),
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.022, -0.185, 0.005),
      ],
      5
    );
  } else if (sex === 'erkek') {
    // Halfmoon / Crowntail / Dumbo (Uzun gösterişli yüzgeç seti)
    const rayReach = finVariant === 'crowntail' ? 1.08 : 1.0;

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.028, -0.215),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.014, -0.222),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.000, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.014, -0.222),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.028, -0.215),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.245 * rayReach, -0.255),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.175 * rayReach, -0.425 * rayReach),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.000, -0.485 * rayReach),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.175 * rayReach, -0.425 * rayReach),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.245 * rayReach, -0.255),
      ],
      10
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.054, 0.015),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.050, -0.050),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.042, -0.115),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.034, -0.175),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.185 * rayReach, -0.010),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.225 * rayReach, -0.110),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.220 * rayReach, -0.215),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.175 * rayReach, -0.295),
      ],
      8
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.050, -0.020),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.045, -0.080),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.038, -0.145),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.030, -0.200),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.195 * rayReach, -0.045),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.235 * rayReach, -0.135),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.240 * rayReach, -0.230),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.205 * rayReach, -0.310),
      ],
      8
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0.016, -0.052, 0.085),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.014, -0.052, 0.058),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0.032, -0.220, 0.015),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.026, -0.235, -0.015),
      ],
      6
    );
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.016, -0.052, 0.085),
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.014, -0.052, 0.058),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.032, -0.220, 0.015),
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.026, -0.235, -0.015),
      ],
      6
    );
  } else {
    // Dişi Roundtail
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.026, -0.212),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.008, -0.220),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.008, -0.220),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.026, -0.212),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.085, -0.315),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.035, -0.350),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.035, -0.350),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.085, -0.315),
      ],
      6
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.052, -0.025),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.046, -0.075),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.038, -0.125),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.105, -0.055),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.112, -0.115),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.088, -0.165),
      ],
      5
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.056, -0.045),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.044, -0.105),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.032, -0.165),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.118, -0.065),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.115, -0.130),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.088, -0.185),
      ],
      5
    );

    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0.018, -0.065, 0.075),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.016, -0.068, 0.050),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0.030, -0.145, 0.030),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.025, -0.152, 0.010),
      ],
      4
    );
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.018, -0.065, 0.075),
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.016, -0.068, 0.050),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.030, -0.145, 0.030),
        new THREE.Vector3(-0.025, -0.152, 0.010),
      ],
      4
    );
  }

  // E. Göğüs Yüzgeçleri (Dumbo varyantında "Fil Kulağı" gibi 2.1 kat büyük yelpaze!)
  const isDumbo = finVariant === 'dumbo';
  const pecY = sex === 'erkek' ? -0.014 : -0.020;
  const pecX = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.044 : 0.048;
  const pecSpan = isDumbo ? 0.235 : sex === 'erkek' ? 0.115 : 0.095;
  const pecZ = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.115 : 0.105;

  if (isDumbo) {
    // Dumbo: Geniş 4 noktalı yelpaze fil kulağı pektoral yüzgeç
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY + 0.020, pecZ + 0.005),
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY + 0.000, pecZ - 0.005),
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY - 0.020, pecZ - 0.015),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan, pecY + 0.075, pecZ - 0.065),
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan * 1.05, pecY - 0.010, pecZ - 0.095),
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan * 0.90, pecY - 0.085, pecZ - 0.085),
      ],
      7
    );
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY + 0.020, pecZ + 0.005),
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY + 0.000, pecZ - 0.005),
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY - 0.020, pecZ - 0.015),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan, pecY + 0.075, pecZ - 0.065),
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan * 1.05, pecY - 0.010, pecZ - 0.095),
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan * 0.90, pecY - 0.085, pecZ - 0.085),
      ],
      7
    );
  } else {
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY + 0.015, pecZ),
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY - 0.015, pecZ - 0.012),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan, pecY + 0.025, pecZ - 0.075),
        new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan * 0.92, pecY - 0.045, pecZ - 0.085),
      ],
      5
    );
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY + 0.015, pecZ),
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY - 0.015, pecZ - 0.012),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan, pecY + 0.025, pecZ - 0.075),
        new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan * 0.92, pecY - 0.045, pecZ - 0.085),
      ],
      5
    );
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

function buildBettaEyesGeometry(sex: BettaSex): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const eyeRadius = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.018 : 0.019;
  const eyeX = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.030 : 0.032;
  const eyeY = 0.015;
  const eyeZ = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.168 : 0.156;

  const leftEye = new THREE.SphereGeometry(eyeRadius, 14, 12);
  leftEye.rotateY(Math.PI / 2);
  leftEye.translate(eyeX, eyeY, eyeZ);

  const rightEye = new THREE.SphereGeometry(eyeRadius, 14, 12);
  rightEye.rotateY(-Math.PI / 2);
  rightEye.translate(-eyeX, eyeY, eyeZ);

  const nonIdxLeft = leftEye.toNonIndexed();
  const nonIdxRight = rightEye.toNonIndexed();

  const totalVerts = nonIdxLeft.attributes.position.count + nonIdxRight.attributes.position.count;
  const positions = new Float32Array(totalVerts * 3);
  const normals = new Float32Array(totalVerts * 3);
  const uvs = new Float32Array(totalVerts * 2);

  positions.set(nonIdxLeft.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, 0);
  positions.set(nonIdxRight.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, nonIdxLeft.attributes.position.count * 3);

  normals.set(nonIdxLeft.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, 0);
  normals.set(nonIdxRight.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, nonIdxLeft.attributes.normal.count * 3);

  uvs.set(nonIdxLeft.attributes.uv.array as Float32Array, 0);
  uvs.set(nonIdxRight.attributes.uv.array as Float32Array, nonIdxLeft.attributes.uv.count * 2);

  const merged = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  merged.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  merged.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(normals, 3));
  merged.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  return merged;
}

function applyBettaSwimShader(
  material: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial,
  timeUniform: { value: number },
  finMultiplier: number
) {
  material.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.uTime = timeUniform;
    shader.uniforms.uFinAmp = { value: finMultiplier };

    shader.vertexShader =
      `
      uniform float uTime;
      uniform float uFinAmp;
      ` + shader.vertexShader;

    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
      '#include <begin_vertex>',
      `
      #include <begin_vertex>
      float normalizedBack = clamp((0.18 - transformed.z) / 0.68, 0.0, 1.4);
      float spineWave = sin(uTime * 5.2 + transformed.z * 11.5) * pow(normalizedBack, 1.65) * 0.048 * uFinAmp;
      transformed.x += spineWave;

      float radialDist = length(vec2(transformed.y, min(0.0, transformed.z + 0.05)));
      float veilRipple = sin(uTime * 4.4 + transformed.y * 18.0 + transformed.z * 14.0) * smoothstep(0.06, 0.28, radialDist) * 0.022 * (uFinAmp - 0.8);
      transformed.x += max(0.0, uFinAmp - 1.0) * veilRipple;

      float pectoralMask = smoothstep(0.046, 0.13, abs(transformed.x));
      transformed.y += sin(uTime * 11.5 + transformed.x * 18.0) * pectoralMask * 0.024;
      transformed.z += cos(uTime * 11.5) * pectoralMask * 0.012;
      `
    );
  };
}

export function buildAnatomicalBettaAssets(
  sex: BettaSex,
  finVariant: BettaFinVariant = sex === 'erkek' ? 'halfmoon' : 'round'
): ProceduralBettaAssets {
  const timeUniform = { value: 0 };

  const bodyGeometry = buildBettaBodyGeometry(sex, finVariant);
  const { diffuseMap, bumpMap, roughnessMap } = createBettaSkinTextures(sex);

  const bodyMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff', // instanceColor ile tüm renk varyantlarını boyar
    map: diffuseMap,
    bumpMap: bumpMap,
    bumpScale: 0.02,
    roughnessMap: roughnessMap,
    roughness: 0.34,
    metalness: 0.16,
    iridescence: sex === 'erkek' ? 0.92 : 0.52,
    iridescenceIOR: 1.42,
    iridescenceThicknessRange: [200, 520],
    clearcoat: 0.72,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.14,
  });
  applyBettaSwimShader(bodyMaterial, timeUniform, 1.0);

  const finGeometry = buildBettaFinsGeometry(sex, finVariant);
  const { finDiffuse, finAlpha, finBump } = createBettaFinTextures(sex, finVariant);

  const finMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff', // instanceColor ile yüzgeç renklerini boyar
    map: finDiffuse,
    alphaMap: finAlpha,
    bumpMap: finBump,
    bumpScale: 0.014,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: sex === 'erkek' ? 0.90 : 0.80,
    alphaTest: finVariant === 'crowntail' ? 0.45 : 0.06,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    roughness: 0.26,
    metalness: 0.08,
    iridescence: sex === 'erkek' ? 0.75 : 0.35,
    depthWrite: false,
  });
  applyBettaSwimShader(finMaterial, timeUniform, finVariant === 'plakat' ? 1.15 : 1.55);

  const eyeGeometry = buildBettaEyesGeometry(sex);
  const eyeTexture = createBettaEyeTexture(sex);

  const eyeMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    map: eyeTexture,
    roughness: 0.08,
    metalness: 0.12,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
  });
  applyBettaSwimShader(eyeMaterial, timeUniform, 0.15);

  return {
    bodyGeometry,
    bodyMaterial,
    finGeometry,
    finMaterial,
    eyeGeometry,
    eyeMaterial,
    updateShaderTime: (time: number) => {
      timeUniform.value = time;
    },
  };
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\baliklar\lepistes\lepistesAnatomyBuilder.ts

import * as THREE from 'three';

export type GuppySex = 'erkek' | 'disi';
export type GuppyTailVariant = 'delta' | 'double-sword' | 'lyra' | 'round';

export interface ProceduralFishAssets {
  bodyGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  bodyMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  finGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  finMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  eyeGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  eyeMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  updateShaderTime: (time: number) => void;
}

/**
 * Nötr İridofor + Sikloid Pul + Lateral Çizgi + Kromatofor Desen Dokusu
 * (instanceColor ile çarpıldığında Golden, Moscow Blue, Blue Neon, Full Red vb. her rengi taşır)
 */
function createGuppySkinTextures(sex: GuppySex): {
  diffuseMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  bumpMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  roughnessMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
} {
  const width = 512;
  const height = 256;

  const diffCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  diffCanvas.width = width;
  diffCanvas.height = height;
  const dCtx = diffCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const bumpCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  bumpCanvas.width = width;
  bumpCanvas.height = height;
  const bCtx = bumpCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const roughCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  roughCanvas.width = width;
  roughCanvas.height = height;
  const rCtx = roughCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  // A. Açık Gümüşi-İnci Baz (instanceColor tonunu bozmadan derinlik verir)
  const baseGrad = dCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#64748b'); // Sırt gölgesi
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.28, '#cbd5e1'); // Üst yan
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.55, '#ffffff'); // Parlak iridofor orta hat
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.88, '#f8fafc'); // İnci karın
    baseGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#e2e8f0');
  } else {
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#78716c');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.32, '#d6d3d1');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(0.65, '#f5f5f4');
    baseGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#ffffff');
  }
  dCtx.fillStyle = baseGrad;
  dCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  bCtx.fillStyle = '#404040';
  bCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
  rCtx.fillStyle = '#343434';
  rCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  // B. Kiremit Dizilimli Sikloid Pullar
  const scaleStartX = Math.floor(width * 0.24);
  const scaleRadiusX = 9;
  const scaleRadiusY = 8;
  const stepX = 11;
  const stepY = 10;

  for (let row = 0; row < height / stepY + 2; row++) {
    const offsetX = (row % 2) * (stepX * 0.5);
    const cy = row * stepY;

    for (let cx = scaleStartX + offsetX; cx < width + stepX; cx += stepX) {
      const distFromMid = Math.abs(cy - height * 0.5) / (height * 0.5);
      if (distFromMid > 0.92) continue;

      const scaleGrad = dCtx.createRadialGradient(cx - 2, cy - 1, 1, cx, cy, scaleRadiusX);
      scaleGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.45)');
      scaleGrad.addColorStop(0.75, 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)');
      scaleGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.28)');

      dCtx.beginPath();
      dCtx.ellipse(cx, cy, scaleRadiusX, scaleRadiusY, 0, -Math.PI * 0.45, Math.PI * 0.45);
      dCtx.fillStyle = scaleGrad;
      dCtx.fill();
      dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.22)';
      dCtx.lineWidth = 1;
      dCtx.stroke();

      const bumpGrad = bCtx.createRadialGradient(cx - 1, cy, 1, cx, cy, scaleRadiusX);
      bumpGrad.addColorStop(0, '#e8e8e8');
      bumpGrad.addColorStop(0.8, '#808080');
      bumpGrad.addColorStop(1, '#202020');

      bCtx.beginPath();
      bCtx.ellipse(cx, cy, scaleRadiusX, scaleRadiusY, 0, -Math.PI * 0.48, Math.PI * 0.48);
      bCtx.fillStyle = bumpGrad;
      bCtx.fill();

      rCtx.beginPath();
      rCtx.ellipse(cx, cy, scaleRadiusX * 0.8, scaleRadiusY * 0.8, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      rCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(24, 24, 24, 0.35)';
      rCtx.fill();
    }
  }

  // C. Cinsiyet Desenleri
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    // Hafif Cobra/Snakeskin melanofor şeritleri (renk katmanının altından seçilir)
    dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.36)';
    dCtx.lineWidth = 2.6;
    for (let x = width * 0.32; x < width * 0.88; x += 15) {
      dCtx.beginPath();
      dCtx.moveTo(x, height * 0.24);
      dCtx.quadraticCurveTo(x + 4, height * 0.5, x - 2, height * 0.74);
      dCtx.stroke();
    }
  } else {
    // Dişi Gravid Spot (gebelik lekesi)
    const gravidGrad = dCtx.createRadialGradient(
      width * 0.56,
      height * 0.74,
      2,
      width * 0.56,
      height * 0.74,
      22
    );
    gravidGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(15, 10, 10, 0.90)');
    gravidGrad.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(69, 26, 3, 0.48)');
    gravidGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(69, 26, 3, 0)');
    dCtx.fillStyle = gravidGrad;
    dCtx.beginPath();
    dCtx.ellipse(width * 0.56, height * 0.74, 22, 14, -0.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    dCtx.fill();
  }

  // D. Lateral Çizgi & Operculum
  dCtx.save();
  dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)';
  dCtx.lineWidth = 1.6;
  ctxDashedLine(dCtx, width, height);
  dCtx.restore();

  bCtx.save();
  bCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
  bCtx.lineWidth = 2.4;
  ctxDashedLine(bCtx, width, height);
  bCtx.restore();

  // Solungaç kapağı
  dCtx.save();
  dCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.65)';
  dCtx.lineWidth = 2.4;
  dCtx.beginPath();
  dCtx.arc(width * 0.19, height * 0.50, 26, -Math.PI * 0.42, Math.PI * 0.42);
  dCtx.stroke();
  dCtx.restore();

  const diffuseMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(diffCanvas);
  diffuseMap.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  diffuseMap.needsUpdate = true;

  const bumpMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(bumpCanvas);
  bumpMap.needsUpdate = true;

  const roughnessMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(roughCanvas);
  roughnessMap.needsUpdate = true;

  return { diffuseMap, bumpMap, roughnessMap };
}

function ctxDashedLine(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number) {
  ctx.setLineDash([4, 2]);
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(width * 0.24, height * 0.44);
  ctx.bezierCurveTo(
    width * 0.45,
    height * 0.40,
    width * 0.70,
    height * 0.47,
    width * 0.98,
    height * 0.49
  );
  ctx.stroke();
}

/**
 * Yüzgeç Işınları (Lepidotrichia) ve Yarı Şeffaf Zar Dokusu (Renk instanceColor'dan gelir)
 */
function createFinTextures(sex: GuppySex): {
  finDiffuse: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  finAlpha: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  finBump: THREE.CanvasTexture;
} {
  const width = 256;
  const height = 256;

  const diffCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  diffCanvas.width = width;
  diffCanvas.height = height;
  const dCtx = diffCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const alphaCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  alphaCanvas.width = width;
  alphaCanvas.height = height;
  const aCtx = alphaCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const bumpCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  bumpCanvas.width = width;
  bumpCanvas.height = height;
  const bCtx = bumpCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  // Açık beyaz-gümüş zar gradyanı (instanceColor ile çarpıldığında saf, canlı renk verir)
  const memGrad = dCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
  memGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#cbd5e1');
  memGrad.addColorStop(0.35, '#ffffff');
  memGrad.addColorStop(0.85, '#f8fafc');
  memGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#e2e8f0');
  dCtx.fillStyle = memGrad;
  dCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const alphaGrad = aCtx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#ffffff');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.7, '#dadada');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#9e9e9e');
  } else {
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.0, '#d4d4d4');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(0.6, '#888888');
    alphaGrad.addColorStop(1.0, '#555555');
  }
  aCtx.fillStyle = alphaGrad;
  aCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  bCtx.fillStyle = '#303030';
  bCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const rayCount = 18;
  for (let i = 0; i < rayCount; i++) {
    const vNorm = (i + 0.5) / rayCount;
    const yStart = vNorm * height;
    const yMid = yStart + (vNorm - 0.5) * 14;
    const splitX = width * 0.48;

    const rayColor = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.42)';
    drawRaySeg(dCtx, 0, yStart, splitX, yMid, 2.0, rayColor);
    drawRaySeg(aCtx, 0, yStart, splitX, yMid, 2.4, '#ffffff');
    drawRaySeg(bCtx, 0, yStart, splitX, yMid, 2.6, '#ffffff');

    const branchSpread = 5.5;
    drawRaySeg(dCtx, splitX, yMid, width, yMid - branchSpread, 1.2, rayColor);
    drawRaySeg(dCtx, splitX, yMid, width, yMid + branchSpread, 1.2, rayColor);
    drawRaySeg(aCtx, splitX, yMid, width, yMid - branchSpread, 1.6, '#f5f5f5');
    drawRaySeg(aCtx, splitX, yMid, width, yMid + branchSpread, 1.6, '#f5f5f5');
    drawRaySeg(bCtx, splitX, yMid, width, yMid - branchSpread, 1.8, '#e5e5e5');
    drawRaySeg(bCtx, splitX, yMid, width, yMid + branchSpread, 1.8, '#e5e5e5');
  }

  // Erkek kuyruğunda zarif koyu leopar benekleri
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    dCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.55)';
    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
      const sx = width * (0.30 + ((i * 37) % 65) / 100);
      const sy = height * (((i * 53) % 88 + 6) / 100);
      const r = 2.0 + (i % 3) * 0.9;
      dCtx.beginPath();
      dCtx.arc(sx, sy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      dCtx.fill();
    }
  }

  const finDiffuse = new THREE.CanvasTexture(diffCanvas);
  finDiffuse.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  finDiffuse.needsUpdate = true;

  const finAlpha = new THREE.CanvasTexture(alphaCanvas);
  finAlpha.needsUpdate = true;

  const finBump = new THREE.CanvasTexture(bumpCanvas);
  finBump.needsUpdate = true;

  return { finDiffuse, finAlpha, finBump };
}

function drawRaySeg(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  x1: number,
  y1: number,
  x2: number,
  y2: number,
  lw: number,
  color: string
) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = color;
  ctx.lineWidth = lw;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
  ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
  ctx.stroke();
}

function createEyeTexture(sex: GuppySex): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const size = 128;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = size;
  canvas.height = size;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const cx = size / 2;
  const cy = size / 2;

  ctx.fillStyle = '#cbd5e1';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);

  const irisGrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 12, cx, cy, 56);
  irisGrad.addColorStop(0, sex === 'erkek' ? '#fef08a' : '#f1f5f9');
  irisGrad.addColorStop(0.6, sex === 'erkek' ? '#eab308' : '#94a3b8');
  irisGrad.addColorStop(1, '#1e293b');
  ctx.fillStyle = irisGrad;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx, cy, 56, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = '#050505';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx, cy, 28, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(cx - 10, cy - 10, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

function buildAnatomicalBodyGeometry(sex: GuppySex): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  interface CrossSection {
    z: number;
    rx: number;
    ryTop: number;
    ryBot: number;
    yCenter: number;
  }

  const sections: CrossSection[] =
    sex === 'erkek'
      ? [
          { z: 0.230, rx: 0.008, ryTop: 0.006, ryBot: 0.010, yCenter: 0.018 },
          { z: 0.215, rx: 0.022, ryTop: 0.018, ryBot: 0.022, yCenter: 0.014 },
          { z: 0.180, rx: 0.036, ryTop: 0.032, ryBot: 0.034, yCenter: 0.008 },
          { z: 0.135, rx: 0.044, ryTop: 0.042, ryBot: 0.044, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: 0.080, rx: 0.042, ryTop: 0.048, ryBot: 0.048, yCenter: 0.000 },
          { z: 0.010, rx: 0.038, ryTop: 0.050, ryBot: 0.046, yCenter: 0.000 },
          { z: -0.065, rx: 0.030, ryTop: 0.042, ryBot: 0.036, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.145, rx: 0.020, ryTop: 0.030, ryBot: 0.026, yCenter: 0.003 },
          { z: -0.220, rx: 0.012, ryTop: 0.024, ryBot: 0.022, yCenter: 0.003 },
          { z: -0.230, rx: 0.003, ryTop: 0.018, ryBot: 0.018, yCenter: 0.003 },
        ]
      : [
          { z: 0.230, rx: 0.009, ryTop: 0.007, ryBot: 0.011, yCenter: 0.020 },
          { z: 0.215, rx: 0.024, ryTop: 0.020, ryBot: 0.024, yCenter: 0.015 },
          { z: 0.175, rx: 0.040, ryTop: 0.036, ryBot: 0.040, yCenter: 0.008 },
          { z: 0.130, rx: 0.048, ryTop: 0.046, ryBot: 0.052, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: 0.065, rx: 0.054, ryTop: 0.054, ryBot: 0.068, yCenter: -0.004 },
          { z: -0.010, rx: 0.056, ryTop: 0.055, ryBot: 0.074, yCenter: -0.006 },
          { z: -0.075, rx: 0.042, ryTop: 0.046, ryBot: 0.056, yCenter: -0.002 },
          { z: -0.145, rx: 0.024, ryTop: 0.032, ryBot: 0.030, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.220, rx: 0.014, ryTop: 0.026, ryBot: 0.024, yCenter: 0.002 },
          { z: -0.230, rx: 0.003, ryTop: 0.020, ryBot: 0.020, yCenter: 0.002 },
        ];

  const radialSegments = 24;
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const zMin = -0.23;
  const zMax = 0.23;

  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length; s++) {
    const sec = sections[s];
    const u = (zMax - sec.z) / (zMax - zMin);

    for (let r = 0; r <= radialSegments; r++) {
      const theta = (r / radialSegments) * Math.PI * 2;
      const sinT = Math.sin(theta);
      const cosT = Math.cos(theta);

      const x = sinT * sec.rx;
      const ry = cosT >= 0 ? sec.ryTop : sec.ryBot;
      const y = sec.yCenter + cosT * ry;
      const v = 0.5 - cosT * 0.5;

      positions.push(x, y, sec.z);
      uvs.push(u, v);
    }
  }

  const ringStride = radialSegments + 1;
  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length - 1; s++) {
    for (let r = 0; r < radialSegments; r++) {
      const a = s * ringStride + r;
      const b = (s + 1) * ringStride + r;
      const c = (s + 1) * ringStride + (r + 1);
      const d = s * ringStride + (r + 1);

      indices.push(a, b, d);
      indices.push(b, c, d);
    }
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

/**
 * Kuyruk Varyantlarına Göre Yüzgeç Geometrisi:
 * - delta: Geniş üçgen yelpaze kuyruk
 * - double-sword: Üst ve alt kenarlarından ince kılıç uzantılı (Double Swordtail) kuyruk
 * - lyra: Üst ve alt uçları çatal gibi uzayan, ortası oyuk Lira (Lyretail) kuyruk
 * - round: Dişi yuvarlak kuyruk
 */
function buildFinsGeometry(sex: GuppySex, tailVariant: GuppyTailVariant): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const addFinRibbon = (
    rootPoints: THREE.Vector3[],
    tipPoints: THREE.Vector3[],
    uSteps = 6
  ) => {
    const vSteps = rootPoints.length;
    const baseVertexIndex = positions.length / 3;

    for (let ui = 0; ui <= uSteps; ui++) {
      const u = ui / uSteps;
      for (let vi = 0; vi < vSteps; vi++) {
        const v = vi / Math.max(1, vSteps - 1);
        const p = new THREE.Vector3().lerpVectors(rootPoints[vi], tipPoints[vi], u);
        positions.push(p.x, p.y, p.z);
        uvs.push(u, v);
      }
    }

    for (let ui = 0; ui < uSteps; ui++) {
      for (let vi = 0; vi < vSteps - 1; vi++) {
        const a = baseVertexIndex + ui * vSteps + vi;
        const b = baseVertexIndex + (ui + 1) * vSteps + vi;
        const c = baseVertexIndex + (ui + 1) * vSteps + (vi + 1);
        const d = baseVertexIndex + ui * vSteps + (vi + 1);

        indices.push(a, b, d);
        indices.push(b, c, d);
      }
    }
  };

  // A. Kuyruk Yüzgeci (Caudal Fin)
  if (sex === 'disi' || tailVariant === 'round') {
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.024, -0.22),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.008, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.008, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.024, -0.22),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.068, -0.34),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.026, -0.365),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.026, -0.365),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.068, -0.34),
      ],
      6
    );
  } else if (tailVariant === 'double-sword') {
    // Çift Kılıç (Double Swordtail): Üst ve alt kılıç uzantıları + ortada yuvarlak zar
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.024, -0.22),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.016, -0.222),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.000, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.016, -0.222),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.024, -0.22),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.115, -0.52), // Üst kılıç ucu
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.048, -0.37),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.000, -0.38),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.048, -0.37),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.115, -0.52), // Alt kılıç ucu
      ],
      9
    );
  } else if (tailVariant === 'lyra') {
    // Lira Kuyruk (Lyretail): Üst ve alt uçlar dışa kavisli uzar, orta içbükey oyuk kalır
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.024, -0.22),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.012, -0.223),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.000, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.012, -0.223),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.024, -0.22),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.155, -0.49),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.065, -0.37),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.000, -0.34), // Orta içbükey oyuk
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.065, -0.37),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.155, -0.49),
      ],
      9
    );
  } else {
    // Delta / Triangle Yelpaze Kuyruk
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.022, -0.22),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.008, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.008, -0.225),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.022, -0.22),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.135, -0.43),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.050, -0.46),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.045, -0.46),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.130, -0.43),
      ],
      8
    );
  }

  // B. Sırt Yüzgeci (Dorsal Fin)
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    const dorsalHeight = tailVariant === 'lyra' || tailVariant === 'double-sword' ? 0.135 : 0.118;
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.048, 0.02),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.045, -0.025),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.038, -0.07),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, dorsalHeight, -0.05),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, dorsalHeight + 0.012, -0.12),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, dorsalHeight - 0.02, -0.19),
      ],
      5
    );
  } else {
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.054, 0.00),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.050, -0.035),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.044, -0.07),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.092, -0.03),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.095, -0.07),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.076, -0.105),
      ],
      4
    );
  }

  // C. Göğüs Yüzgeçleri (Pectoral Fins)
  const pecY = sex === 'erkek' ? -0.015 : -0.022;
  const pecX = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.040 : 0.046;
  const pecSpan = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.088 : 0.076;

  addFinRibbon(
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY + 0.012, 0.12),
      new THREE.Vector3(pecX, pecY - 0.012, 0.11),
    ],
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan, pecY + 0.005, 0.04),
      new THREE.Vector3(pecX + pecSpan * 0.9, pecY - 0.035, 0.03),
    ],
    4
  );
  addFinRibbon(
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY + 0.012, 0.12),
      new THREE.Vector3(-pecX, pecY - 0.012, 0.11),
    ],
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan, pecY + 0.005, 0.04),
      new THREE.Vector3(-pecX - pecSpan * 0.9, pecY - 0.035, 0.03),
    ],
    4
  );

  // D. Karın Yüzgeçleri (Pelvic Fins)
  const pelvY = sex === 'erkek' ? -0.045 : -0.068;
  addFinRibbon(
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(0.016, pelvY, 0.04),
      new THREE.Vector3(0.014, pelvY, 0.015),
    ],
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(0.034, pelvY - 0.038, -0.01),
      new THREE.Vector3(0.030, pelvY - 0.042, -0.035),
    ],
    3
  );
  addFinRibbon(
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(-0.016, pelvY, 0.04),
      new THREE.Vector3(-0.014, pelvY, 0.015),
    ],
    [
      new THREE.Vector3(-0.034, pelvY - 0.038, -0.01),
      new THREE.Vector3(-0.030, pelvY - 0.042, -0.035),
    ],
    3
  );

  // E. Erkek Gonopodium / Dişi Anal Yüzgeç
  if (sex === 'erkek') {
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.042, -0.025),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.040, -0.038),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.088, -0.125),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.084, -0.132),
      ],
      4
    );
  } else {
    addFinRibbon(
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.062, -0.055),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.052, -0.095),
      ],
      [
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.105, -0.085),
        new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.092, -0.125),
      ],
      4
    );
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

function buildEyesGeometry(sex: GuppySex): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const eyeRadius = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.016 : 0.018;
  const eyeX = sex === 'erkek' ? 0.028 : 0.031;
  const eyeY = 0.014;
  const eyeZ = 0.172;

  const leftEye = new THREE.SphereGeometry(eyeRadius, 14, 12);
  leftEye.rotateY(Math.PI / 2);
  leftEye.translate(eyeX, eyeY, eyeZ);

  const rightEye = new THREE.SphereGeometry(eyeRadius, 14, 12);
  rightEye.rotateY(-Math.PI / 2);
  rightEye.translate(-eyeX, eyeY, eyeZ);

  const nonIdxLeft = leftEye.toNonIndexed();
  const nonIdxRight = rightEye.toNonIndexed();

  const totalVerts = nonIdxLeft.attributes.position.count + nonIdxRight.attributes.position.count;
  const positions = new Float32Array(totalVerts * 3);
  const normals = new Float32Array(totalVerts * 3);
  const uvs = new Float32Array(totalVerts * 2);

  positions.set(nonIdxLeft.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, 0);
  positions.set(nonIdxRight.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, nonIdxLeft.attributes.position.count * 3);

  normals.set(nonIdxLeft.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, 0);
  normals.set(nonIdxRight.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, nonIdxLeft.attributes.normal.count * 3);

  uvs.set(nonIdxLeft.attributes.uv.array as Float32Array, 0);
  uvs.set(nonIdxRight.attributes.uv.array as Float32Array, nonIdxLeft.attributes.uv.count * 2);

  const merged = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  merged.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  merged.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(normals, 3));
  merged.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  return merged;
}

function applySwimVertexShader(
  material: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial,
  timeUniform: { value: number },
  finMultiplier: number
) {
  material.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.uTime = timeUniform;
    shader.uniforms.uFinAmp = { value: finMultiplier };

    shader.vertexShader =
      `
      uniform float uTime;
      uniform float uFinAmp;
      ` + shader.vertexShader;

    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
      '#include <begin_vertex>',
      `
      #include <begin_vertex>
      float normalizedBack = clamp((0.20 - transformed.z) / 0.65, 0.0, 1.4);
      float spineWave = sin(uTime * 8.5 + transformed.z * 14.0) * pow(normalizedBack, 1.7) * 0.042 * uFinAmp;
      transformed.x += spineWave;

      float pectoralMask = smoothstep(0.042, 0.11, abs(transformed.x));
      transformed.y += sin(uTime * 11.0 + transformed.x * 20.0) * pectoralMask * 0.018;
      `
    );
  };
}

export function buildAnatomicalGuppyAssets(
  sex: GuppySex,
  tailVariant: GuppyTailVariant = sex === 'erkek' ? 'delta' : 'round'
): ProceduralFishAssets {
  const timeUniform = { value: 0 };

  const bodyGeometry = buildAnatomicalBodyGeometry(sex);
  const { diffuseMap, bumpMap, roughnessMap } = createGuppySkinTextures(sex);

  const bodyMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff', // instanceColor ile tam uyumlu beyaz taban
    map: diffuseMap,
    bumpMap: bumpMap,
    bumpScale: 0.018,
    roughnessMap: roughnessMap,
    roughness: 0.35,
    metalness: 0.14,
    iridescence: sex === 'erkek' ? 0.85 : 0.45,
    iridescenceIOR: 1.38,
    iridescenceThicknessRange: [180, 480],
    clearcoat: 0.65,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.15,
  });
  applySwimVertexShader(bodyMaterial, timeUniform, 1.0);

  const finGeometry = buildFinsGeometry(sex, tailVariant);
  const { finDiffuse, finAlpha, finBump } = createFinTextures(sex);

  const finMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff', // instanceColor ile tam uyumlu beyaz taban
    map: finDiffuse,
    alphaMap: finAlpha,
    bumpMap: finBump,
    bumpScale: 0.012,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: sex === 'erkek' ? 0.92 : 0.78,
    alphaTest: 0.08,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    roughness: 0.28,
    metalness: 0.08,
    iridescence: sex === 'erkek' ? 0.65 : 0.25,
    depthWrite: false,
  });
  applySwimVertexShader(finMaterial, timeUniform, 1.45);

  const eyeGeometry = buildEyesGeometry(sex);
  const eyeTexture = createEyeTexture(sex);

  const eyeMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    map: eyeTexture,
    roughness: 0.08,
    metalness: 0.1,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
  });
  applySwimVertexShader(eyeMaterial, timeUniform, 0.2);

  return {
    bodyGeometry,
    bodyMaterial,
    finGeometry,
    finMaterial,
    eyeGeometry,
    eyeMaterial,
    updateShaderTime: (time: number) => {
      timeUniform.value = time;
    },
  };
}

import { buildAnatomicalGuppyAssets, ProceduralFishAssets } from './lepistesAnatomyBuilder';

  return buildAnatomicalGuppyAssets('disi');
}

export function createLepistesDisiGeometry() {
  const assets = buildAnatomicalGuppyAssets('disi');
  return {
    geometry: assets.bodyGeometry,
    material: assets.bodyMaterial,
  };
}

export default createAnatomicalLepistesDisi;

========================================
DOSYA: models\baliklar\vatoz\vatozAnatomyBuilder.ts

import * as THREE from 'three';

export type PlecoVariant = 'cuce' | 'leopar';

export interface ProceduralPlecoAssets {
  bodyGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  bodyMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  finGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  finMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  eyeGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  eyeMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  updateShaderTime: (time: number) => void;
}

function createPlecoSkinTexture(variant: PlecoVariant): {
  diffuseMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
  bumpMap: THREE.CanvasTexture;
} {
  const width = 512;
  const height = 256;

  const dCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  dCanvas.width = width;
  dCanvas.height = height;
  const dCtx = dCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  const bCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  bCanvas.width = width;
  bCanvas.height = height;
  const bCtx = bCanvas.getContext('2d')!;

  // Açık taban (instanceColor ile koyu kahve, altın-albino veya leopar rengini alır)
  dCtx.fillStyle = '#e2e8f0';
  dCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  bCtx.fillStyle = '#505050';
  bCtx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  // Zırhlı kemik plakaları (Scutes) ve benek desenleri
  const spotCount = variant === 'leopar' ? 140 : 95;
  for (let i = 0; i < spotCount; i++) {
    const x = ((i * 73) % (width - 20)) + 10;
    const y = ((i * 41) % (height - 20)) + 10;
    const r = variant === 'leopar' ? 6 + (i % 5) : 3.5 + (i % 3);

    dCtx.fillStyle = variant === 'leopar' ? 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.62)' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.55)';
    dCtx.beginPath();
    dCtx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    dCtx.fill();

    bCtx.fillStyle = '#d4d4d4';
    bCtx.beginPath();
    bCtx.arc(x, y, r * 0.9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    bCtx.fill();
  }

  const diffuseMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(dCanvas);
  diffuseMap.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  diffuseMap.needsUpdate = true;

  const bumpMap = new THREE.CanvasTexture(bCanvas);
  bumpMap.needsUpdate = true;

  return { diffuseMap, bumpMap };
}

function buildPlecoBodyGeometry(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  // Karın kısmı düz (vantuzla cama/kuma yapışabilmesi için ryBot küçük), sırtı kubbeli zırhlı kesit
  const sections = [
    { z: 0.23, rx: 0.018, ryTop: 0.012, ryBot: 0.002, y: 0.002 }, // Geniş yuvarlak burun
    { z: 0.18, rx: 0.048, ryTop: 0.028, ryBot: 0.004, y: 0.004 }, // Geniş kafa & vantuz ağız
    { z: 0.11, rx: 0.056, ryTop: 0.038, ryBot: 0.005, y: 0.005 }, // Omuz genişliği
    { z: 0.03, rx: 0.050, ryTop: 0.040, ryBot: 0.005, y: 0.005 }, // Gövde ortası
    { z: -0.06, rx: 0.036, ryTop: 0.030, ryBot: 0.004, y: 0.004 },
    { z: -0.15, rx: 0.022, ryTop: 0.020, ryBot: 0.004, y: 0.004 },
    { z: -0.23, rx: 0.010, ryTop: 0.016, ryBot: 0.006, y: 0.006 }, // Kuyruk sapı
  ];

  const radialSegments = 22;
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length; s++) {
    const sec = sections[s];
    const u = s / (sections.length - 1);
    for (let r = 0; r <= radialSegments; r++) {
      const theta = (r / radialSegments) * Math.PI * 2;
      const sinT = Math.sin(theta);
      const cosT = Math.cos(theta);
      const x = sinT * sec.rx;
      const ry = cosT >= 0 ? sec.ryTop : sec.ryBot;
      const y = sec.y + cosT * ry;
      positions.push(x, y, sec.z);
      uvs.push(u, r / radialSegments);
    }
  }

  const stride = radialSegments + 1;
  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length - 1; s++) {
    for (let r = 0; r < radialSegments; r++) {
      const a = s * stride + r;
      const b = (s + 1) * stride + r;
      const c = (s + 1) * stride + (r + 1);
      const d = s * stride + (r + 1);
      indices.push(a, b, d);
      indices.push(b, c, d);
    }
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

function buildPlecoFinsGeometry(variant: PlecoVariant): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const addRibbon = (p0: THREE.Vector3, p1: THREE.Vector3, p2: THREE.Vector3, p3: THREE.Vector3) => {
    const base = positions.length / 3;
    [p0, p1, p2, p3].forEach((p, idx) => {
      positions.push(p.x, p.y, p.z);
      uvs.push(idx < 2 ? 0 : 1, idx % 2);
    });
    indices.push(base, base + 2, base + 1);
    indices.push(base + 2, base + 3, base + 1);
  };

  const sailBoost = variant === 'leopar' ? 1.35 : 1.0;

  // 1. Yelken Sırt Yüzgeci (Dorsal Fin)
  addRibbon(
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.038, 0.05),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.024, -0.10),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.125 * sailBoost, 0.01),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.105 * sailBoost, -0.15)
  );

  // 2. Güçlü Kuyruk Yüzgeci (Caudal Fin)
  addRibbon(
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.022, -0.225),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.002, -0.225),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.068, -0.35),
    new THREE.Vector3(0, -0.045, -0.36)
  );

  // 3. Geniş Yatay Göğüs Yüzgeçleri (Pectoral Fins - Tabana yayılır)
  [-1, 1].forEach((side) => {
    addRibbon(
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.052, 0.006, 0.12),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.048, 0.005, 0.05),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.135, 0.004, 0.05),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.115, 0.003, -0.03)
    );
    // Karın yüzgeçleri
    addRibbon(
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.036, 0.004, -0.01),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.030, 0.004, -0.06),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.095, 0.003, -0.06),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.080, 0.003, -0.12)
    );
  });

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

function buildPlecoEyesGeometry(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const left = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.011, 12, 10);
  left.translate(0.034, 0.026, 0.155);
  const right = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.011, 12, 10);
  right.translate(-0.034, 0.026, 0.155);

  const lNon = left.toNonIndexed();
  const rNon = right.toNonIndexed();
  const count = lNon.attributes.position.count + rNon.attributes.position.count;
  const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  const norm = new Float32Array(count * 3);

  pos.set(lNon.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, 0);
  pos.set(rNon.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, lNon.attributes.position.count * 3);
  norm.set(lNon.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, 0);
  norm.set(rNon.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, lNon.attributes.normal.count * 3);

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(norm, 3));
  return geo;
}

export function buildAnatomicalPlecoAssets(
  variant: PlecoVariant = 'cuce'
): ProceduralPlecoAssets {
  const timeUniform = { value: 0 };
  const bodyGeometry = buildPlecoBodyGeometry();
  const { diffuseMap, bumpMap } = createPlecoSkinTexture(variant);

  const bodyMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff',
    map: diffuseMap,
    bumpMap,
    bumpScale: 0.025,
    roughness: 0.45,
    metalness: 0.08,
  });

  const finGeometry = buildPlecoFinsGeometry(variant);
  const finMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff',
    map: diffuseMap,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.88,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    roughness: 0.35,
  });

  // Vatoz kuyruk ve göğüs yüzgeci hafif dalgalanma shader'ı
  const applyWave = (mat: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial) => {
    mat.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
      shader.uniforms.uTime = timeUniform;
      shader.vertexShader = `uniform float uTime;\n` + shader.vertexShader;
      shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
        '#include <begin_vertex>',
        `
        #include <begin_vertex>
        float tailMask = smoothstep(-0.10, -0.35, transformed.z);
        transformed.x += sin(uTime * 4.5 + transformed.z * 12.0) * tailMask * 0.025;
        `
      );
    };
  };
  applyWave(bodyMaterial);
  applyWave(finMaterial);

  const eyeGeometry = buildPlecoEyesGeometry();
  const eyeMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#0f172a',
    roughness: 0.1,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
  });

  return {
    bodyGeometry,
    bodyMaterial,
    finGeometry,
    finMaterial,
    eyeGeometry,
    eyeMaterial,
    updateShaderTime: (t: number) => {
      timeUniform.value = t;
    },
  };
}

import * as THREE from 'three';

export function createAnubiasGeometry(): { geometry: THREE.BufferGeometry; material: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial } {
  const geometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.1, 0.2, 1.5, 6);
  // Kök tabanını (alt yüzeyi) tam Y = 0 seviyesine taşıyarak sandTopY'ye kusursuz oturmasını sağla
  geometry.translate(0, 0.75, 0);

  const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
    color: '#2b8a3e',
    roughness: 0.5,
  });

  return { geometry, material };
}


========================================
DOSYA: models\canlilar\karides\karidesAnatomyBuilder.ts
