import * as THREE from 'three';

export type ShrimpVariant = 'neocaridina' | 'rilli' | 'amano';

export interface ProceduralShrimpAssets {
  bodyGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  bodyMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  appendageGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  appendageMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  eyeGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  eyeMaterial: THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial;
  updateShaderTime: (time: number) => void;
}

function createShrimpShellTexture(variant: ShrimpVariant): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const width = 256;
  const height = 128;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = width;
  canvas.height = height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  // Nötr beyaz-sedef taban (instanceColor ile çarpılır)
  const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
  grad.addColorStop(0.0, '#f8fafc');
  grad.addColorStop(0.45, '#ffffff');
  grad.addColorStop(1.0, '#e2e8f0');
  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  // Rilli varyantında orta segmentlerde şeffaf bant efekti
  if (variant === 'rilli') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(203, 213, 225, 0.35)';
    ctx.fillRect(width * 0.38, 0, width * 0.28, height);
  }

  // Amano varyantında yan çizgi benekleri
  if (variant === 'amano') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(71, 85, 105, 0.55)';
    for (let x = width * 0.30; x < width * 0.85; x += 14) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(x, height * 0.52, 4, 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }

  // Kabuk segment çizgileri (Carapace & Abdomen eklemleri)
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.28)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  const segments = [0.34, 0.46, 0.58, 0.70, 0.82];
  segments.forEach((u) => {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(width * u, 0);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(width * (u + 0.03), height * 0.5, width * u, height);
    ctx.stroke();
  });

  // Sırt yarış çizgisi (Racing stripe)
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.42)';
  ctx.fillRect(width * 0.15, height * 0.45, width * 0.75, height * 0.10);

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

function buildShrimpBodyGeometry(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  // Karidesin karakteristik kambur/kavisli gövde kesitleri (+Z baş/rostrum, -Z telson kuyruk)
  const sections = [
    { z: 0.24, rx: 0.002, ry: 0.003, y: 0.025 }, // Rostrum sivri uç
    { z: 0.19, rx: 0.018, ry: 0.020, y: 0.022 }, // Ön kafa zırhı
    { z: 0.12, rx: 0.028, ry: 0.032, y: 0.024 }, // Carapace orta
    { z: 0.04, rx: 0.030, ry: 0.034, y: 0.028 }, // Abdomen 1 (kambur tepe)
    { z: -0.04, rx: 0.026, ry: 0.030, y: 0.024 }, // Abdomen 2
    { z: -0.11, rx: 0.020, ry: 0.023, y: 0.012 }, // Abdomen 3 (aşağı kıvrım)
    { z: -0.17, rx: 0.014, ry: 0.016, y: -0.004 }, // Abdomen 4
    { z: -0.22, rx: 0.008, ry: 0.010, y: -0.016 }, // Kuyruk sapı
  ];

  const radialSegments = 16;
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length; s++) {
    const sec = sections[s];
    const u = s / (sections.length - 1);
    for (let r = 0; r <= radialSegments; r++) {
      const theta = (r / radialSegments) * Math.PI * 2;
      const x = Math.sin(theta) * sec.rx;
      const y = sec.y + Math.cos(theta) * sec.ry;
      positions.push(x, y, sec.z);
      uvs.push(u, r / radialSegments);
    }
  }

  const stride = radialSegments + 1;
  for (let s = 0; s < sections.length - 1; s++) {
    for (let r = 0; r < radialSegments; r++) {
      const a = s * stride + r;
      const b = (s + 1) * stride + r;
      const c = (s + 1) * stride + (r + 1);
      const d = s * stride + (r + 1);
      indices.push(a, b, d);
      indices.push(b, c, d);
    }
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

function buildShrimpAppendagesGeometry(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const positions: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const addRibbon = (p0: THREE.Vector3, p1: THREE.Vector3, p2: THREE.Vector3, p3: THREE.Vector3) => {
    const base = positions.length / 3;
    [p0, p1, p2, p3].forEach((p, idx) => {
      positions.push(p.x, p.y, p.z);
      uvs.push(idx < 2 ? 0 : 1, idx % 2);
    });
    indices.push(base, base + 2, base + 1);
    indices.push(base + 2, base + 3, base + 1);
  };

  // 1. Uzun İki Çift Duyarga (Antennae)
  [-1, 1].forEach((side) => {
    addRibbon(
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.008, 0.024, 0.21),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.011, 0.021, 0.21),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.075, 0.095, 0.42),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.078, 0.092, 0.42)
    );
    addRibbon(
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.006, 0.020, 0.21),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.008, 0.017, 0.21),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.055, 0.035, 0.36),
      new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.057, 0.032, 0.36)
    );
  });

  // 2. 5 Çift Yürüyüş Bacağı (Pereiopods)
  const legZs = [0.15, 0.11, 0.07, 0.03, -0.01];
  legZs.forEach((zPos, i) => {
    [-1, 1].forEach((side) => {
      const spread = 0.032 + (i % 2) * 0.006;
      addRibbon(
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.016, 0.004, zPos + 0.004),
        new THREE.Vector3(side * 0.016, 0.004, zPos - 0.004),
        new THREE.Vector3(side * spread, -0.036, zPos + 0.012),
        new THREE.Vector3(side * spread, -0.036, zPos + 0.004)
      );
    });
  });

  // 3. Yelpaze Telson ve Uropod Kuyruk
  addRibbon(
    new THREE.Vector3(-0.008, -0.015, -0.215),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.008, -0.015, -0.215),
    new THREE.Vector3(-0.036, -0.032, -0.285),
    new THREE.Vector3(0.036, -0.032, -0.285)
  );

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  geo.computeVertexNormals();
  return geo;
}

function buildShrimpEyesGeometry(): THREE.BufferGeometry {
  const left = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.008, 10, 8);
  left.translate(0.015, 0.028, 0.195);
  const right = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.008, 10, 8);
  right.translate(-0.015, 0.028, 0.195);

  const lNon = left.toNonIndexed();
  const rNon = right.toNonIndexed();
  const count = lNon.attributes.position.count + rNon.attributes.position.count;
  const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  const norm = new Float32Array(count * 3);

  pos.set(lNon.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, 0);
  pos.set(rNon.attributes.position.array as Float32Array, lNon.attributes.position.count * 3);
  norm.set(lNon.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, 0);
  norm.set(rNon.attributes.normal.array as Float32Array, lNon.attributes.normal.count * 3);

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(norm, 3));
  return geo;
}

export function buildAnatomicalShrimpAssets(
  variant: ShrimpVariant = 'neocaridina'
): ProceduralShrimpAssets {
  const timeUniform = { value: 0 };
  const isTranslucent = variant === 'amano' || variant === 'rilli';

  const bodyGeometry = buildShrimpBodyGeometry();
  const shellTex = createShrimpShellTexture(variant);

  const bodyMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff',
    map: shellTex,
    roughness: 0.25,
    metalness: 0.05,
    transparent: isTranslucent,
    opacity: variant === 'amano' ? 0.68 : variant === 'rilli' ? 0.85 : 0.96,
    clearcoat: 0.8,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.12,
  });

  const appendageGeometry = buildShrimpAppendagesGeometry();
  const appendageMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#ffffff',
    roughness: 0.35,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.82,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide,
  });

  // Bacak yürüme ve duyarga titreşim shader'ı
  appendageMaterial.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.uTime = timeUniform;
    shader.vertexShader = `uniform float uTime;\n` + shader.vertexShader;
    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
      '#include <begin_vertex>',
      `
      #include <begin_vertex>
      if (transformed.y < 0.0) {
        transformed.z += sin(uTime * 16.0 + transformed.z * 80.0 + transformed.x * 40.0) * 0.012;
      } else if (transformed.z > 0.22) {
        transformed.x += sin(uTime * 9.0 + transformed.z * 15.0) * 0.014;
      }
      `
    );
  };

  const eyeGeometry = buildShrimpEyesGeometry();
  const eyeMaterial = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
    color: '#09090b',
    roughness: 0.1,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
  });

  return {
    bodyGeometry,
    bodyMaterial,
    appendageGeometry,
    appendageMaterial,
    eyeGeometry,
    eyeMaterial,
    updateShaderTime: (t: number) => {
      timeUniform.value = t;
    },
  };
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\canlilar\salyangoz\salyangozAnatomyBuilder.ts

import * as THREE from 'three';

export type SnailSpeciesType = 'elma' | 'nerite';

export interface SnailAnatomyGeometries {
  shellGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
  bodyGeometry: THREE.BufferGeometry;
}

export function buildSnailGeometries(
  species: SnailSpeciesType = 'elma'
): SnailAnatomyGeometries {
  const isNerite = species === 'nerite';

  // 1. KABUK GEOMETRİSİ (Logaritmik Sarmal + Vertex Renk Deseni)
  const turns = isNerite ? 2.1 : 2.8;
  const uSteps = 56;
  const vSteps = 24;

  const positions: number[] = [];
  const colors: number[] = [];
  const uvs: number[] = [];
  const indices: number[] = [];

  const baseColor = new THREE.Color(isNerite ? '#d97706' : '#f59e0b');
  const stripeColor = new THREE.Color(isNerite ? '#18181b' : '#b45309');
  const tmpColor = new THREE.Color();

  for (let i = 0; i <= uSteps; i++) {
    const u = i / uSteps;
    const theta = u * turns * Math.PI * 2;
    const growth = Math.pow(u, isNerite ? 0.72 : 0.85);
    const tubeRadius = (isNerite ? 0.011 : 0.014) * growth + 0.0015;
    const spiralRadius = (isNerite ? 0.008 : 0.012) * growth;
    const spireOffset = isNerite
      ? (1 - u) * 0.004
      : (1 - u) * 0.011;

    for (let j = 0; j <= vSteps; j++) {
      const v = j / vSteps;
      const phi = v * Math.PI * 2;

      const localR = spiralRadius + tubeRadius * Math.cos(phi);
      const x = localR * Math.cos(theta);
      const z = localR * Math.sin(theta);
      const y = tubeRadius * Math.sin(phi) + spireOffset + 0.008;

      positions.push(x, y, z);
      uvs.push(u, v);

      if (isNerite) {
        // Zebra Nerite karakteristik siyah-kehribar zikzak çizgiler
        const stripeWave = Math.sin(theta * 5.0 + Math.sin(phi * 2.0) * 0.9);
        const factor = stripeWave > 0.05 ? 1.0 : 0.0;
        tmpColor.copy(baseColor).lerp(stripeColor, factor);
      } else {
        // Elma salyangozu yumuşak büyüme halkaları
        const band = (Math.sin(theta * 3.0) + 1) * 0.12;
        tmpColor.copy(baseColor).lerp(stripeColor, band);
      }

      colors.push(tmpColor.r, tmpColor.g, tmpColor.b);
    }
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < uSteps; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < vSteps; j++) {
      const a = i * (vSteps + 1) + j;
      const b = (i + 1) * (vSteps + 1) + j;
      const c = (i + 1) * (vSteps + 1) + (j + 1);
      const d = i * (vSteps + 1) + (j + 1);

      indices.push(a, b, d);
      indices.push(b, c, d);
    }
  }

  const shellGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  shellGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  shellGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
  shellGeometry.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  shellGeometry.setIndex(indices);
  shellGeometry.computeVertexNormals();

  // 2. AYAK / GÖVDE + ANTENLER
  const bodyGeo = new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(
    isNerite ? 0.006 : 0.0075,
    isNerite ? 0.014 : 0.022,
    8,
    14
  );
  bodyGeo.rotateX(Math.PI / 2);
  bodyGeo.scale(1.15, 0.45, 1.0);
  bodyGeo.translate(0, 0.002, 0.002);

  return {
    shellGeometry,
    bodyGeometry: bodyGeo,
  };
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\kumlar\silis-kum\SilisKumMesh.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';

interface SilisKumMeshProps {
  tankWidth: number;
  tankDepth: number;
  tankHeight: number;
  totalSandKg: number;
  sandType?: 'silis' | 'hagen' | 'aktif-toprak';
}

export default function SilisKumMesh({
  tankWidth,
  tankDepth,
  tankHeight,
  totalSandKg,
  sandType = 'silis',
}: SilisKumMeshProps) {
  // Sahne ölçeği: 1 birim = 10 cm -> 1 litre = 1 birim³ -> 1 kg kum = 0.65 birim³
  const glassThickness = 0.04;
  const innerW = Math.max(0.1, tankWidth - glassThickness * 2);
  const innerD = Math.max(0.1, tankDepth - glassThickness * 2);

  const sandVolumeScene = Math.max(0, totalSandKg) * 0.65;
  const baseArea = innerW * innerD;
  const sandHeight = Math.min(tankHeight * 0.35, sandVolumeScene / baseArea);

  const geometry = useMemo(() => {
    const h = Math.max(0.01, sandHeight);
    const geo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(innerW, h, innerD, 32, 1, 24);
    const pos = geo.attributes.position;

    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
      const y = pos.getY(i);
      if (y > 0) {
        const x = pos.getX(i);
        const z = pos.getZ(i);
        const wave =
          Math.sin(x * 2.2) * Math.cos(z * 2.0) * 0.018 +
          Math.sin(x * 4.5 + z * 3.5) * 0.008;
        pos.setY(i, y + wave);
      }
    }
    geo.computeVertexNormals();
    return geo;
  }, [innerW, innerD, sandHeight]);

  const color = useMemo(() => {
    if (sandType === 'hagen') return '#1e2229';
    if (sandType === 'aktif-toprak') return '#3b281c';
    return '#e6dcc8';
  }, [sandType]);

  if (totalSandKg <= 0 || sandHeight <= 0.005) {
    return null;
  }

  const bottomInnerY = -tankHeight / 2 + glassThickness;

  return (
    <mesh
      geometry={geometry}
      position={[0, bottomInnerY + sandHeight / 2, 0]}
      receiveShadow
    >
      <meshStandardMaterial
        color={color}
        roughness={0.92}
        metalness={0.02}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\malzemeler\aydinlatma\baseLightFixture.ts
========================================
import * as THREE from 'three';

export interface SimpleLightFixtureProps {
  lengthCm: number;
  tankWidth: number;
  tankHeight: number;
  kelvin: number;
  ledStripColor: string;
  pointLightColor: string;
  lumen: number;
  lightHours: number;
  slotIndex?: number;
}

export function computeFixtureDimensions(
  lengthCm: number,
  tankWidth: number,
  tankHeight: number,
  slotIndex = 0
) {
  // 1 birim = 10 cm; armatür uzunluğu tank genişliğini aşarsa cam ayaklarıyla tanka oturur
  const rawLength = lengthCm / 10;
  const bodyLength = Math.min(rawLength, tankWidth + 0.2);
  const tankTopY = tankHeight / 2;
  const fixtureY = tankTopY + 0.14;
  // Birden fazla ışık eklenirse ön-arka eksende (Z) yan yana dizilir
  const zOffset =
    slotIndex === 0
      ? 0
      : (slotIndex % 2 === 1 ? 0.35 : -0.35) * Math.ceil(slotIndex / 2);

  return {
    bodyLength,
    tankTopY,
    fixtureY,
    zOffset,
  };
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { computeFixtureDimensions } from './baseLightFixture';

interface LightMeshProps {
  lengthCm: number;
  tankWidth: number;
  tankHeight: number;
  kelvin: number;
  lumen: number;
  lightHours: number;
  slotIndex?: number;
}

export default function BeyazIsikMesh({
  lengthCm,
  tankWidth,
  tankHeight,
  kelvin,
  lumen,
  lightHours,
  slotIndex = 0,
}: LightMeshProps) {
  const { bodyLength, tankTopY, fixtureY, zOffset } = computeFixtureDimensions(
    lengthCm,
    tankWidth,
    tankHeight,
    slotIndex
  );

  const isOn = lightHours > 0;
  const chipColor = kelvin >= 10000 ? '#e0f2fe' : '#fffbeb';
  const intensity = isOn ? Math.min(2.6, (lumen / 1500) * (lightHours / 8)) : 0;

  return (
    <group position={[0, 0, zOffset]}>
      {/* İnce Alüminyum Kasa */}
      <mesh position={[0, fixtureY, 0]} castShadow>
        <boxGeometry args={[bodyLength, 0.06, 0.24]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#cbd5e1" metalness={0.75} roughness={0.25} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Alt LED Şerit Paneli */}
      <mesh position={[0, fixtureY - 0.032, 0]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[bodyLength - 0.12, 0.012, 0.16]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          color={chipColor}
          emissive={chipColor}
          emissiveIntensity={isOn ? 1.8 : 0}
        />
      </mesh>

      {/* Sol & Sağ Cam Tutucu Ayaklar */}
      <mesh position={[-bodyLength / 2 + 0.04, tankTopY + 0.07, 0]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.04, 0.14, 0.22]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#334155" roughness={0.5} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh position={[bodyLength / 2 - 0.04, tankTopY + 0.07, 0]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.04, 0.14, 0.22]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#334155" roughness={0.5} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Suya Vuran Gerçek Işık Kaynağı */}
      {isOn && (
        <pointLight
          position={[0, fixtureY - 0.12, 0]}
          color={chipColor}
          intensity={intensity}
          distance={tankHeight + 2.5}
        />
      )}
    </group>
  );
}

'use client';

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { PlacedEquipment } from '../../../store/useAquariumStore';

function createSpongeTexture(): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const size = 128;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = size;
  canvas.height = size;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  ctx.fillStyle = '#18181b';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);

  for (let i = 0; i < 720; i++) {
    const x = (i * 47) % size;
    const y = (i * 83) % size;
    const r = (i % 2) + 0.8;
    ctx.fillStyle = i % 3 === 0 ? '#09090b' : '#27272a';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.repeat.set(3, 3);
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

interface FiltreEkipmanMeshProps {
  item: PlacedEquipment;
  waterSurfaceY: number;
  tankHeight?: number;
}

export default function FiltreEkipmanMesh({
  item,
  waterSurfaceY,
  tankHeight = 3.5,
}: FiltreEkipmanMeshProps) {
  const slug = (item.product.slug || '').toLowerCase();
  const name = (item.product.name || '').toLowerCase();
  const isSpongeFilter =
    item.product.needs_air_pump === true ||
    slug.includes('pipo') ||
    name.includes('pipo');

  const isDualSponge =
    slug.includes('cift') ||
    slug.includes('ikili') ||
    name.includes('çift') ||
    name.includes('ikili');

  const spongeTex = useMemo(() => createSpongeTexture(), []);
  const availableWaterH = Math.max(0.45, waterSurfaceY - item.position[1] - 0.08);

  // 1. PİPO FİLTRELER (Kum tabanına oturan, hava motoruyla çalışan)
  if (isSpongeFilter) {
    const riserHeight = Math.min(0.55, availableWaterH * 0.72);

    if (isDualSponge) {
      return (
        <group>
          {/* Ağır Taban Kaidesi */}
          <mesh position={[0, 0.024, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.24, 0.048, 0.14]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.6} />
          </mesh>

          {/* Merkez T-Bağlantı Gövdesi */}
          <mesh position={[0, 0.14, 0]} castShadow>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.032, 0.038, 0.22, 16]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.45} />
          </mesh>

          {/* Sol & Sağ Silindirik Biyolojik Süngerler */}
          {[-0.125, 0.125].map((sx, idx) => (
            <mesh key={idx} position={[sx, 0.15, 0]} castShadow>
              <cylinderGeometry args={[0.068, 0.068, 0.24, 22]} />
              <meshStandardMaterial
                map={spongeTex}
                bumpMap={spongeTex}
                bumpScale={0.015}
                roughness={0.95}
              />
            </mesh>
          ))}

          {/* Hava Hortumu Giriş Nipeli */}
          <mesh position={[0, 0.24, 0.032]} rotation={[Math.PI / 3, 0, 0]}>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.012, 0.014, 0.05, 10]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.3} />
          </mesh>

          {/* Şeffaf Yükseltici (Riser) Su Çıkış Borusu */}
          <mesh position={[0, 0.24 + riserHeight / 2, 0]}>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.026, 0.026, riserHeight, 16]} />
            <meshPhysicalMaterial
              color="#e2e8f0"
              transmission={0.72}
              transparent
              opacity={0.75}
              roughness={0.15}
            />
          </mesh>
        </group>
      );
    }

    // Tek Süngerli Klasik Pipo Filtre
    const singleRiserHeight = Math.min(0.48, availableWaterH * 0.65);
    return (
      <group>
        {/* Yuvarlak Taban Ağırlığı */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.022, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.125, 0.135, 0.044, 24]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.65} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Silindirik Biyolojik Sünger */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.19, 0]} castShadow>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.105, 0.105, 0.30, 24]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            map={spongeTex}
            bumpMap={spongeTex}
            bumpScale={0.018}
            roughness={0.95}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Üst Plastik Kapak & Hava Hortumu Dirseği */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.35, 0]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.065, 0.095, 0.035, 20]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.45} />
        </mesh>
        <mesh position={[0.045, 0.36, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, -Math.PI / 4]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.012, 0.014, 0.05, 10]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.3} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Şeffaf Yükseltici (Riser) Boru */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.36 + singleRiserHeight / 2, 0]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.028, 0.028, singleRiserHeight, 16]} />
          <meshPhysicalMaterial
            color="#e2e8f0"
            transmission={0.72}
            transparent
            opacity={0.75}
            roughness={0.15}
          />
        </mesh>
      </group>
    );
  }

  // 2. MOTORLU İÇ FİLTRE (Akvaryum Camına Vantuzlarla Asılı Yüksek Detaylı Model)
  const totalH = 0.88;
  const spongePartH = 0.50;
  const motorPartH = 0.38;

  // Üst cam kenarından dışarı taşan güç kablosu geometrisi
  const powerCordGeo = useMemo(() => {
    const rimOffsetY = Math.max(0.25, tankHeight / 2 - item.position[1] + 0.04);
    const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
      new THREE.Vector3(0, totalH - 0.03, -0.04),
      new THREE.Vector3(0, rimOffsetY + 0.03, -0.07),
      new THREE.Vector3(0, rimOffsetY + 0.05, -0.14),
      new THREE.Vector3(0.05, rimOffsetY - 0.35, -0.18),
    ]);
    return new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 24, 0.011, 8, false);
  }, [tankHeight, item.position]);

  return (
    <group>
      {/* Arka Cam Tutucu Braket Plakası */}
      <mesh position={[0, totalH * 0.52, -0.085]} castShadow>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.15, totalH * 0.78, 0.018]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.5} />
      </mesh>

      {/* 4 Adet Kauçuk Vantuz (Akvaryum camına tam yapışır) */}
      {[
        [-0.055, totalH * 0.22],
        [0.055, totalH * 0.22],
        [-0.055, totalH * 0.82],
        [0.055, totalH * 0.82],
      ].map(([vx, vy], idx) => (
        <group key={idx} position={[vx, vy, -0.105]}>
          {/* Vantuz sapı */}
          <mesh rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.012, 0.015, 0.022, 12]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#18181b" roughness={0.8} />
          </mesh>
          {/* Vantuz çanağı (cama yapışan disk) */}
          <mesh position={[0, 0, -0.012]} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.032, 0.022, 0.014, 18]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#09090b" roughness={0.45} />
          </mesh>
        </group>
      ))}

      {/* Alt Filtre Kartuş Haznesi (Koyu Antrasit Kasa) */}
      <mesh position={[0, spongePartH / 2, 0]} castShadow>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.165, spongePartH, 0.145]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.48} metalness={0.15} />
      </mesh>

      {/* İçeride Görünür Biyolojik Sünger Çekirdeği */}
      <mesh position={[0, spongePartH / 2, 0.01]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.135, spongePartH - 0.05, 0.11]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          map={spongeTex}
          bumpMap={spongeTex}
          bumpScale={0.014}
          roughness={0.95}
        />
      </mesh>

      {/* Ön Su Emiş Izgara Yarıkları */}
      {[0.18, 0.34, 0.50, 0.66, 0.82].map((ratio, idx) => (
        <mesh key={idx} position={[0, spongePartH * ratio, 0.074]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.125, 0.016, 0.008]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#020617" />
        </mesh>
      ))}

      {/* Alt Kartuş Kilit Mandalı */}
      <mesh position={[0, 0.014, 0]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.172, 0.028, 0.152]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.55} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Orta Conta & Kilitleme Bileziği */}
      <mesh position={[0, spongePartH + 0.01, 0]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.175, 0.024, 0.155]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.35} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Üst Su Geçirmez Motor Kafası */}
      <mesh position={[0, spongePartH + 0.02 + motorPartH / 2, 0]} castShadow>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.172, motorPartH, 0.152]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0284c7" roughness={0.28} metalness={0.35} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Motor Kafası Üst Kapağı */}
      <mesh position={[0, totalH + 0.01, 0]}>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.162, 0.024, 0.142]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.4} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Yönlendirilebilir Su Çıkış Nozulu (Ördek Gagası / Difüzör Uç) */}
      <group position={[0, spongePartH + motorPartH * 0.68, 0.078]}>
        <mesh rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.022, 0.028, 0.055, 16]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.35} />
        </mesh>
        <mesh position={[0, 0, 0.042]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.068, 0.026, 0.042]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.25} />
        </mesh>
        {/* Venturi Hava Emiş Hortum Ucu */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.028, 0.015]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.007, 0.009, 0.032, 10]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#e2e8f0" roughness={0.3} />
        </mesh>
      </group>

      {/* Üst Cam Kenarından Dışarı Çıkan Elektrik Güç Kablosu */}
      <mesh geometry={powerCordGeo} raycast={() => null}>
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#09090b" roughness={0.75} />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\malzemeler\ekipman\HavaMotoruMesh.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React, { useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { PlacedEquipment } from '../../../store/useAquariumStore';

interface HavaMotoruMeshProps {
  item: PlacedEquipment;
  isActive: boolean;
}

export default function HavaMotoruMesh({ item, isActive }: HavaMotoruMeshProps) {
  const bodyGroupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
  const outletCount = item.product.outlet_count ?? 1;
  const isDual = outletCount >= 2;
  const watt = item.product.watt ?? 3;
  const scaleBoost = watt >= 5 ? 1.15 : 1.0;

  useFrame(({ clock }) => {
    if (!bodyGroupRef.current) return;
    if (isActive) {
      const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
      bodyGroupRef.current.position.x = Math.sin(t * 75) * 0.0022;
      bodyGroupRef.current.position.z = Math.cos(t * 82) * 0.0022;
    } else {
      bodyGroupRef.current.position.set(0, 0, 0);
    }
  });

  const bodyW = isDual ? 0.38 : 0.31;
  const bodyH = isDual ? 0.23 : 0.20;
  const bodyD = isDual ? 0.50 : 0.39;

  const cordGeo = useMemo(() => {
    const curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
      new THREE.Vector3(-bodyW / 2 - 0.01, 0.075, 0),
      new THREE.Vector3(-bodyW / 2 - 0.12, 0.03, 0.04),
      new THREE.Vector3(-bodyW / 2 - 0.24, 0.012, 0.14),
    ]);
    return new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 16, 0.011, 8, false);
  }, [bodyW]);

  return (
    <group scale={scaleBoost}>
      {/* 4 Adet Kauçuk Titreşim Emici Ayak */}
      {[
        [-bodyW * 0.36, 0.016, -bodyD * 0.35],
        [bodyW * 0.36, 0.016, -bodyD * 0.35],
        [-bodyW * 0.36, 0.016, bodyD * 0.35],
        [bodyW * 0.36, 0.016, bodyD * 0.35],
      ].map(([fx, fy, fz], idx) => (
        <mesh key={idx} position={[fx, fy, fz]} castShadow>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.026, 0.034, 0.032, 16]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#09090b" roughness={0.95} />
        </mesh>
      ))}

      {/* Titreşen Ana Motor Gövdesi */}
      <group ref={bodyGroupRef}>
        {/* Alt Şasi Gövdesi */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.032 + bodyH * 0.42, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[bodyW, bodyH * 0.84, bodyD]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.40} metalness={0.25} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Orta Sızdırmazlık Kauçuk Şeridi */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.032 + bodyH * 0.84, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[bodyW * 1.01, 0.014, bodyD * 1.01]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.85} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Üst Aerodinamik Kapak */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.032 + bodyH * 0.96, 0]} castShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[bodyW * 0.88, bodyH * 0.24, bodyD * 0.90]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            color={isDual ? '#0369a1' : '#0284c7'}
            roughness={0.28}
            metalness={0.38}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Yan Soğutma / Hava Emiş Izgaraları */}
        {[-0.09, -0.03, 0.03, 0.09].map((gz, idx) => (
          <mesh key={idx} position={[0, 0.032 + bodyH * 0.45, gz]}>
            <boxGeometry args={[bodyW + 0.006, bodyH * 0.45, 0.014]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#09090b" roughness={0.7} />
          </mesh>
        ))}

        {/* Üst Marka Plakası & LED Güç Göstergesi */}
        <mesh position={[0.02, 0.032 + bodyH * 1.09, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[bodyW * 0.42, 0.006, bodyD * 0.48]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.3} />
        </mesh>
        <mesh position={[-bodyW * 0.24, 0.032 + bodyH * 1.09, 0]}>
          <sphereGeometry args={[0.014, 12, 12]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            color={isActive ? '#22c55e' : '#64748b'}
            emissive={isActive ? '#22c55e' : '#000000'}
            emissiveIntensity={isActive ? 2.4 : 0}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Sol Tarafta Güç Kablosu Rakoru ve Kablosu */}
        <mesh
          position={[-bodyW / 2 - 0.02, 0.075, 0]}
          rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]}
        >
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.016, 0.022, 0.045, 12]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#09090b" roughness={0.8} />
        </mesh>
        <mesh geometry={cordGeo} raycast={() => null}>
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#09090b" roughness={0.75} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Sağ Tarafta (Akvaryuma Bakan Yüz) Krom Hava Çıkış Nozulları */}
        {isDual ? (
          <>
            {[-0.085, 0.085].map((nz, idx) => (
              <group key={idx} position={[bodyW / 2 + 0.035, 0.032 + bodyH * 0.55, nz]}>
                <mesh rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]} castShadow>
                  <cylinderGeometry args={[0.017, 0.022, 0.07, 14]} />
                  <meshStandardMaterial color="#e2e8f0" metalness={0.85} roughness={0.18} />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0.022, 0, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]}>
                  <cylinderGeometry args={[0.021, 0.021, 0.012, 14]} />
                  <meshStandardMaterial color="#cbd5e1" metalness={0.9} roughness={0.15} />
                </mesh>
              </group>
            ))}
          </>
        ) : (
          <group position={[bodyW / 2 + 0.035, 0.032 + bodyH * 0.55, 0]}>
            <mesh rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]} castShadow>
              <cylinderGeometry args={[0.018, 0.023, 0.07, 14]} />
              <meshStandardMaterial color="#e2e8f0" metalness={0.85} roughness={0.18} />
            </mesh>
            <mesh position={[0.022, 0, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]}>
              <cylinderGeometry args={[0.022, 0.022, 0.012, 14]} />
              <meshStandardMaterial color="#cbd5e1" metalness={0.9} roughness={0.15} />
            </mesh>
          </group>
        )}
      </group>
    </group>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\malzemeler\ekipman\HavaTasiMesh.tsx

'use client';

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { PlacedEquipment } from '../../../store/useAquariumStore';

function createPorousStoneTexture(baseHex: string, poreHex: string): THREE.CanvasTexture {
  const size = 128;
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = size;
  canvas.height = size;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')!;

  ctx.fillStyle = baseHex;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);

  for (let i = 0; i < 850; i++) {
    const x = (i * 53) % size;
    const y = (i * 97) % size;
    const r = (i % 2) * 0.7 + 0.6;
    ctx.fillStyle = poreHex;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.repeat.set(3, 2);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

export type AirStoneVariant = 'cylinder' | 'bar' | 'disc';

export function getAirStoneVariant(item: PlacedEquipment): AirStoneVariant {
  const slug = (item.product.slug || '').toLowerCase();
  const name = (item.product.name || '').toLowerCase();
  const len = item.product.length_cm || 0;

  if (
    slug.includes('disk') ||
    slug.includes('difuzor') ||
    name.includes('disk') ||
    name.includes('difüzör')
  ) {
    return 'disc';
  }

  if (
    slug.includes('uzun') ||
    slug.includes('cubuk') ||
    slug.includes('bar') ||
    name.includes('uzun') ||
    name.includes('çubuk') ||
    name.includes('cubuk') ||
    len >= 8
  ) {
    return 'bar';
  }

  return 'cylinder';
}

export function getAirBarSceneLength(item: PlacedEquipment): number {
  const lenCm = item.product.length_cm || 15;
  return Math.min(1.25, Math.max(0.45, lenCm / 22));
}

interface HavaTasiMeshProps {
  item: PlacedEquipment;
}

export default function HavaTasiMesh({ item }: HavaTasiMeshProps) {
  const variant = getAirStoneVariant(item);

  const bluePorousTex = useMemo(
    () => createPorousStoneTexture('#38bdf8', '#0284c7'),
    []
  );
  const ceramicPorousTex = useMemo(
    () => createPorousStoneTexture('#f8fafc', '#cbd5e1'),
    []
  );

  // 1. UZUN / ÇUBUK HAVA TAŞI (Bar Air Stone)
  if (variant === 'bar') {
    const barLength = getAirBarSceneLength(item);
    const bodyHeight = 0.052;
    const bodyDepth = 0.056;
    const capWidth = 0.036;
    const stoneLength = barLength - capWidth * 2;

    return (
      <group>
        {/* Alt Plastik Denge Kızakları (Kuma gömülmeden sabit durması için) */}
        {[-stoneLength * 0.32, stoneLength * 0.32].map((kx, idx) => (
          <mesh key={idx} position={[kx, 0.008, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.045, 0.016, bodyDepth + 0.028]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.65} />
          </mesh>
        ))}

        {/* Yatay Gözenekli Mineral Çubuk Gövde */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.016 + bodyHeight / 2, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[stoneLength, bodyHeight, bodyDepth]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            map={bluePorousTex}
            bumpMap={bluePorousTex}
            bumpScale={0.012}
            roughness={0.94}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Sol Koruyucu Kapak (Hortum Giriş Tarafı) */}
        <mesh position={[-barLength / 2 + capWidth / 2, 0.016 + bodyHeight / 2, 0]} castShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[capWidth, bodyHeight + 0.008, bodyDepth + 0.008]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.45} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Sağ Koruyucu Kapak (Kapalı Uç) */}
        <mesh position={[barLength / 2 - capWidth / 2, 0.016 + bodyHeight / 2, 0]} castShadow>
          <boxGeometry args={[capWidth, bodyHeight + 0.008, bodyDepth + 0.008]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.45} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Sol Uçta 45° Açılı Hava Hortumu Giriş Nipeli */}
        <mesh
          position={[-barLength / 2 - 0.016, 0.016 + bodyHeight * 0.72, 0]}
          rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 3]}
        >
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.011, 0.014, 0.048, 12]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#e2e8f0" roughness={0.28} metalness={0.4} />
        </mesh>
      </group>
    );
  }

  // 2. DİSK HAVA TAŞI / SERAMİK DİFÜZÖR
  if (variant === 'disc') {
    const discRadius = Math.min(0.18, Math.max(0.11, (item.product.length_cm || 6) / 55));

    return (
      <group>
        {/* Ağır Kauçuk/Plastik Alt Taban */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.018, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[discRadius, discRadius + 0.012, 0.036, 32]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.6} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Koruyucu Üst Çerçeve Halkası */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.038, 0]} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
          <torusGeometry args={[discRadius * 0.92, 0.009, 10, 32]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.45} />
        </mesh>

        {/* Beyaz Mikro Gözenekli Seramik Difüzör Plakası */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.04, 0]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[discRadius * 0.88, discRadius * 0.88, 0.014, 32]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            map={ceramicPorousTex}
            bumpMap={ceramicPorousTex}
            bumpScale={0.008}
            roughness={0.92}
          />
        </mesh>

        {/* Yan Hortum Giriş Nipeli */}
        <mesh position={[-discRadius - 0.022, 0.022, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]}>
          <cylinderGeometry args={[0.011, 0.014, 0.045, 12]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.3} />
        </mesh>
      </group>
    );
  }

  // 3. KLASİK SİLİNDİRİK MİNERAL HAVA TAŞI (Kompakt 2-4 cm)
  return (
    <group>
      {/* Alt Kauçuk Destek Halkası */}
      <mesh position={[0, 0.009, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.046, 0.050, 0.018, 22]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f172a" roughness={0.7} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Silindirik Mineral Gözenekli Gövde */}
      <mesh position={[0, 0.062, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.042, 0.045, 0.092, 22]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          map={bluePorousTex}
          bumpMap={bluePorousTex}
          bumpScale={0.012}
          roughness={0.94}
        />
      </mesh>

      {/* Üst Plastik Konik Başlık */}
      <mesh position={[0, 0.116, 0]}>
        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.026, 0.042, 0.018, 22]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#1e293b" roughness={0.45} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Üst Metalik/Plastik Hortum Nipeli */}
      <mesh position={[0, 0.140, 0]}>
        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.010, 0.013, 0.036, 12]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#e2e8f0" roughness={0.28} metalness={0.5} />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: models\malzemeler\ic-filtre\IcFiltreMesh.tsx
========================================
'use client';

import React, { useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';

interface IcFiltreMeshProps {
  tankWidth: number;
  tankHeight: number;
  tankDepth: number;
  sandTopY: number;
  waterSurfaceY: number;
}

export default function IcFiltreMesh({
  tankWidth,
  tankHeight,
  tankDepth,
  sandTopY,
  waterSurfaceY,
}: IcFiltreMeshProps) {
  const bubblesRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
  const count = 20;
  const dummy = useMemo(() => new THREE.Object3D(), []);

  // İç filtrenin tankın sağ-arka iç köşesindeki pozisyonu
  const filterX = tankWidth / 2 - 0.35;
  const filterZ = -tankDepth / 2 + 0.35;

  // Filtre gövde yüksekliği
  const filterHeight = 1.4;
  // Filtre tabanının tam sandTopY seviyesine oturması için merkez Y
  const filterCenterY = sandTopY + filterHeight / 2;
  // Filtre çıkış ağzı Y yüksekliği
  const outletY = sandTopY + filterHeight - 0.15;
  // Su yüzeyi sınırı
  const maxWaterY = waterSurfaceY - 0.05;

  const bubbleGeo = useMemo(() => new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 8, 8), []);
  const bubbleMat = useMemo(
    () => new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#e0f2fe', transparent: true, opacity: 0.75 }),
    []
  );

  useFrame(({ clock }) => {
    if (!bubblesRef.current) return;
    const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
    const maxRise = Math.max(0, maxWaterY - outletY);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      if (maxRise <= 0.02) {
        dummy.scale.set(0, 0, 0);
        dummy.updateMatrix();
        bubblesRef.current.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
        continue;
      }

      // Kabarcık filtre ağzından başlar, tam su yüzeyi altında kaybolur
      const progress = (t * 0.8 + i * (1 / count)) % 1;
      const y = outletY + progress * maxRise;
      const x = filterX + Math.sin(t * 3 + i) * 0.035;
      const z = filterZ + Math.cos(t * 2.5 + i) * 0.035;

      dummy.position.set(x, y, z);
      const scale = 0.025 + (i % 3) * 0.01;
      dummy.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
      dummy.updateMatrix();
      bubblesRef.current.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
    }
    bubblesRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <group>
      {/* Filtre Gövdesi - Tabanı tam sandTopY üzerine oturur */}
      <mesh position={[filterX, filterCenterY, filterZ]} castShadow>
        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.2, 0.2, filterHeight, 16]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0284c7" roughness={0.35} metalness={0.6} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Filtre Çıkış Nozulu */}
      <mesh position={[filterX, outletY, filterZ]} castShadow>
        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.07, 0.1, 0.18, 12]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#38bdf8" roughness={0.3} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Hava Kabarcığı Parçacıkları (Çıkış ağzından su yüzeyine) */}
      <instancedMesh ref={bubblesRef} args={[bubbleGeo, bubbleMat, count]} />
    </group>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Fish, ShoppingCart, AlertTriangle, Sun } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAquariumStore } from '@/store/useAquariumStore';
import AquariumScene from '@/components/canvas/AquariumScene';
import SearchBar from '@/components/ui/SearchBar';
import CatalogDrawer from '@/components/ui/CatalogDrawer';
import SideStatusPanel from '@/components/ui/SideStatusPanel';
import ShoppingCartPanel from '@/components/ui/ShoppingCartPanel';

export default function HomePage() {
  const loadCatalog = useAquariumStore((state) => state.loadCatalog);
  const tankItems = useAquariumStore((state) => state.tankItems);
  const ecosystemStatus = useAquariumStore((state) => state.ecosystemStatus);
  const lightHours = useAquariumStore((state) => state.lightHours);
  const setLightHours = useAquariumStore((state) => state.setLightHours);

  const [fps, setFps] = useState(60);
  const [isCatalogOpen, setIsCatalogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isCartOpen, setIsCartOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (loadCatalog) {
      loadCatalog();
    }
  }, [loadCatalog]);

  const totalItemsCount = tankItems.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.quantity, 0);
  const totalPrice = tankItems.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.product.price * item.quantity, 0);

  // Tanka LED aydınlatma eklendi mi kontrolü
  const hasLightAdded = tankItems.some((item) => item.product.lumen > 0 && item.quantity > 0);
  const warnings = ecosystemStatus.warnings;

  return (
    <main
      className="relative w-screen h-screen overflow-hidden bg-[#060d18] select-none flex"
      style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100vw', height: '100vh', overflow: 'hidden', backgroundColor: '#060d18' }}
    >
      {/* 1. Sol Genişletilmiş Katalog Paneli (Açıldığında sağ tarafı sıkıştırır) */}
      {isCatalogOpen && (
        <aside className="w-[38vw] min-w-[380px] max-w-[540px] h-full z-30 shrink-0 pointer-events-auto">
          <CatalogDrawer onClose={() => setIsCatalogOpen(false)} />
        </aside>
      )}

      {/* 2. Sağ / Ana Sahne Alanı (Katalog açılınca kalan alana göre otomatik daralır) */}
      <div className="relative flex-1 h-full overflow-hidden">
        {/* Tam Alan 3D Akvaryum Sahnesi */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full z-0">
          <AquariumScene onFpsUpdate={setFps} />
        </div>

        {/* Sol Üst Marka & Gerçek Zamanlı FPS Kartı */}
        <header className="absolute top-4 left-4 z-30 pointer-events-auto">
          <div className="bg-slate-900/75 backdrop-blur-md border border-cyan-500/20 rounded-2xl px-4 py-2 flex items-center gap-3 shadow-lg">
            <Fish className="w-6 h-6 text-cyan-400" />
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-white leading-none">
                fishLab
              </h1>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                3D Akvaryum Simülasyonu
              </p>
            </div>
            <span
              className={`ml-2 text-xs font-mono px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                fps >= 50
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30'
                  : fps >= 30
                  ? 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30'
                  : 'bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/30'
              }`}
            >
              {fps} FPS
            </span>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* Sol Üst Katalog Tetikleyici Butonu (Katalog kapalıyken görünür) */}
        {!isCatalogOpen && (
          <div className="absolute left-4 top-24 z-20 pointer-events-auto">
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsCatalogOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border backdrop-blur-md shadow-lg transition bg-slate-900/80 text-slate-200 border-slate-700/70 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:border-cyan-500/40"
            >
              <Fish className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400" />
              <span>Katalog</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Sayfanın En Üst Kısmında Kullanıcının Göreceği Uyarı Şeridi */}
        {warnings.length > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-20 max-w-[44%] w-max pointer-events-auto">
            <div className="bg-rose-950/85 backdrop-blur-xl border border-rose-500/50 text-rose-100 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="font-medium truncate">{warnings[0]}</span>
              {warnings.length > 1 && (
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold bg-rose-500/30 text-rose-200 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full shrink-0">
                  +{warnings.length - 1}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Işık Eklendiğinde Eklenen Işığın Tam Üst Hizasında Yarı Saydam Saat Çubuğu */}
        {hasLightAdded && (
          <div
            className={`absolute ${
              warnings.length > 0 ? 'top-16' : 'top-5'
            } left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-20 pointer-events-auto transition-all`}
          >
            <div className="bg-slate-900/55 hover:bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full px-4 py-1.5 shadow-xl flex items-center gap-3 transition">
              <Sun className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300 shrink-0" />
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0}
                max={16}
                step={1}
                value={lightHours}
                onChange={(e) => setLightHours(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-32 h-1.5 bg-slate-700/80 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-amber-400"
                title="Günlük Aydınlatma Süresi"
              />
              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-200 whitespace-nowrap">
                {lightHours} saat/gün
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Sağ Üst Kompakt Ekosistem & Litre Paneli */}
        <aside className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20 w-72 pointer-events-auto">
          <SideStatusPanel />
        </aside>

        {/* Alt Orta SearchBar */}
        <div className="absolute bottom-5 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-20 w-[400px] max-w-[60%] pointer-events-auto">
          <SearchBar />
        </div>

        {/* Sağ En Altta Sepet İkonu ve Açılır Sepet Penceresi */}
        <div className="absolute bottom-5 right-4 z-30 flex flex-col items-end gap-2.5 pointer-events-auto">
          {isCartOpen && (
            <ShoppingCartPanel onClose={() => setIsCartOpen(false)} />
          )}

          <button
            onClick={() => setIsCartOpen((prev) => !prev)}
            className={`relative flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl border backdrop-blur-xl shadow-2xl transition ${
              isCartOpen
                ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 border-cyan-300 shadow-cyan-500/30'
                : 'bg-slate-900/85 text-slate-100 border-slate-700/80 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:border-cyan-500/40'
            }`}
            title="Alışveriş Sepeti"
          >
            <div className="relative">
              <ShoppingCart className="w-5 h-5" />
              {totalItemsCount > 0 && (
                <span
                  className={`absolute -top-2 -right-2.5 text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full ${
                    isCartOpen
                      ? 'bg-slate-950 text-cyan-300'
                      : 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950'
                  }`}
                >
                  {totalItemsCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold">
              {totalPrice.toFixed(0)} ₺
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

========================================
DOSYA: store\useAquariumStore.ts
========================================
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';

export type TankSizeKey = '50L' | '100L' | '200L';

export interface TankDimensions {
  width: number;  // 1 birim = 10 cm (örn: 8.0 = 80 cm)
  height: number;
  depth: number;
}

export interface TankConfig {
  sizeKey: TankSizeKey;
  liters: number;
  dimensions: TankDimensions;
}

export interface CatalogItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  category: 'fish' | 'shrimp' | 'snail' | 'plant' | 'decor' | 'hardscape' | 'substrate' | 'lighting' | 'equipment' | string;
  price: number;
  image_url?: string;
  description?: string;
  bioload?: number;          // Biyolojik yük katsayısı (0 - 10)
  min_liters?: number;       // Minimum akvaryum hacmi
  min_temp?: number;
  max_temp?: number;
  min_ph?: number;
  max_ph?: number;
  aggression_level?: 'peaceful' | 'semi-aggressive' | 'aggressive' | string;
  incompatible_with?: string[];
  filtration_power?: number; // Ekipmanların biyolojik yük temizleme kapasitesi
  oxygen_boost?: number;     // Hava motoru / hava taşı oksijen katkısı
}

export interface TankInstanceItem {
  instanceId: string;
  itemId: string;
}

export type EquipmentType = 'filter' | 'air_pump' | 'air_stone';

export interface PlacedEquipment {
  instanceId: string;
  itemId: string;
  type: EquipmentType;
  position: [number, number, number];
}

export interface EcosystemWarning {
  id: string;
  severity: 'info' | 'warning' | 'danger';
  message: string;
}

export interface EcosystemStatus {
  healthScore: number;       // 0 - 100
  bioloadRatio: number;      // 0.0 - 2.0+ (1.0 üzeri aşırı yük)
  totalCost: number;
  warnings: EcosystemWarning[];
  waterVisuals: {
    color: string;
    opacity: number;
  };
}

const TANK_PRESETS: Record<TankSizeKey, TankConfig> = {
  '50L': {
    sizeKey: '50L',
    liters: 50,
    dimensions: { width: 5.0, height: 3.5, depth: 3.0 }, // 50x35x30 cm
  },
  '100L': {
    sizeKey: '100L',
    liters: 100,
    dimensions: { width: 8.0, height: 4.0, depth: 3.5 }, // 80x40x35 cm
  },
  '200L': {
    sizeKey: '200L',
    liters: 200,
    dimensions: { width: 10.0, height: 5.0, depth: 4.0 }, // 100x50x40 cm
  },
};

// Supabase bağlantısı gecikirse veya offline kalırsa kullanılacak yedek katalog
const FALLBACK_CATALOG: CatalogItem[] = [
  { id: 'beta-halfmoon', name: 'Halfmoon Beta (Kırmızı)', category: 'fish', price: 250, bioload: 2.0, min_liters: 20, aggression_level: 'aggressive' },
  { id: 'beta-crowntail', name: 'Crowntail Beta (Mavi)', category: 'fish', price: 280, bioload: 2.0, min_liters: 20, aggression_level: 'aggressive' },
  { id: 'lepistes-red', name: 'Kırmızı Lepistes', category: 'fish', price: 65, bioload: 1.0, min_liters: 30, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'lepistes-moscow', name: 'Moskova Mavi Lepistes', category: 'fish', price: 85, bioload: 1.0, min_liters: 30, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'vatoz-leopar', name: 'Leopar Vatoz', category: 'fish', price: 140, bioload: 2.5, min_liters: 50, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'vatoz-albino', name: 'Albino Cüce Vatoz (L144)', category: 'fish', price: 190, bioload: 2.0, min_liters: 40, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'karides-bloody-mary', name: 'Bloody Mary Karides', category: 'shrimp', price: 55, bioload: 0.3, min_liters: 15, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'karides-mavi-melek', name: 'Mavi Melek Karides', category: 'shrimp', price: 60, bioload: 0.3, min_liters: 15, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'karides-carbon-rilli', name: 'Carbon Rilli Karides', category: 'shrimp', price: 65, bioload: 0.3, min_liters: 15, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'karides-sari-ates', name: 'Sarı Ateş Neon Karides', category: 'shrimp', price: 60, bioload: 0.3, min_liters: 15, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'salyangoz-elma', name: 'Kahverengi Elma Salyangozu', category: 'snail', price: 40, bioload: 0.8, min_liters: 20, aggression_level: 'peaceful' },
  { id: 'bitki-java-moss', name: 'Java Moss (Porsiyon)', category: 'plant', price: 90, bioload: -0.8, min_liters: 10 },
  { id: 'bitki-anubias', name: 'Anubias Barteri', category: 'plant', price: 130, bioload: -1.0, min_liters: 10 },
  { id: 'dekor-lav-kirigi', name: 'Lav Kırığı Kayası', category: 'decor', price: 75, bioload: -0.3, min_liters: 10 },
  { id: 'kum-silis', name: 'İnce Silis Kum (5 kg)', category: 'substrate', price: 110, bioload: 0, min_liters: 10 },
  { id: 'isik-beyaz-led', name: '6500K Beyaz LED Armatür', category: 'lighting', price: 420, bioload: 0, min_liters: 20 },
  { id: 'isik-rgb-led', name: 'Full Spectrum RGB LED', category: 'lighting', price: 650, bioload: 0, min_liters: 20 },
  { id: 'ekipman-pipo-filtre', name: 'Biyolojik Pipo Filtre', category: 'equipment', price: 180, filtration_power: 12, min_liters: 10 },
  { id: 'ekipman-hava-motoru', name: 'Çift Çıkışlı Hava Motoru', category: 'equipment', price: 290, oxygen_boost: 10, min_liters: 20 },
  { id: 'ekipman-hava-tasi', name: 'Nano Hava Taşı', category: 'equipment', price: 45, oxygen_boost: 4, min_liters: 10 },
];

interface AquariumStoreState {
  // Katalog & Yükleme Durumu
  catalog: CatalogItem[];
  isLoadingCatalog: boolean;
  catalogError: string | null;

  // Tank Ayarları
  tankConfig: TankConfig;

  // Tank İçeriği
  tankItems: TankInstanceItem[];
  placedEquipments: PlacedEquipment[];

  // Sürükleme & Seçim Durumu
  isDraggingEquipment: boolean;
  selectedEquipmentId: string | null;

  // Ekosistem & Uyumluluk Analizi
  ecosystemStatus: EcosystemStatus;

  // Aksiyonlar
  loadCatalog: () => Promise<void>;
  setTankSize: (sizeKey: TankSizeKey) => void;
  addItem: (itemId: string) => void;
  removeItem: (instanceId: string) => void;
  clearTank: () => void;

  // Ekipman Etkileşim Aksiyonları
  updateEquipmentPosition: (instanceId: string, position: [number, number, number]) => void;
  removeEquipment: (instanceId: string) => void;
  setIsDraggingEquipment: (dragging: boolean) => void;
  setSelectedEquipmentId: (instanceId: string | null) => void;

  // Hesaplama Motoru
  recalculateEcosystem: () => void;
}

export const useAquariumStore = create<AquariumStoreState>((set, get) => ({
  catalog: [],
  isLoadingCatalog: false,
  catalogError: null,

  tankConfig: TANK_PRESETS['100L'],

  tankItems: [],
  placedEquipments: [],

  isDraggingEquipment: false,
  selectedEquipmentId: null,

  ecosystemStatus: {
    healthScore: 100,
    bioloadRatio: 0,
    totalCost: 0,
    warnings: [],
    waterVisuals: {
      color: '#38bdf8',
      opacity: 0.2,
    },
  },

  loadCatalog: async () => {
    if (get().isLoadingCatalog) return;
    set({ isLoadingCatalog: true, catalogError: null });

    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('catalog_items')
        .select('*')
        .order('category', { ascending: true });

      if (error || !data || data.length === 0) {
        set({
          catalog: FALLBACK_CATALOG,
          isLoadingCatalog: false,
          catalogError: error ? error.message : null,
        });
        get().recalculateEcosystem();
        return;
      }

      set({
        catalog: data as CatalogItem[],
        isLoadingCatalog: false,
      });
      get().recalculateEcosystem();
    } catch (err: any) {
      set({
        catalog: FALLBACK_CATALOG,
        isLoadingCatalog: false,
        catalogError: err?.message || 'Katalog yüklenemedi, yerel veriler aktif.',
      });
      get().recalculateEcosystem();
    }
  },

  setTankSize: (sizeKey: TankSizeKey) => {
    const preset = TANK_PRESETS[sizeKey];
    if (!preset) return;

    // Tank küçüldüyse dışarıda kalan ekipmanları tank sınırları içine çek
    const { width, height, depth } = preset.dimensions;
    const clampedEquipments = get().placedEquipments.map((eq) => {
      if (eq.type === 'air_pump') {
        return {
          ...eq,
          position: [width / 2 + 1.2, -height / 2, 0] as [number, number, number],
        };
      }
      const clampedX = Math.max(-width / 2 + 0.4, Math.min(width / 2 - 0.4, eq.position[0]));
      const clampedY = Math.max(-height / 2 + 0.4, Math.min(height / 2 - 0.4, eq.position[1]));
      const clampedZ = Math.max(-depth / 2 + 0.4, Math.min(depth / 2 - 0.4, eq.position[2]));
      return {
        ...eq,
        position: [clampedX, clampedY, clampedZ] as [number, number, number],
      };
    });

    set({
      tankConfig: preset,
      placedEquipments: clampedEquipments,
    });
    get().recalculateEcosystem();
  },

  addItem: (itemId: string) => {
    const { catalog, tankItems, placedEquipments, tankConfig } = get();
    const catalogItem = catalog.find((c) => c.id === itemId);
    const key = `${itemId} ${catalogItem?.name || ''}`.toLowerCase();

    // Ekipman kategorisindeki ürünler (Filtre, Hava Motoru, Hava Taşı) sürüklenebilir ekipman olarak eklenir
    if (
      catalogItem?.category === 'equipment' ||
      key.includes('filtre') ||
      key.includes('filter') ||
      key.includes('hava motoru') ||
      key.includes('hava taşı') ||
      key.includes('air')
    ) {
      const eqType: EquipmentType =
        key.includes('hava motoru') ? 'air_pump' :
        key.includes('hava taşı') ? 'air_stone' : 'filter';

      const { width, height } = tankConfig.dimensions;
      const defaultPos: [number, number, number] =
        eqType === 'air_pump'
          ? [width / 2 + 1.2, -height / 2, 0] // Hava motoru tankın yanındaki masa üstüne konur
          : eqType === 'air_stone'
          ? [width * 0.25, -height / 2 + 0.3, 0] // Hava taşı tabana konur
          : [-width * 0.32, 0, -tankConfig.dimensions.depth * 0.25]; // Filtre arka köşeye konur

      const newEquipment: PlacedEquipment = {
        instanceId: `${itemId}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
        itemId,
        type: eqType,
        position: defaultPos,
      };

      set({
        placedEquipments: [...placedEquipments, newEquipment],
        selectedEquipmentId: newEquipment.instanceId,
      });
      get().recalculateEcosystem();
      return;
    }

    // Canlı, bitki, dekor, taban kumu veya aydınlatma ekle
    const newItem: TankInstanceItem = {
      instanceId: `${itemId}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      itemId,
    };

    set({ tankItems: [...tankItems, newItem] });
    get().recalculateEcosystem();
  },

  removeItem: (instanceId: string) => {
    set((state) => ({
      tankItems: state.tankItems.filter((item) => item.instanceId !== instanceId),
    }));
    get().recalculateEcosystem();
  },

  clearTank: () => {
    set({
      tankItems: [],
      placedEquipments: [],
      selectedEquipmentId: null,
      isDraggingEquipment: false,
    });
    get().recalculateEcosystem();
  },

  updateEquipmentPosition: (instanceId: string, position: [number, number, number]) => {
    set((state) => ({
      placedEquipments: state.placedEquipments.map((eq) =>
        eq.instanceId === instanceId ? { ...eq, position } : eq
      ),
    }));
  },

  removeEquipment: (instanceId: string) => {
    set((state) => ({
      placedEquipments: state.placedEquipments.filter((eq) => eq.instanceId !== instanceId),
      selectedEquipmentId: state.selectedEquipmentId === instanceId ? null : state.selectedEquipmentId,
    }));
    get().recalculateEcosystem();
  },

  setIsDraggingEquipment: (dragging: boolean) => {
    set({ isDraggingEquipment: dragging });
  },

  setSelectedEquipmentId: (instanceId: string | null) => {
    set({ selectedEquipmentId: instanceId });
  },

  recalculateEcosystem: () => {
    const { catalog, tankItems, placedEquipments, tankConfig } = get();
    const warnings: EcosystemWarning[] = [];

    let totalCost = 0;
    let rawBioload = 0;
    let filtrationCapacity = 10; // Temel doğal bakteri döngüsü kapasitesi

    const speciesCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
    let hasBeta = 0;
    let hasLepistes = 0;
    let hasShrimp = 0;
    let hasAirPump = false;
    let hasAirStone = false;

    // 1. Tank içi canlı ve bitkileri hesapla
    tankItems.forEach((inst) => {
      const meta = catalog.find((c) => c.id === inst.itemId);
      if (!meta) return;

      totalCost += meta.price || 0;
      rawBioload += meta.bioload ?? 1.0;

      const nameLower = meta.name.toLowerCase();
      speciesCounts[meta.id] = (speciesCounts[meta.id] || 0) + 1;

      if (nameLower.includes('beta') || nameLower.includes('betta')) hasBeta += 1;
      if (nameLower.includes('lepistes') || nameLower.includes('guppy')) hasLepistes += 1;
      if (meta.category === 'shrimp' || nameLower.includes('karides')) hasShrimp += 1;

      // Minimum hacim kontrolü
      if (meta.min_liters && tankConfig.liters < meta.min_liters) {
        warnings.push({
          id: `vol_${inst.instanceId}`,
          severity: 'warning',
          message: `${meta.name} için en az ${meta.min_liters}L hacim önerilir (Mevcut: ${tankConfig.liters}L).`,
        });
      }
    });

    // 2. Ekipman katkıları ve maliyetlerini hesapla
    placedEquipments.forEach((eq) => {
      const meta = catalog.find((c) => c.id === eq.itemId);
      if (meta) {
        totalCost += meta.price || 0;
        filtrationCapacity += meta.filtration_power || 0;
      }
      if (eq.type === 'filter') filtrationCapacity += 12;
      if (eq.type === 'air_pump') hasAirPump = true;
      if (eq.type === 'air_stone') hasAirStone = true;
    });

    // 3. Türler Arası Uyumluluk Kuralları
    if (hasBeta > 1) {
      warnings.push({
        id: 'compat_multi_beta',
        severity: 'danger',
        message: 'Aynı akvaryumda birden fazla erkek Beta balığı kavga ederek birbirine zarar verir!',
      });
    }

    if (hasBeta > 0 && hasLepistes > 0) {
      warnings.push({
        id: 'compat_beta_guppy',
        severity: 'danger',
        message: 'Beta balıkları uzun ve renkli kuyruklu Lepisteslere saldırabilir.',
      });
    }

    if (hasBeta > 0 && hasShrimp > 0) {
      warnings.push({
        id: 'compat_beta_shrimp',
        severity: 'warning',
        message: 'Beta balığı yavru ve küçük süs karideslerini avlayabilir; yoğun Java Moss saklanma alanı ekleyin.',
      });
    }

    if (hasAirStone && !hasAirPump) {
      warnings.push({
        id: 'equip_airstone_no_pump',
        severity: 'info',
        message: 'Hava taşının kabarcık üretmesi için tank dışına bir Hava Motoru eklemelisiniz.',
      });
    }

    // Tekrarlayan uyarı mesajlarını tekilleştir
    const uniqueWarnings = Array.from(new Map(warnings.map((w) => [w.message, w])).values());

    // 4. Biyolojik Yük ve Su Görseli Hesabı
    const effectiveCapacity = (tankConfig.liters / 10) + filtrationCapacity;
    const netBioload = Math.max(0, rawBioload);
    const bioloadRatio = effectiveCapacity > 0 ? Number((netBioload / effectiveCapacity).toFixed(2)) : 0;

    if (bioloadRatio > 1.0) {
      uniqueWarnings.unshift({
        id: 'bioload_high',
        severity: 'danger',
        message: `Biyolojik yük kapasitesi aşıldı (%${Math.round(bioloadRatio * 100)}). Pipo/dış filtre ekleyin veya canlı sayısını azaltın.`,
      });
    }

    // Sağlık Puanı Hesaplama
    let healthScore = 100;
    if (bioloadRatio > 0.75) {
      healthScore -= Math.round((bioloadRatio - 0.75) * 60);
    }
    uniqueWarnings.forEach((w) => {
      if (w.severity === 'danger') healthScore -= 18;
      else if (w.severity === 'warning') healthScore -= 8;
    });
    healthScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, healthScore));

    // Su Rengi & Bulanıklık (Amonyak / yük arttıkça berrak maviden yeşilimsi sarıya döner)
    let waterColor = '#38bdf8'; // Berrak cam göbeği/mavi
    let waterOpacity = 0.22;

    if (bioloadRatio > 1.15) {
      waterColor = '#84cc16'; // Yoğun amonyak / alg bulanıklığı
      waterOpacity = 0.42;
    } else if (bioloadRatio > 0.85) {
      waterColor = '#2dd4bf'; // Hafif yüklenmiş su
      waterOpacity = 0.30;
    }

    set({
      ecosystemStatus: {
        healthScore,
        bioloadRatio,
        totalCost,
        warnings: uniqueWarnings,
        waterVisuals: {
          color: waterColor,
          opacity: waterOpacity,
        },
      },
    });
  },
}));
